    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नाविक</span> <i>nāvika</i> [<a href="#entry-nau_1" class="skt-link">nau_1</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-naavika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naavika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naavikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nāvikī</i> relatif au bateau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> batelier; navigateur, capitaine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नाव्य</span> <i>nāvya</i> [<a href="#entry-nau_1" class="skt-link">nau_1</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-naavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nāvyā</i> navigable — <a href="#entry-naavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nāvyā</i> rivière navigable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naay">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नाय्</span> <i>nāy</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-nii_1" class="skt-link">nī_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(nāyayati)</i> faire mener, faire conduire — ps. <i>(nāyyate)</i> être amené à conduire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नाय</span> <i>nāya</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nii_1" class="skt-link">nī_1</a>] <a href="#entry-naaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> guide; chef — <a href="#entry-naaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> politique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naayaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नायक</span> <i>nāyaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nii_1" class="skt-link">nī_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-naayaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naayaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naayikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nāyikā</i> qui conduit, qui guide — <a href="#entry-naayaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> chef, capitaine, dirigeant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. not. chef militaire du pays <a href="#entry-Uandhra" class="skt-link">Andhra</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukaapaya" class="skt-link">Kāpaya</a>; après la conquête d'<a href="#entry-Uekazilaa" class="skt-link">Ekaśilā</a> par le Sultanat de Bahmani, les <i>nāyakās</i> fondèrent l'empire de <a href="#entry-Uvijayanagara" class="skt-link">Vijayanagara</a>; la dynastie <a href="#entry-Unaayaka" class="skt-link">Nāyaka</a> remplaça les <a href="#entry-Ucola" class="skt-link">Cola</a> à Thanjavur vers 1532 <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a> Nāyaka, philosophe du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> (11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle), critique d'<a href="#entry-Uaanandavardhana" class="skt-link">Ānandavardhana</a>; il redéfinit le <a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a> comme l'expression du sentiment chez l'auditeur (et non chez le héros); il est l'auteur du <a href="#entry-Uh_rdayadarpa_na" class="skt-link">Hṛdayadarpaṇa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> héros, jeune premier (premier rôle masculin d'un drame) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> joyau central (d'un collier) — <a href="#entry-naayikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nāyikā</i> noble dame <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> héroïne de théâtre, jeune première; not. <a href="#entry-abhisaarikaa" class="skt-link">abhisārikā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> courtisane.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naaz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नाश्</span> <i>nāś</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-naz_1" class="skt-link">naś_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(nāśayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nāśita</i><i>)</i> faire périr, détruire; s'écarter de ⟨abl.⟩; oublier; renoncer à ⟨acc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नाश</span> <i>nāśa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-naz_1" class="skt-link">naś_1</a>] <a href="#entry-naaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> perte, ruine, destruction, anéantissement.<br> <i>cittanāśa</i> m. phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] cessation de l'activité mentale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naazaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नाशक</span> <i>nāśaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-naz_1" class="skt-link">naś_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-naazaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naazaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naazikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nāśikā</i> qui détruit, qui supprime ⟨g. iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui gaspille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naazana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नाशन</span> <i>nāśana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-naz_1" class="skt-link">naś_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-naazana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naazana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naazanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nāśanī</i> qui détruit — <a href="#entry-naazana" class="skt-link">n.</a> annihilation, ruine, destruction, perte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naazin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नाशिन्</span> <i>nāśin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-naz_1" class="skt-link">naś_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-naazin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naazin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naazinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nāśinī</i> périssable — ifc. qui détruit, qui enlève.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naazita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नाशित</span> <i>nāśita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-naaz" class="skt-link">nāś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-naazita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naazita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naazitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nāśitā</i> détruit, ruiné, perdu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> exclu — <a href="#entry-naazita" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. hors-caste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nabhas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नभस्</span> <i>nabhas</i> <a href="#entry-nabhas" class="skt-link">n.</a> ciel, voûte céleste; atmosphère <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> nuage, brume, vapeur — <a href="#entry-nabhas" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. nom d'un mois véd. (<a href="#entry-zraava_na_2" class="skt-link">śrāvaṇa_2</a>, juillet-août) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>nabha</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. νεφος; lat. <i>nebula</i>; all. Nebel; fr. nuage, nébuleux; ru. nebo.<br> <i>tārakitaṃ</i> <i>nabhaḥ</i> le ciel étoilé.<br> <i>nabhaḥspṛśaṃ</i> <i>dīptam</i> (devise de l'armée de l'air indienne, extraite de la BhG.) En incandescence je touche au ciel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nabhasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नभस्य</span> <i>nabhasya</i> [<a href="#entry-nabhas" class="skt-link">nabhas</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nabhasya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nabhasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nabhasyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nabhasyā</i> nuageux, brumeux — <a href="#entry-nabhasya" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. nom d'un mois véd. (<a href="#entry-bhaadrapada" class="skt-link">bhādrapada</a>, août-septembre).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naciketas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नचिकेतस्</span> <i>naciketas</i> var. <i>naciketa</i> [<a href="#entry-na" class="skt-link">na</a>-<a href="#entry-cit_1" class="skt-link">cit_1</a>] <a href="#entry-naciketas" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [BrP.] np. de Naciketā, jeune brâhmane «qui ne comprenait pas» (pourquoi son père se libérait de tous ses biens); [KU.] il fut maudit par son père <a href="#entry-Uvaajazravasa" class="skt-link">Vājaśravasa</a>, qui l'envoya à la Mort; <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a>, pour s'excuser de ne pas l'avoir accueilli de suite, lui accorda la réalisation de 3 vœux: le pardon de son père, l'immortalité, et la connaissance du Soi [<a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">ātman</a>]; puis il lui enseigna comment construire l'autel védique [<a href="#entry-agnicayana" class="skt-link">agnicayana</a>] pour célébrer le feu sacré [<a href="#entry-naaciketa" class="skt-link">nāciketa</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nacira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नचिर</span> <i>nacira</i> [<a href="#entry-na" class="skt-link">na</a>-<a href="#entry-cira" class="skt-link">cira</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nacira" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nacira" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naciraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nacirā</i> pas long; court.<br> <i>nacirāt</i> adv. bientôt.<br> <i>nacirādiva</i> adv. très bientôt, tout de suite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">नद्</span> <i>nad</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nadati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nadita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ni</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> murmurer; bruire, résonner, rendre un son; crier, hurler, hennir — ca. <i>(nādayati)</i> ca. <i>(nadayati)</i> faire résonner, remplir de bruit ou de cris — dés. <i>(ninadiṣati)</i> vouloir crier — int. <i>(nānadati)</i> hurler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नद</span> <i>nada</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nad" class="skt-link">nad</a>] <a href="#entry-nada" class="skt-link">m.</a> rivière <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être bruyant (not. nuage, cheval, taureau).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nadaanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nadānta</i> [<a href="#entry-anta" class="skt-link">anta</a>] <a href="#entry-nadaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. danse cosmique de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>-<a href="#entry-Una_taraaja" class="skt-link">Naṭarāja</a>, qui fait «cesser le bruit» de l'univers lors de sa dissolution finale; représentée dans des idoles de bronze dans l'Inde du Sud, notamment à l'époque <a href="#entry-Ucola" class="skt-link">Cola</a> (11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> en <a href="#entry-Ugafgaadhara" class="skt-link">Gaṅgādhara</a> tient dans sa main droite supérieure un tambour [<a href="#entry-_damarin" class="skt-link">ḍamarin</a>] symbolisant le son primordial, dans sa main gauche supérieure une flamme symbolisant la destruction du monde, sa main droite inférieure est en <a href="#entry-abhaya" class="skt-link">abhaya</a> <a href="#entry-mudraa" class="skt-link">mudrā</a> symbolisant la protection divine, sa main gauche inférieure montre son pied gauche levé symbolisant l'espoir de libération, le pied droit reposant sur le nain épileptique <a href="#entry-Uapasmaarapuru_sa" class="skt-link">Apasmārapuruṣa</a> symbolisant l'ignorance; le tout entouré d'un halo de feu [<a href="#entry-prabhaavali" class="skt-link">prabhāvali</a>]; cette danse est aussi appelée <a href="#entry-aanandataa_n_dava" class="skt-link">ānandatāṇḍava</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-nada" class="skt-link">nada</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naddha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नद्ध</span> <i>naddha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nah" class="skt-link">nah</a>] a. <a href="#entry-naddha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naddha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naddhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>naddhā</i> attaché, lié; assemblé, réuni; équipé, ceint — ifc. fixé à — <a href="#entry-naddha" class="skt-link">n.</a> lien, attache, entrave; nœud.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nadii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नदी</span> <i>nadī</i> [<a href="#entry-nad" class="skt-link">nad</a>] <a href="#entry-nadii" class="skt-link">f.</a> rivière, courant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méd. circulation sanguine; tension.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nadiija">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nadīja</i> [<a href="#entry-ja" class="skt-link">ja</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nadiija" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nadiija" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nadiijaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nadījā</i> issu de la rivière — <a href="#entry-nadiija" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Nadīja, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ubhii_sma" class="skt-link">Bhīṣma</a> «fils de <a href="#entry-Ugafgaa" class="skt-link">Gaṅgā</a>» — <a href="#entry-nadiija" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nadijaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nadijā</i> natu. lotus.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nadiisafgama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nadīsaṃgama</i> [<a href="#entry-safgama" class="skt-link">saṃgama</a>] <a href="#entry-nadiisa_mgama" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. confluence de deux rivières.<br> <a href="#entry-nadii" class="skt-link">nadī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nadiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nadīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nadiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nadiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nadiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nadīyā</i> région «autour de la rivière» au Bengale, de capitale <a href="#entry-Unavadviipa" class="skt-link">Navadvīpa</a>, où <a href="#entry-Uraghunaatha_1" class="skt-link">Raghunātha_1</a> <a href="#entry-Uziroma_ni" class="skt-link">Śiromaṇi</a> fonda l'université Nadīyā au 15⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, centre important de dialectique, de logique et de droit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नग</span> <i>naga</i> [<a href="#entry-na" class="skt-link">na</a>-<a href="#entry-ga_1" class="skt-link">ga_1</a>] <a href="#entry-naga" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«être immobile»] montagne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> arbre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nagara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नगर</span> <i>nagara</i> <a href="#entry-nagara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nagara" class="skt-link">n.</a> ville, cité; capitale — <a href="#entry-nagarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nagarī</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nagaraaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nagarāja</i> [<a href="#entry-raajan" class="skt-link">rājan</a>] <a href="#entry-nagaraaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> grande montagne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nagaraajadhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nagarājadhara</i> [<a href="#entry-dhara" class="skt-link">dhara</a>] <a href="#entry-nagaraajadhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Nagarājadhara «Qui supporte la grande montagne», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> soutenant le Mont <a href="#entry-Ugovardhana" class="skt-link">Govardhana</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-nagaraaja" class="skt-link">nagarāja</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nagarakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nagarakāra</i> [<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-nagarakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> architecte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nagarakiirtana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nagarakīrtana</i> [<a href="#entry-kiirtana" class="skt-link">kīrtana</a>] <a href="#entry-nagarakiirtana" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. procession dévotionnelle de rue, avec chants et danses; cf. <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nagarazobhinii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nagaraśobhinī</i> [<a href="#entry-zobhinii" class="skt-link">śobhinī</a>] <a href="#entry-nagarazobhinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. [«parure urbaine»] courtisane (not. au temps de <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>).<br> <a href="#entry-nagara" class="skt-link">nagara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nagavaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nagavāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-vaasa_1" class="skt-link">vāsa_1</a>] <a href="#entry-nagavaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«qui vit dans les arbres»] paon.<br> <a href="#entry-naga" class="skt-link">naga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nagna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नग्न</span> <i>nagna</i> a. <a href="#entry-nagna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nagna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nagnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nagnā</i> nu; désert, aride — <a href="#entry-nagna" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. religieux errant nu; <a href="#entry-saadhu" class="skt-link">sādhu</a> combattant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. naked; all. nackt; fr. nu; hi. <i>nagā</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nagnajit">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nagnajit</i> [<a href="#entry-jit" class="skt-link">jit</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nagnajit" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nagnajit" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nagnajit" class="skt-link">f.</a> [«vainqueur d'un opposant nu»] athlète — <a href="#entry-nagnajit" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du roi Nagnajit du <a href="#entry-Ugandhaara" class="skt-link">Gandhāra</a>; <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> soumis ses fils et épousa l'une de ses filles <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Nagnajit_1, expert en arts [<a href="#entry-zilpazaastra" class="skt-link">śilpaśāstra</a>], auteur du <a href="#entry-Ucitralak_sa_na" class="skt-link">Citralakṣaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nagnatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nagnatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-nagnatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> nudité.<br> <a href="#entry-nagna" class="skt-link">nagna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nah">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">नह्</span> <i>nah</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(nahyati)</i> pft. <i>(nanāha)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>natsyati</i><i>)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>naddha</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ut</i>, <i>upa</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> lier, nouer, fixer; joindre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> équiper — pr. md. <i>(nahyate)</i> s'équiper, se revêtir de ⟨acc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>necto</i>; fr. nouer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nahi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नहि</span> <i>nahi</i> [<a href="#entry-na" class="skt-link">na</a>-<a href="#entry-hi_1" class="skt-link">hi_1</a>] part. non, pas du tout, nullement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nahu_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नहुष</span> <i>nahuṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-nahus" class="skt-link">nahus</a>] <a href="#entry-nahu_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du roi Nahuṣa, 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ roi de la lignée lunaire, fils d'<a href="#entry-Uaayu" class="skt-link">Āyu</a> et père de <a href="#entry-Uyayaati" class="skt-link">Yayāti</a>; il s'empara du trône d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>; l'assemblée des sages [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] porta son chariot dans les airs, mais son pied ayant touché <a href="#entry-Uagastya" class="skt-link">Agastya</a>, celui-ci le changea en serpent, et il fut contraint de vivre sur terre 100000 ans <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du roi d'<a href="#entry-Uayodhyaa" class="skt-link">Ayodhyā</a> Nahuṣa_1, fils d'<a href="#entry-Uambarii_sa_1" class="skt-link">Ambarīṣa_1</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Uyayaati_1" class="skt-link">Yayāti_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nahus">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नहुस्</span> <i>nahus</i> [<a href="#entry-nah" class="skt-link">nah</a>] <a href="#entry-nahus" class="skt-link">m.</a> voisin; son prochain; homme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> l'humanité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>nahuṣaṣ</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nai_s_thika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैष्ठिक</span> <i>naiṣṭhika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ni_s_tha" class="skt-link">niṣṭha</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nai_s_thika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nai_s_thika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nai_s_thikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>naiṣṭhikī</i> terminal, définitif, ferme; parfait, complet, supérieur — ifc. parfait connaisseur de — <a href="#entry-nai_s_thika" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. [«qui rend ses vœux définitifs»] étudiant brâmanique [<a href="#entry-brahmacarya" class="skt-link">brahmacarya</a>] optant à la fin de ses études pour une vie de renonçant; opp. <a href="#entry-upakurvaa_na" class="skt-link">upakurvāṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nai_sadha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैषध</span> <i>naiṣadha</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ni_sadha" class="skt-link">niṣadha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nai_sadha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nai_sadha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nai_sadhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>naiṣadhī</i> géo. relatif au pays ou au peuple <a href="#entry-ni_sadha" class="skt-link">niṣadha</a> — <a href="#entry-nai_sadha" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. épith. de <a href="#entry-Unala" class="skt-link">Nala</a>, roi du pays <a href="#entry-ni_sadha" class="skt-link">niṣadha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. géo. le peuple des Naiṣadhās ou <a href="#entry-Uni_sadhaas" class="skt-link">Niṣadhās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nai_sadhaananda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>naiṣadhānanda</i> [<a href="#entry-aananda" class="skt-link">ānanda</a>] <a href="#entry-nai_sadhaananda" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Naiṣadhānanda, drame [<a href="#entry-naa_taka" class="skt-link">nāṭaka</a>] dû à <a href="#entry-Uk_semiizvara" class="skt-link">Kṣemīśvara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nai_sadhacarita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>naiṣadhacarita</i> [<a href="#entry-carita" class="skt-link">carita</a>] <a href="#entry-nai_sadhacarita" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du <a href="#entry-mahaakaavya" class="skt-link">mahākāvya</a> Naiṣadhacarita de <a href="#entry-Uzriihar_sa" class="skt-link">Śrīharṣa</a>, aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Unai_sadhiiya" class="skt-link">Naiṣadhīya</a>; c'est une variante du début de l'histoire de <a href="#entry-Unala" class="skt-link">Nala</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Upa_ncanaliiya" class="skt-link">Pañcanalīya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nai_sadhiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>naiṣadhīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nai_sadhiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nai_sadhiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nai_sadhiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>naiṣadhīyā</i> relatif au pays ou au peuple <a href="#entry-ni_sadha" class="skt-link">niṣadha</a> — <a href="#entry-nai_sadhiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>naiṣadhīyacarita</i>] lit. np. du <a href="#entry-mahaakaavya" class="skt-link">mahākāvya</a> Naiṣadhīya; cf. <a href="#entry-Unai_sadhacarita" class="skt-link">Naiṣadhacarita</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-nai_sadha" class="skt-link">naiṣadha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nai_skarmya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैष्कर्म्य</span> <i>naiṣkarmya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ni_skarman" class="skt-link">niṣkarman</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-nai_skarmya" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. le non-agir, liberté de ne pas s'engager en action.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nai_skarmyasiddhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>naiṣkarmyasiddhi</i> phil. perfection de la libération des actes <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. de la Naiṣkarmyasiddhi, œuvre phil. <a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a> due à <a href="#entry-Usurezvara" class="skt-link">Sureśvara</a>.<br> <i>naiṣkarmyasiddhim</i> <i>paramāṃ</i> <i>saṃnyāsenādhigacchati</i> [BhG.] Par son renoncement il atteint la perfection suprême d'être délivré de ses actes.<br> <a href="#entry-nai_skarmya" class="skt-link">naiṣkarmya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naigama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैगम</span> <i>naigama</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-nigama" class="skt-link">nigama</a>] a. <a href="#entry-naigama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naigama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naigamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>naigamī</i> relatif au <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> et à son vocabulaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. collection de 278 mots du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> ou de dialogues <a href="#entry-nigama" class="skt-link">nigama</a> par <a href="#entry-Uyaaska" class="skt-link">Yāska</a> — <a href="#entry-naigama" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. interprète de la tradition sacrée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> moyen, expédient; conduite prudente <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> citoyen respectable; homme avisé; bourgeois, marchand.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naigamakha_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>naigamakhaṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-kha_n_da" class="skt-link">khaṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-naigamakha_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naigamakha_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. section de 3 chapitres du <a href="#entry-Unirukta" class="skt-link">Nirukta</a> où <a href="#entry-Uyaaska" class="skt-link">Yāska</a> glose le vocabulaire <a href="#entry-naigama" class="skt-link">naigama</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naigame_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैगमेष</span> <i>naigameṣa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-nejame_sa" class="skt-link">nejameṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-naigame_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Naigameṣa «Croque-mitaine», aspect et compagnon de <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a> à tête de bélier; il saisit les enfants et leur fait du mal; cf. <a href="#entry-Unejame_sa" class="skt-link">Nejameṣa</a>, <a href="#entry-Unaigameya" class="skt-link">Naigameya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naigameya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>naigameya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] <a href="#entry-naigameya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Naigameya «Interprète de la tradition sacrée», aspect et compagnon de <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Unaigame_sa" class="skt-link">Naigameṣa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-naigama" class="skt-link">naigama</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naija">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैज</span> <i>naija</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-nija" class="skt-link">nija</a>] a. <a href="#entry-naija" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naija" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naijaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>naijā</i> qui appartient en propre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैक</span> <i>naika</i> [<a href="#entry-na" class="skt-link">na</a>-<a href="#entry-eka" class="skt-link">eka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-naika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>naikā</i> non unique, multiple; nombreux, varié.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naikadhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>naikadhā</i> [<a href="#entry--dhaa" class="skt-link">-dhā</a>] adv. multiplement; de diverses manières.<br> <a href="#entry-naika" class="skt-link">naika</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naimi_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैमिष</span> <i>naimiṣa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-nimi_sa" class="skt-link">nimiṣa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-naimi_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naimi_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naimi_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>naimiṣī</i> temporaire, momentané; instantané — <a href="#entry-naimi_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de la forêt [<a href="#entry-ara_nya" class="skt-link">araṇya</a>] et du gué sacré [<a href="#entry-tiirtha" class="skt-link">tīrtha</a>] Naimiṣa; <a href="#entry-Uugrazravaa" class="skt-link">Ugraśravā</a> <a href="#entry-Usauti_2" class="skt-link">Sauti_2</a> y conta le <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a> aux sages [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] rassemblés par <a href="#entry-Uzaunaka" class="skt-link">Śaunaka</a>; <a href="#entry-Usuuta" class="skt-link">Sūta</a> y récita le <a href="#entry-Ubhaagavatapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Bhāgavatapurāṇa</a>; on dit que cette forêt est ainsi nommée car une armée d'<a href="#entry-asuraas" class="skt-link">asurās</a> y fut défaite «en un clin d'œil» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. mod. Nimsar, sur les rives de la <a href="#entry-Ugomatii" class="skt-link">Gomatī</a>, dans le district de Sītāpur en Uttar Pradesh.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naimi_saara_nya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>naimiṣāraṇya</i> [<a href="#entry-ara_nya" class="skt-link">araṇya</a>] <a href="#entry-naimi_saara_nya" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. np. de Naimiṣāraṇya, la forêt <a href="#entry-Unaimi_sa" class="skt-link">Naimiṣa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-naimi_sa" class="skt-link">naimiṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naimitta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैमित्त</span> <i>naimitta</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-nimitta" class="skt-link">nimitta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-naimitta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naimitta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naimittaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>naimittā</i> relatif aux signes — <a href="#entry-naimitta" class="skt-link">m.</a> devin [«qui explique les présages»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naimittika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैमित्तिक</span> <i>naimittika</i> [<a href="#entry-naimitta" class="skt-link">naimitta</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-naimittika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naimittika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naimittikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>naimittikā</i> déterminé, causé par; accidentel, fortuit, occasionnel (opp. <a href="#entry-nitya" class="skt-link">nitya</a>) — <a href="#entry-naimittika" class="skt-link">n.</a> effet — <a href="#entry-naimittika" class="skt-link">m.</a> devin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naipu_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैपुण</span> <i>naipuṇa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-nipu_na" class="skt-link">nipuṇa</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-naipu_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naipu_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naipu_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>naipuṇā</i> cf. <a href="#entry-nipu_na" class="skt-link">nipuṇa</a> — <a href="#entry-naipu_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> expertise; syn. <a href="#entry-naipu_nya" class="skt-link">naipuṇya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naipu_nya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैपुण्य</span> <i>naipuṇya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-nipu_na" class="skt-link">nipuṇa</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-naipu_nya" class="skt-link">n.</a> habileté, dextérité; excellence en ⟨g. iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> art nécessitant d'être adroit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> plénitude, totalité.<br> <i>naipuṇyena</i> totalement; exactement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nair_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैरृत</span> <i>nairṛta</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-nir_rti" class="skt-link">nirṛti</a>] <a href="#entry-nair_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [«issu de <a href="#entry-Unir_rti" class="skt-link">Nirṛti</a>»] ogre-vampire; syn. <a href="#entry-raak_sasa" class="skt-link">rākṣasa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nairaatmya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैरात्म्य</span> <i>nairātmya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-niraatman" class="skt-link">nirātman</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-nairaatmya" class="skt-link">n.</a> bd. doctrine de la négation du Soi; syn. <a href="#entry-anaatman" class="skt-link">anātman</a> — <a href="#entry-nairaatmyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nairātmyā</i> bd. [tantr.] np. de Prajñānairātmyā «Celle qui connaît la vacuité intrinsèque», parèdre de <a href="#entry-Uhevajra" class="skt-link">Hevajra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nairaatmyavaadin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nairātmyavādin</i> [<a href="#entry-vaadin" class="skt-link">vādin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nairaatmyavaadin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nairaatmyavaadin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nairaatmyavaadinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nairātmyavādinī</i> bd. qui enseigne la doctrine de la négation du Soi.<br> <a href="#entry-nairaatmya" class="skt-link">nairātmya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nairaazya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैराश्य</span> <i>nairāśya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-niraazaa" class="skt-link">nirāśā</a>] <a href="#entry-nairaazya" class="skt-link">n.</a> indifférence, absence de désir ou d'espérance; dépression, désespoir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nairgu_nya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैर्गुण्य</span> <i>nairguṇya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-nirgu_na" class="skt-link">nirguṇa</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-nairgu_nya" class="skt-link">n.</a> absence de qualités <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> absence de bonnes qualités, médiocrité — a. <a href="#entry-nairgu_nya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nairgu_nya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nairgu_nyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nairguṇyā</i> sans connection avec des qualités.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nairlajjya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैर्लज्ज्य</span> <i>nairlajjya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-nirlajja" class="skt-link">nirlajja</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-nairlajjya" class="skt-link">n.</a> absence de honte; impudence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nairmalya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैर्मल्य</span> <i>nairmalya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-nirmala" class="skt-link">nirmala</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-nairmalya" class="skt-link">n.</a> impeccabilité; pureté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naiva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैव</span> <i>naiva</i> [<a href="#entry-na" class="skt-link">na</a>-<a href="#entry-eva" class="skt-link">eva</a>] adv. pas même.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naivedya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैवेद्य</span> <i>naivedya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-nivedya" class="skt-link">nivedya</a>] <a href="#entry-naivedya" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. offrande de nourriture à une idole.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naiyaayika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैयायिक</span> <i>naiyāyika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-naiyaayika" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. logicien; adepte ou doctrinaire du <a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>; on en distingue l'école ancienne [<a href="#entry-praaciina" class="skt-link">prācīna</a>] et l'école nouvelle [<a href="#entry-navya" class="skt-link">navya</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naiza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नैश</span> <i>naiśa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-nizaa_1" class="skt-link">niśā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-naiza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naiza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naizii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>naiśī</i> nocturne — <a href="#entry-naiza" class="skt-link">m.</a> animal sauvage nocturne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nak_satra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नक्षत्र</span> <i>nakṣatra</i> <a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">n.</a> (<i>nakṣarati</i> «immobile») astre, étoile, corps céleste <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> l'ensemble des étoiles <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. position céleste de la pleine lune, astérisme lunaire, maison lunaire; il y a eu d'abord 27 <i>nakṣatrāṇi</i>, puis 28 avec l'ajout d'<a href="#entry-Uabhijit" class="skt-link">Abhijit</a>; chaque <a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a> désigne en fait une division de 13° 20' de l'écliptique; chacun est divisée en 4 quarts [<a href="#entry-paada" class="skt-link">pāda</a>] de 3° 20' <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. une lunaison personnifiée, fille de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a> et épouse de <a href="#entry-Ucandra" class="skt-link">Candra</a>; elles se nomment: <a href="#entry-Uk_rttikaas" class="skt-link">Kṛttikās</a> (Pléiades), <a href="#entry-Urohi_nii_1" class="skt-link">Rohiṇī_1</a> ou <a href="#entry-Ubraahmii" class="skt-link">Brāhmī</a> (Aldébaran), <a href="#entry-Um_rgaziras" class="skt-link">Mṛgaśiras</a> ou <a href="#entry-Uaagrahaaya_nii" class="skt-link">Āgrahāyaṇī</a> (Baudrier d'Orion), <a href="#entry-Uaardraa" class="skt-link">Ārdrā</a> ou <a href="#entry-Ubaahuu" class="skt-link">Bāhū</a> (Bételgeuse), <a href="#entry-Upunarvasuu" class="skt-link">Punarvasū</a> ou <a href="#entry-Uyamakau" class="skt-link">Yamakau</a> (Castor et Pollux), <a href="#entry-Upu_sya" class="skt-link">Puṣya</a> ou <a href="#entry-Uti_sya" class="skt-link">Tiṣya</a> ou <a href="#entry-Usidhya" class="skt-link">Sidhya</a> (Cancer), <a href="#entry-Uaazle_saas" class="skt-link">Āśleṣās</a> (Hydre), <a href="#entry-Umaghaas" class="skt-link">Maghās</a> (Régulus), <a href="#entry-Upuurvaphalgunii" class="skt-link">Pūrvaphalgunī</a> (δ du Lion), <a href="#entry-Uuttaraphalgunii" class="skt-link">Uttaraphalgunī</a> (β du Lion), <a href="#entry-Uhasta" class="skt-link">Hasta</a> (Corbeau), <a href="#entry-Ucitraa" class="skt-link">Citrā</a> (Épi de la Vierge), <a href="#entry-Usvaati" class="skt-link">Svāti</a> ou <a href="#entry-Usvaatii" class="skt-link">Svātī</a> (Arcturus), <a href="#entry-Uvizaakhe" class="skt-link">Viśākhe</a> ou <a href="#entry-Uraadhaa_2" class="skt-link">Rādhā_2</a> (Balance), <a href="#entry-Uanuraadhaas" class="skt-link">Anurādhās</a> (δ du Scorpion), <a href="#entry-Ujye_s_thaa" class="skt-link">Jyeṣṭhā</a> (Antarès), <a href="#entry-Umuulabarha_nii" class="skt-link">Mūlabarhaṇī</a> (ε du Scorpion), <a href="#entry-Upuurvaa_saa_dhaas" class="skt-link">Pūrvāṣāḍhās</a> (δ du Sagittaire), <a href="#entry-Uuttaraa_saa_dhaas" class="skt-link">Uttarāṣāḍhās</a> (σ du Sagittaire), <a href="#entry-Uabhijit" class="skt-link">Abhijit</a> (Véga), <a href="#entry-Uzrava_naa" class="skt-link">Śravaṇā</a> (Altaïr), <a href="#entry-Uzravi_s_thaa" class="skt-link">Śraviṣṭhā</a> ou <a href="#entry-Udhani_s_thaa" class="skt-link">Dhaniṣṭhā</a> (β du Dauphin), <a href="#entry-Uzatabhi_sak" class="skt-link">Śatabhiṣak</a> (λ du Verseau), <a href="#entry-Upuurvapro_s_thapadaas" class="skt-link">Pūrvaproṣṭhapadās</a> ou <a href="#entry-Upuurvabhaadrapadaas" class="skt-link">Pūrvabhādrapadās</a> (αβ de Pégase), <a href="#entry-Uuttarapro_s_thapadaas" class="skt-link">Uttaraproṣṭhapadās</a> ou <a href="#entry-Uuttarabhaadrapadaas" class="skt-link">Uttarabhādrapadās</a> (γ de Pégase et α d'Andromède), <a href="#entry-Urevatii" class="skt-link">Revatī</a> (ζ des Poissons), <a href="#entry-Uazvinii" class="skt-link">Aśvinī</a> ou <a href="#entry-Uazviyujau" class="skt-link">Aśviyujau</a> (βζ du Bélier) et <a href="#entry-Ubhara_nii" class="skt-link">Bharaṇī</a> (Mouche).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nak_satraraaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nakṣatrarāja</i> [<a href="#entry-raaja" class="skt-link">rāja</a>] <a href="#entry-nak_satraraaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> «roi des étoiles», épith. de la Lune.<br> <a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नकार</span> <i>nakāra</i> [<i>(na)</i>-<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-nakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> le son ou la lettre ‘<i>na</i>’.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nakha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नख</span> <i>nakha</i> <a href="#entry-nakha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nakha" class="skt-link">n.</a> ongle; griffe.<br> <i>nakhāni</i> <i>kṛ</i> tailler les ongles.<br> <i>nakhāni</i> <i>kḷp</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nakhalekhaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nakhalekhaka</i> [<a href="#entry-lekhaka" class="skt-link">lekhaka</a>] <a href="#entry-nakhalekhaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> manucure.<br> <a href="#entry-nakha" class="skt-link">nakha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nakhamuca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nakhamuca</i> [<a href="#entry-muca" class="skt-link">muca</a>] <a href="#entry-nakhamuca" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«qui libère l'ongle»] se dit d'un arc.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nakhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nakhara</i> [<a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] <a href="#entry-nakhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> ongle; griffe, éperon — a. <a href="#entry-nakhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nakhara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nakharaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nakharā</i> recourbé, crochu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nakra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नक्र</span> <i>nakra</i> <a href="#entry-nakra" class="skt-link">m.</a> crocodile.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nakta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नक्त</span> <i>nakta</i> <a href="#entry-nakta" class="skt-link">n.</a> nuit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. νυξ; lat. <i>nox</i>; ang. night; all. Nacht; fr. nuit.<br> <i>naktam</i> adv. de nuit; opp. <a href="#entry-divaa" class="skt-link">divā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nakula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नकुल</span> <i>nakula</i> <a href="#entry-nakula" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. <i>Herpestes edwardsii</i>, mangouste <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. la mangouste contrôle les richesses des serpents [<a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a>]; on la représente versant des joyaux dans les mains de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Nakula «Mangouste», l'un des 5 <a href="#entry-paa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">pāṇḍavās</a>, fils des <a href="#entry-Uazvinau" class="skt-link">Aśvinau</a> et de <a href="#entry-Umaadrii" class="skt-link">Mādrī</a>, frère jumeau de <a href="#entry-Usahadeva" class="skt-link">Sahadeva</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ugranthika" class="skt-link">Granthika</a>, <a href="#entry-Usugho_sa" class="skt-link">Sughoṣa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Nakula_1, auteur de l'<a href="#entry-Uazvazaastra" class="skt-link">Aśvaśāstra</a> — <a href="#entry-nakulii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nakulī</i> mangouste femelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नल</span> <i>nala</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-na_da" class="skt-link">naḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-nala" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Amphidonax karka</i>, espèce de grand roseau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du héros Nala «Roseau», roi des <a href="#entry-Uni_sadhaas" class="skt-link">Niṣadhās</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Uviirasena" class="skt-link">Vīrasena</a>; beau, brave, et généreux, il tomba amoureux de la belle princesse <a href="#entry-Udamayantii" class="skt-link">Damayantī</a> qui le choisit pour époux; pour cela, elle dut déjouer avec l'aide de <a href="#entry-Usarasvatii" class="skt-link">Sarasvatī</a> la tromperie des dieux cardinaux <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, <a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a>, <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a> qui avaient prit l'apparence de <a href="#entry-Unala" class="skt-link">Nala</a>; <a href="#entry-Unala" class="skt-link">Nala</a> fut dépossédé de son royaume par la perfidie de son frère <a href="#entry-Upu_skara" class="skt-link">Puṣkara</a> et par le démon du jeu <a href="#entry-Ukali" class="skt-link">Kali</a>, et il abandonna <a href="#entry-Udamayantii" class="skt-link">Damayantī</a> aux dangers de la forêt, mais ils seront heureusement réunis à la fin du récit; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukarko_taka" class="skt-link">Karkoṭaka</a>, <a href="#entry-Unai_sadhacarita" class="skt-link">Naiṣadhacarita</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Nala_1, 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ fils de <a href="#entry-Uyadu" class="skt-link">Yadu</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du singe Nala_2, fils de <a href="#entry-Uvizvakarmaa" class="skt-link">Viśvakarmā</a>, ayant hérité de ses dons d'architecte; il construisit pour <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> la jetée [<a href="#entry-setu" class="skt-link">setu</a>] lui permettant d'envahir <a href="#entry-Ulafkaa" class="skt-link">Laṅkā</a> — <a href="#entry-nala" class="skt-link">n.</a> odeur, parfum — <a href="#entry-nalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nalā</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Nalā, fille de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a> et <a href="#entry-Utaamraa" class="skt-link">Tāmrā</a>; elle enfanta de <a href="#entry-Uvinataa" class="skt-link">Vinatā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nalada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nalada</i> [<a href="#entry-da" class="skt-link">da</a>] <a href="#entry-nalada" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nalada" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. bot. <i>Nardostachys grandiflora</i>, nard indien; plante de l'<a href="#entry-Uhimaalaya" class="skt-link">Himālaya</a> au rhizôme fibreux aromatique; syn. <a href="#entry-ja_taamaa_msii" class="skt-link">jaṭāmāṃsī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nalaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नलक</span> <i>nalaka</i> [<a href="#entry-nala" class="skt-link">nala</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-nalaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> os long (creux comme un roseau); not. tibia — <a href="#entry-nalikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nalikā</i> vaisseau du corps; syn. <a href="#entry-naa_dii" class="skt-link">nāḍī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> carquois.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nalakiila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nalakīla</i> [<a href="#entry-kiila" class="skt-link">kīla</a>] <a href="#entry-nalakiila" class="skt-link">m.</a> genou.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nalakuubara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nalakūbara</i> [<a href="#entry-kuubara" class="skt-link">kūbara</a>] <a href="#entry-nalakuubara" class="skt-link">m.</a> var. <i>nalakūvara</i> myth. np. du génie [<a href="#entry-guhyaka" class="skt-link">guhyaka</a>] Nalakūbara ou Nalakūvara, fils de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a>; il épousa la nymphe [<a href="#entry-apsaras" class="skt-link">apsaras</a>] <a href="#entry-Urambhaa" class="skt-link">Rambhā</a>; celle-ci ayant été violée par <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Unalakuubara" class="skt-link">Nalakūbara</a> maudit celui-ci de ne pouvoir toucher une femme non consentante sans en mourir, contribuant ainsi à la protection de <a href="#entry-Usitaa" class="skt-link">Sitā</a>; [BhP.] il s'enivra de <a href="#entry-vaaru_nii" class="skt-link">vāruṇī</a> avec son frère <a href="#entry-Uma_nigriiva" class="skt-link">Maṇigrīva</a>, et ils dansèrent nus avec des nymphes devant <a href="#entry-Unaarada" class="skt-link">Nārada</a>; celui-ci les condamna à devenir des arbres <a href="#entry-arjuna" class="skt-link">arjuna</a> pendant 100 années célestes; <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> enfant les libéra en déracinant les arbres.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nalapaaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nalapāka</i> [<a href="#entry-paaka" class="skt-link">pāka</a>] <a href="#entry-nalapaaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] don de faire de la cuisine savoureuse, offert par <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a> à <a href="#entry-Unala" class="skt-link">Nala</a> en récompense de son rôle de messager.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nalapaakazaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nalapākaśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-nalapaakazaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Nalapākaśāstra «Science des mets», enseignant l'art de la préparation des plats.<br> <a href="#entry-nalapaaka" class="skt-link">nalapāka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nalasetu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nalasetu</i> [<a href="#entry-setu" class="skt-link">setu</a>] <a href="#entry-nalasetu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du Nalasetu «Pont de <a href="#entry-Unala_2" class="skt-link">Nala_2</a>», construit par les singes pour que l'armée de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> puisse envahir <a href="#entry-Ulafkaa" class="skt-link">Laṅkā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nalina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नलिन</span> <i>nalina</i> <a href="#entry-nalina" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. bot. <i>Nelumbium speciosum</i>, lotus — <a href="#entry-nalinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nalinī</i> étang couvert de lotus <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de la Nalinī, une <a href="#entry-divyagafgaa" class="skt-link">divyagaṅgā</a>; c'est une branche de la Voie lactée ou Gange céleste [<a href="#entry-Umandaakinii" class="skt-link">Mandākinī</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nalopaakhyaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nalopākhyāna</i> [<a href="#entry-upaakhyaana" class="skt-link">upākhyāna</a>] <a href="#entry-nalopaakhyaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. np. du Nalopākhyāna (histoire de <a href="#entry-Unala" class="skt-link">Nala</a>), section du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a> que <a href="#entry-Ub_rhadazva" class="skt-link">Bṛhadaśva</a> raconte à <a href="#entry-Uyudhi_s_thira" class="skt-link">Yudhiṣṭhira</a> pour lui montrer que ses malheurs pourraient être bien pires.<br> <a href="#entry-nala" class="skt-link">nala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nalva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नल्व</span> <i>nalva</i> <a href="#entry-nalva" class="skt-link">m.</a> mesure de longueur, environ 200 m.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">नम्</span> <i>nam</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(namati)</i> pr. md. <i>(namate)</i> pft. <i>(nanāma)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] <i>(anaṃsīt)</i> imp. <i>(nama)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nata</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>namanīya</i>, <i>namitavya</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>natvā</i>, <i>-natya</i>, <i>-namya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ava</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>ut</i>, <i>upa</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> courber, incliner, pencher <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'incliner, se baisser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> saluer, honorer, rendre hommage à — ps. <i>(namyate)</i> être courbé; se soumettre, céder — ca. <i>(nāmayati)</i> incliner, courber; faire ployer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. νεμος; lat. <i>nemus</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-namana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नमन</span> <i>namana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nam" class="skt-link">nam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-namana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-namana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-namanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>namanā</i> ifc. qui courbe, qui incline — <a href="#entry-namana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait d'incliner, de courber; fait de bander un arc.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-namaniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नमनीय</span> <i>namanīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-nam" class="skt-link">nam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-namaniiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-namaniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-namaniiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>namanīyā</i> qui doit être salué.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-namas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नमस्</span> <i>namas</i> [<a href="#entry-nam" class="skt-link">nam</a>] <a href="#entry-namas" class="skt-link">n.</a> salut, hommage, adoration — prép. gloire à ⟨dat.⟩ — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(namasyati)</i> rendre hommage à ⟨acc.⟩, honorer, saluer; révérer, adorer.<br> <i>namaste</i> [«je te salue»] salutations; bonjour, bienvenue; bonsoir.<br> <i>oṃ</i> <i>namaḥ</i> <i>śivāya</i> [<i>pañcākṣarī</i>] gloire à <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>!<br> <i>śrī</i> <i>gaṇeśāya</i> <i>namaḥ</i> en hommage à <a href="#entry-Uga_neza" class="skt-link">Gaṇeśa</a> (invocation au début d'un ouvrage sanskrit).<br> <i>haraye</i> <i>namaḥ</i> salutations à <a href="#entry-Uhari" class="skt-link">Hari</a>.<br> <i>śrī</i> <i>śrī</i> <i>gurave</i> <i>namaḥ</i> en hommage au vénéré maître (invocation au début d'un ouvrage sanskrit).<br> <i>namo'stu</i> <i>te</i> Ô ⟨voc.⟩ je te rends grâce; gloire à toi (invocation dans une prière).<br> <i>namo</i> <i>namaḥ</i> salutations.<br> <i>namasyati</i> <i>devān</i> il adore les dieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-namaskaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>namaskāra</i> [<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-namaskaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> salut, hommage; adoration (s'effectue la tête inclinée dans les mains en <a href="#entry-a_njali" class="skt-link">añjali</a> <a href="#entry-mudraa" class="skt-link">mudrā</a>, et en prononçant <i>namaḥ</i>).<br> <i>namaskāram</i> adr. soyez le bienvenu; salutations; bonjour, bonsoir.<br> <a href="#entry-namas" class="skt-link">namas</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naminaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नमिनाथ</span> <i>naminātha</i> var. <i>niminātha</i> <a href="#entry-naminaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. np. de Naminātha, 21⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-tiirthafkara" class="skt-link">tīrthaṅkara</a> mythique; son corps est doré, son symbole est le lotus bleu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-namitavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नमितव्य</span> <i>namitavya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <a href="#entry-nam" class="skt-link">nam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-namitavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-namitavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-namitavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>namitavyā</i> qui doit être salué.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-namra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नम्र</span> <i>namra</i> [<a href="#entry-nam" class="skt-link">nam</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-namra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-namra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-namraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>namrā</i> incliné, courbé, penché; déférent, humble <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> adr. votre serviteur — ifc. soumis à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-namuci">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नमुचि</span> <i>namuci</i> [<a href="#entry-na" class="skt-link">na</a>-<a href="#entry-muc_2" class="skt-link">muc_2</a>] <a href="#entry-namuci" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [MkP.] np. du démon Namuci «Qui ne délivre pas»; <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> avait promis de ne l'attaquer ni de jour ni de nuit, ni avec du sec ni avec de l'humide; profitant de l'ivresse d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, il lui vola la moitié de sa force; <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> la regagna avec l'aide de <a href="#entry-Usarasvatii" class="skt-link">Sarasvatī</a> et des <a href="#entry-Uazvinau" class="skt-link">Aśvinau</a>, et tua <a href="#entry-Unamuci" class="skt-link">Namuci</a> «en barattant sa tête avec de l'écume».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nanaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नना</span> <i>nanā</i> <a href="#entry-nanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> mère, maman.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nanaand_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ननान्दृ</span> <i>nanāndṛ</i> <a href="#entry-nanaand_r" class="skt-link">f.</a> belle-sœur (sœur de son mari) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>nanandṛ</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nanaand_rpati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nanāndṛpati</i> [<a href="#entry-pati" class="skt-link">pati</a>] <a href="#entry-nanaand_rpati" class="skt-link">m.</a> mari de sa belle-sœur.<br> <a href="#entry-nanaand_r" class="skt-link">nanāndṛ</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nanand_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ननन्दृ</span> <i>nanandṛ</i> cf. <a href="#entry-nanaand_r" class="skt-link">nanāndṛ</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nand">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">नन्द्</span> <i>nand</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nandati)</i> pr. md. <i>(nandate)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>nandya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>abhi</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>prati</i><i>)</i> se réjouir; se réjouir de ⟨acc.⟩ — ps. <i>(nandyate)</i> être réjoui; jouir de — ca. <i>(nandayati)</i> réjouir, combler de joie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nanda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नन्द</span> <i>nanda</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nand" class="skt-link">nand</a>] <a href="#entry-nanda" class="skt-link">m.</a> joie, plaisir, bonheur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] «Joyeux», np. de Nanda <a href="#entry-Umahaaraaja" class="skt-link">Mahārāja</a>, roi des bergers, qui éleva <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> avec sa femme <a href="#entry-Uyazodaa" class="skt-link">Yaśodā</a> à <a href="#entry-Uvraja" class="skt-link">Vraja</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] nom d'un tambour de <a href="#entry-Uyudhi_s_thira" class="skt-link">Yudhiṣṭhira</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. d'un <a href="#entry-dvaarapaala" class="skt-link">dvārapāla</a> (son double étant appelé <a href="#entry-Usunanda" class="skt-link">Sunanda</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. nom de la dynastie des <a href="#entry-Unanda" class="skt-link">Nanda</a> du <a href="#entry-Umagadha" class="skt-link">Magadha</a>; elle fut renversée par <a href="#entry-Ucandragupta" class="skt-link">Candragupta</a> qui fonda la dynastie <a href="#entry-Umaurya" class="skt-link">Maurya</a> — <a href="#entry-nandaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nandā</i> félicité, joie, bonheur; prospérité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. de la montagne Nandā <a href="#entry-devii" class="skt-link">devī</a>, sommet de 7816 m de l'<a href="#entry-Uhimaalaya" class="skt-link">Himālaya</a>, dans l'état mod. d'Uttarakhaṇḍa; myth. elle incarne <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a>; son pic Est, haut de 7434 n, est sa compagne <a href="#entry-Usunandaa" class="skt-link">Sunandā</a> <a href="#entry-devii" class="skt-link">devī</a> — <a href="#entry-nandii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nandī</i> bénédiction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nandaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नन्दक</span> <i>nandaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nand" class="skt-link">nand</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nandaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nandaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nandikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nandikā</i> joyeux, heureux — ifc. qui prend plaisir à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Nandaka «Joyeux», glaive [<a href="#entry-kha_dga" class="skt-link">khaḍga</a>] de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> — <a href="#entry-nandaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> joie, plaisir.<br> <i>nandakaḥ</i> nom. (bénédiction) puisse-t-il se réjouir — <a href="#entry-nandakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nandakā</i> puisse-t-elle se réjouir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nandana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नन्दन</span> <i>nandana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nand" class="skt-link">nand</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nandana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nandana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nandanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nandanā</i> qui réjouit, qui rend heureux — <a href="#entry-nandana" class="skt-link">m.</a> fils <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. np. de Nandana «Bonheur», personnifiant l'an 26 d'un cycle de vie [<a href="#entry-sa_mvatsara" class="skt-link">saṃvatsara</a>] — <a href="#entry-nandana" class="skt-link">n.</a> félicité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. paradis d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-svarga" class="skt-link">svarga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nandi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नन्दि</span> <i>nandi</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nand" class="skt-link">nand</a>] <a href="#entry-nandi" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nandi" class="skt-link">n.</a> joie; jeu — <a href="#entry-nandi" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Nandi «Joyeux», épith. de divers dieux — <a href="#entry-nandi" class="skt-link">f.</a> joie, bonheur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nandigho_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nandighoṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-gho_sa" class="skt-link">ghoṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-nandigho_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> cris de joie, musique joyeuse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. proclamation par un hérault ou un panégyriste <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Nandighoṣa, char d'<a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. np. du char à 18 roues de <a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha" class="skt-link">Jagannātha</a> au festival de <a href="#entry-rathayaatraa" class="skt-link">rathayātrā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nandiizvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nandīśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-nandiizvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Nandīśvara, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Unandii" class="skt-link">Nandī</a>, premier assistant de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nandika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नन्दिक</span> <i>nandika</i> [<a href="#entry-nanda" class="skt-link">nanda</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-nandika" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Nandika «Joyeux»; cf. <a href="#entry-Unandii" class="skt-link">Nandī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nandikezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nandikeśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-nandikezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Nandikeśvara, forme de <a href="#entry-Unandii" class="skt-link">Nandī</a> musicien; il est représenté avec une tête de taureau et un corps humain avec 4 bras, les supérieurs tenant la hache [<a href="#entry-parazu" class="skt-link">paraśu</a>] et la gazelle [<a href="#entry-m_rga" class="skt-link">mṛga</a>], les inférieurs jouant du <a href="#entry-m_rdafga" class="skt-link">mṛdaṅga</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Nandikeśvara_1, auteur présumé de l'<a href="#entry-Uabhinayadarpa_na" class="skt-link">Abhinayadarpaṇa</a>, qu'il composa à la demande d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> comme abrégé du <a href="#entry-Ubharataar_nava" class="skt-link">Bharatārṇava</a> composé selon les règles de <a href="#entry-Ubharatamuni" class="skt-link">Bharatamuni</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nandikezvarasa_mhitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nandikeśvarasaṃhitā</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">saṃhitā</a>] <a href="#entry-nandikezvarasa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Nandikeśvarasaṃhitā, recueil de rituel shivaïste; cf. <a href="#entry-Uzivadharma" class="skt-link">Śivadharma</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-nandikezvara" class="skt-link">nandikeśvara</a> <br> <a href="#entry-nandika" class="skt-link">nandika</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nandin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नन्दिन्</span> <i>nandin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nand" class="skt-link">nand</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nandin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nandin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nandinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nandinī</i> joyeux, réjoui — <a href="#entry-nandin" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Nandī «Joyeux», membre du <a href="#entry-ga_na" class="skt-link">gaṇa</a>, suivant de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>, armé d'une lance [<a href="#entry-zuula" class="skt-link">śūla</a>]; plus tard identifié à sa monture [<a href="#entry-vaahana" class="skt-link">vāhana</a>] le taureau <a href="#entry-Uv_r_sabha" class="skt-link">Vṛṣabha</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-U_r_sabha" class="skt-link">Ṛṣabha</a>, <a href="#entry-Ubasava" class="skt-link">Basava</a> — <a href="#entry-nandinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nandinī</i> myth. np. de Nandinī «Joyeuse», vache d'abondance [<a href="#entry-kaamadughadhenu" class="skt-link">kāmadughadhenu</a>] de <a href="#entry-Uvasi_s_tha" class="skt-link">Vasiṣṭha</a>; elle est fille de <a href="#entry-Usurabhii" class="skt-link">Surabhī</a>; aussi appelée <a href="#entry-Uzabalaa" class="skt-link">Śabalā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Nandinī_1, fille d'un roi de <a href="#entry-Ukaazii" class="skt-link">Kāśī</a>, épouse de <a href="#entry-Uzvaphalka" class="skt-link">Śvaphalka</a> et mère d'<a href="#entry-Uakruura" class="skt-link">Akrūra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nandirudra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nandirudra</i> [<a href="#entry-rudra" class="skt-link">rudra</a>] <a href="#entry-nandirudra" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Nandirudra, aspect joyeux de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nandivardhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nandivardhana</i> [<a href="#entry-vardhana" class="skt-link">vardhana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nandivardhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nandivardhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nandivardhanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nandivardhanā</i> qui augmente la joie, qui apporte le bonheur (de ⟨g.⟩) — <a href="#entry-nandivardhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [BhP.] np. du roi Nandivardhana <a href="#entry-Ujanaka" class="skt-link">Janaka</a> de <a href="#entry-Umithilaa" class="skt-link">Mithilā</a>, fils d'<a href="#entry-Uudaavasu" class="skt-link">Udāvasu</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Usuketu_1" class="skt-link">Suketu_1</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> np. de nombreux personnages.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nandyaavarta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nandyāvarta</i> [<a href="#entry-aavarta" class="skt-link">āvarta</a>] <a href="#entry-nandyaavarta" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. [«tourbillon de joie»] diagramme symbole du <a href="#entry-tiirthafkara" class="skt-link">tīrthaṅkara</a> <a href="#entry-Uaranaatha" class="skt-link">Aranātha</a>, formé d'un <a href="#entry-svastika" class="skt-link">svastika</a> se prolongeant en grecque dans un carré.<br> <a href="#entry-nandi" class="skt-link">nandi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nanu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ननु</span> <i>nanu</i> [<a href="#entry-na" class="skt-link">na</a>-<a href="#entry-nu_2" class="skt-link">nu_2</a>] part. non, pas du tout; jamais <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (interrogation ou affirmation insistante) n'est ce pas? n'est-il pas vrai que? <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> certainement, en effet, sans doute; bien sûr <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ⟨interr. imp.⟩ s'il vous plaît, je vous en prie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> adr. exprime la gentillesse, le reproche ou la perplexité.<br> <i>nanūcyatām</i> veuillez nous dire.<br> <i>nanūpaviśāmaḥ</i> hé bien, nous nous asseyons.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-napaat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नपात्</span> <i>napāt</i> <a href="#entry-napaat" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. descendant; petit-fils, fils <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>nepos</i>; ang. nephew; all. Neffe; fr. neveu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-napt_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नप्तृ</span> <i>naptṛ</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-napaat" class="skt-link">napāt</a>] <a href="#entry-napt_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> petit-fils; cf. <a href="#entry-pautra" class="skt-link">pautra</a>, <a href="#entry-dauhitra" class="skt-link">dauhitra</a> — <a href="#entry-naptrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>naptrī</i> petite-fille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-napu_ms">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नपुंस्</span> <i>napuṃs</i> [contr. <i>nastrīpuṃs</i>] <a href="#entry-napu_ms" class="skt-link">m.</a> eunuque <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>napuṃsa</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-napu_msaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>napuṃsaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-napu_msaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-napu_msaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«ni mâle ni femelle»] hermaphrodite, androgyne — <a href="#entry-napu_msaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> eunuque — <a href="#entry-napu_msaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. genre neutre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-napu_msakanaaman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>napuṃsakanāman</i> [<a href="#entry-naaman" class="skt-link">nāman</a>] a. <a href="#entry-napu_msakanaaman" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-napu_msakanaaman" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-napu_msakanaamnii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>napuṃsakanāmnī</i> qui a un nom neutre.<br> <a href="#entry-napu_msaka" class="skt-link">napuṃsaka</a> <br> <a href="#entry-napu_ms" class="skt-link">napuṃs</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-napul_lifga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नपुलँलिङ्ग</span> <i>napulṁliṅga</i> [contr. <i>nastrīpulṁliṅga</i>] <a href="#entry-napul__lifga" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. genre neutre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नर</span> <i>nara</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-n_r" class="skt-link">nṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-nara" class="skt-link">m.</a> homme, mâle, personne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Nara, l'Homme primordial, fils de <a href="#entry-Udharma" class="skt-link">Dharma</a> et <a href="#entry-Uahi_msaa" class="skt-link">Ahiṃsā</a>, souvent associé à son jumeau <a href="#entry-Unaaraaya_na" class="skt-link">Nārāyaṇa</a>; <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a> est son incarnation sur Terre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pion au jeu d'échecs <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>narās</i> gens, peuple, humanité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naraadhipa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>narādhipa</i> [<a href="#entry-adhipa" class="skt-link">adhipa</a>] <a href="#entry-naraadhipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi [«maître des hommes»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naraaza_msa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>narāśaṃsa</i> [<a href="#entry-za_msa" class="skt-link">śaṃsa</a>] <a href="#entry-naraaza_msa" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. np. de Narāśaṃsa «Qui fait la louange des hommes», forme d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> qui reçoit le premier l'offrande aux dieux; on dit qu'il commande aux épouses des dieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narabali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>narabali</i> [<a href="#entry-bali" class="skt-link">bali</a>] <a href="#entry-narabali" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. sacrifice humain; syn. <a href="#entry-naramedha" class="skt-link">naramedha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narahari">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>narahari</i> [<a href="#entry-hari_2" class="skt-link">hari_2</a>] <a href="#entry-narahari" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Narahari, auteur du Bodhasāra (<a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a>, 18⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नरक</span> <i>naraka</i> [<a href="#entry-nara" class="skt-link">nara</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-naraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. enfer (gardé par <a href="#entry-Unara" class="skt-link">Nara</a>-<a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du titan [<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>] Naraka «Infernal», fils de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>-<a href="#entry-Uvaraaha" class="skt-link">Varāha</a> et de <a href="#entry-Ubhuumi" class="skt-link">Bhūmi</a>; il régnait en Assam sur <a href="#entry-Upraagjyoti_sa" class="skt-link">Prāgjyotiṣa</a> gardé par <a href="#entry-Umura" class="skt-link">Mura</a>, et est considéré l'ancêtre des rois <a href="#entry-Ukaamaruupaas" class="skt-link">Kāmarūpās</a>; par ses pénitences il acquit de grands pouvoirs de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> et devint un tyran; il avait obtenu de ne pouvoir être tué que par sa propre mère, et fut vaincu par <a href="#entry-Usatyabhaamaa" class="skt-link">Satyabhāmā</a>, réincarnation de <a href="#entry-Ubhuumi" class="skt-link">Bhūmi</a>, et achevé par <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>; il se réincarna en <a href="#entry-Ukar_na" class="skt-link">Karṇa</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ubhauma_1" class="skt-link">Bhauma_1</a>, <a href="#entry-diipaavali" class="skt-link">dīpāvali</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narakacaturdazii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>narakacaturdaśī</i> [<a href="#entry-caturdazii" class="skt-link">caturdaśī</a>] <a href="#entry-narakacaturdazii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. quatorzième jour de la quinzaine claire du mois <a href="#entry-kaarttika" class="skt-link">kārttika</a>, commémorant la victoire de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> sur <a href="#entry-Unaraka" class="skt-link">Naraka</a>, aussi appelé <a href="#entry-vaiku_n_thacaturdazii" class="skt-link">vaikuṇṭhacaturdaśī</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-diipaavali" class="skt-link">dīpāvali</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-naraka" class="skt-link">naraka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narakaku_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>narakakuṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_n_da" class="skt-link">kuṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-narakaku_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. puits de torture des méchants en enfer; il y en a 86.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naramedha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>naramedha</i> [<a href="#entry-medha" class="skt-link">medha</a>] <a href="#entry-naramedha" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. sacrifice humain; syn. <a href="#entry-narabali" class="skt-link">narabali</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. crémation funéraire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naranaaraaya_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>naranārāyaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-naaraaya_na" class="skt-link">nārāyaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-naranaaraaya_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> «le Seigneur fait Homme» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> du. <i>naranārāyaṇau</i> myth. <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a> et <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naranaarii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
