    <i>naranārī</i> [<a href="#entry-naarii" class="skt-link">nārī</a>] <a href="#entry-naranaarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> du. <i>naranaryau</i> homme et femme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narapati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>narapati</i> [<a href="#entry-pati" class="skt-link">pati</a>] <a href="#entry-narapati" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-n_rpati" class="skt-link">nṛpati</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narapazu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>narapaśu</i> [<a href="#entry-pazu" class="skt-link">paśu</a>] <a href="#entry-narapazu" class="skt-link">m.</a> brute sous forme humaine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> homme victime d'un sacrifice.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nararaaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nararāja</i> [<a href="#entry-raaja" class="skt-link">rāja</a>] <a href="#entry-nararaaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Nararāja, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a> «Maître des hommes (esclaves des richesses)».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narasi_mha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>narasiṃha</i> var. <i>nṛsiṃha</i> [<a href="#entry-si_mha" class="skt-link">siṃha</a>] <a href="#entry-narasi_mha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Narasiṃha «Homme-lion», 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-avataara" class="skt-link">avatāra</a> de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>, qui détruisit le <a href="#entry-daitya" class="skt-link">daitya</a> <a href="#entry-Uhira_nyakazipu" class="skt-link">Hiraṇyakaśipu</a> en lui arrachant les entrailles; il est le Roi du <a href="#entry-k_rtayuga" class="skt-link">kṛtayuga</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukaraalanarasi_mha" class="skt-link">Karālanarasiṃha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Narasiṃha_1, fils de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nuvardhana" class="skt-link">Viṣṇuvardhana</a>, roi <a href="#entry-Uhoysala" class="skt-link">Hoysala</a> de 1152 à 1173 <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Narasiṃha_2, roi du <a href="#entry-Ukalifga" class="skt-link">Kaliṅga</a> de 1238 à 1264, descendant d'<a href="#entry-Uanantavarmaa" class="skt-link">Anantavarmā</a> <a href="#entry-Ucodagafga" class="skt-link">Codagaṅga</a>; il construisit le temple de <a href="#entry-Uko_naarka" class="skt-link">Koṇārka</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Narasiṃha_3, roi de la dynastie du Deccan au 15⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de <a href="#entry-Usaluva" class="skt-link">Saluva</a> Narasiṃha_4, roi de <a href="#entry-Uvijayanagara" class="skt-link">Vijayanagara</a> (1486–1491); il combattit de nombreux chefs rebelles, et fut fait prisonnier par <a href="#entry-Ugajapati" class="skt-link">Gajapati</a> <a href="#entry-Upuru_sottamadeva_1" class="skt-link">Puruṣottamadeva_1</a>; il monnaya sa liberté contre la reddition de la citadelle d'<a href="#entry-Uudayagiri" class="skt-link">Udayagiri</a>; Narasa <a href="#entry-Unaayaka" class="skt-link">Nāyaka</a> était son ministre et chef des armées; en 1496 celui-ci fit une longue campagne pour soumettre les rois du Sud (<a href="#entry-Ucola" class="skt-link">Cola</a>, <a href="#entry-Uhoysala" class="skt-link">Hoysala</a>, etc.), et il tint en échec le roi <a href="#entry-Uga_napati" class="skt-link">Gaṇapati</a> au Nord; à la mort de l'empereur il assura la régence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Narasiṃha_5 <a href="#entry-Uraaya" class="skt-link">Rāya</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Usaluva" class="skt-link">Saluva</a>, roi de <a href="#entry-Uvijayanagara" class="skt-link">Vijayanagara</a> (1491–1505) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de <a href="#entry-Uviira_1" class="skt-link">Vīra_1</a> Narasiṃha_6, fils aîné de Narasa <a href="#entry-Unaayaka" class="skt-link">Nāyaka</a>; il devint roi de <a href="#entry-Uvijayanagara" class="skt-link">Vijayanagara</a> (1505–1509), inaugurant la dynastie Tuluva.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narasi_mhadiik_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>narasiṃhadīkṣā</i> [<a href="#entry-diik_saa" class="skt-link">dīkṣā</a>] <a href="#entry-narasi_mhadiik_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. rite de consécration royale de la tradition <a href="#entry-vai_s_nava" class="skt-link">vaiṣṇava</a> <a href="#entry-pa_ncaraatra" class="skt-link">pañcarātra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narasi_mhagupta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>narasiṃhagupta</i> [<a href="#entry-gupta" class="skt-link">gupta</a>] <a href="#entry-narasi_mhagupta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de l'empereur Narasiṃhagupta d'Inde du Nord (début du 6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narasi_mhapuraa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>narasiṃhapurāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-narasi_mhapuraa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Narasiṃhapurāṇa, un <a href="#entry-upapuraa_na" class="skt-link">upapurāṇa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-narasi_mha" class="skt-link">narasiṃha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naratva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>naratva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-naratva" class="skt-link">n.</a> qualité d'être un homme, humanité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naravaahana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>naravāhana</i> [<a href="#entry-vaahana" class="skt-link">vāhana</a>] <a href="#entry-naravaahana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Naravāhana, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a> «qui a un homme pour monture».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naravaahanadatta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>naravāhanadatta</i> [<a href="#entry-datta" class="skt-link">datta</a>] <a href="#entry-naravaahanadatta" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-Ub_rhatkathaa" class="skt-link">Bṛhatkathā</a>] np. du prince Naravāhanadatta «don de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a>», mythique fils du roi <a href="#entry-Uudayana" class="skt-link">Udayana</a>; le conte raconte ses amours, et comment il devint empereur des génies <a href="#entry-Uvidyaadharaas" class="skt-link">Vidyādharās</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-naravaahana" class="skt-link">naravāhana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naravarman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>naravarman</i> [<a href="#entry-varman" class="skt-link">varman</a>] <a href="#entry-naravarman" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Naravarmā «Protecteur des hommes», roi <a href="#entry-Uparamaara" class="skt-link">Paramāra</a>, prince du <a href="#entry-Umaalava" class="skt-link">Mālava</a>, fils d'<a href="#entry-Uudayaaditya" class="skt-link">Udayāditya</a>; vers 1100 il rénova le temple de <a href="#entry-Umahaakaala" class="skt-link">Mahākāla</a> à <a href="#entry-Uujjayinii" class="skt-link">Ujjayinī</a>; il encouragea l'étude du sanskrit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nard">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">नर्द्</span> <i>nard</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nardati)</i> pft. <i>(nanarda)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nardita</i><i>)</i> inf. <i>(narditum)</i> mugir; rugir; crier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नर्द</span> <i>narda</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nard" class="skt-link">nard</a>] a. <a href="#entry-narda" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-narda" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nardaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nardā</i> qui mugit, qui rugit; bruyant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nardana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नर्दन</span> <i>nardana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nard" class="skt-link">nard</a>] <a href="#entry-nardana" class="skt-link">n.</a> beuglement; rugissement; cri <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> éloge, célébration.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nardat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नर्दत्</span> <i>nardat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-nard" class="skt-link">nard</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nardat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nardat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nardantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nardantī</i> mugissant, rugissant; criant; proclamant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nardita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नर्दित</span> <i>nardita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nard" class="skt-link">nard</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nardita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nardita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-narditaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>narditā</i> qui a mugit, qui a crié — <a href="#entry-nardita" class="skt-link">n.</a> beuglement; rugissement; cri — <a href="#entry-nardita" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. dé vainqueur au jeu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narendra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>narendra</i> [<a href="#entry-indra" class="skt-link">indra</a>] <a href="#entry-narendra" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi [«seigneur des hommes»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nareza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nareśa</i> [<a href="#entry-iiza" class="skt-link">īśa</a>] <a href="#entry-nareza" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi [«seigneur des hommes»].<br> <i>kāśinareśa</i> titre de roi de Bénarès; durant <a href="#entry-zivaraatri" class="skt-link">śivarātri</a> il est seul à officier dans le sanctum du <a href="#entry-Uvizvanaatha" class="skt-link">Viśvanātha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nareśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-narezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi [«seigneur des hommes»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नर्म</span> <i>narma</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-narman" class="skt-link">narman</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narmada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>narmada</i> [<a href="#entry-da" class="skt-link">da</a>] a. <a href="#entry-narmada" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-narmada" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-narmadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>narmadā</i> amusant, qui divertit — f. cf. <a href="#entry-narmadaa" class="skt-link">narmadā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narmadaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नर्मदा</span> <i>narmadā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-narmada" class="skt-link">narmada</a>] <a href="#entry-narmadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. de la Narmadā, fleuve qui prend naissance au pic <a href="#entry-Uamaraka_n_taka" class="skt-link">Amarakaṇṭaka</a> des monts <a href="#entry-Uvindhya" class="skt-link">Vindhya</a> et se jette dans le golfe de Cambaye (mod. Nerbudda).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narmadaaparikrama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>narmadāparikrama</i> [<a href="#entry-parikrama" class="skt-link">parikrama</a>] <a href="#entry-narmadaaparikrama" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. pélerinage de la <a href="#entry-Unarmadaa" class="skt-link">Narmadā</a>; il suit la forme du symbole <a href="#entry-om" class="skt-link">om</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uofkaarezvara" class="skt-link">Oṃkāreśvara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narmadezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>narmadeśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-narmadezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. galet de quartz sacré à <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>, originaire de la <a href="#entry-Unarmadaa" class="skt-link">Narmadā</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-baa_nalifga" class="skt-link">bāṇaliṅga</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-narmadaa" class="skt-link">narmadā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नर्मन्</span> <i>narman</i> <a href="#entry-narman" class="skt-link">n.</a> amusement, plaisir; divertissement, passe-temps; jeu, sport; plaisanterie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narmasaciva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>narmasaciva</i> [<a href="#entry-saciva" class="skt-link">saciva</a>] <a href="#entry-narmasaciva" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. compagnon de jeux d'un prince.<br> <a href="#entry-narma" class="skt-link">narma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narottama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>narottama</i> [<a href="#entry-uttama" class="skt-link">uttama</a>] <a href="#entry-narottama" class="skt-link">m.</a> le meilleur des hommes <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Narottama, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> «l'Être suprême» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. épith. de <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-nara" class="skt-link">nara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nart">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नर्त्</span> <i>nart</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-n_rt" class="skt-link">nṛt</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(nartayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nartita</i><i>)</i> faire danser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-narta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नर्त</span> <i>narta</i> [act. <a href="#entry-n_rt" class="skt-link">nṛt</a>] <a href="#entry-narta" class="skt-link">m.</a> danse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nartaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नर्तक</span> <i>nartaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-n_rt" class="skt-link">nṛt</a>] <a href="#entry-nartaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> danseur, acteur; maître de danse — <a href="#entry-nartakii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nartakī</i> danseuse, actrice <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>narttaka</i> f. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nartana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नर्तन</span> <i>nartana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-n_rt" class="skt-link">nṛt</a>] <a href="#entry-nartana" class="skt-link">m.</a> danseur — <a href="#entry-nartana" class="skt-link">n.</a> danse, thâtre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nartita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नर्तित</span> <i>nartita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nart" class="skt-link">nart</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nartita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nartita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nartitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nartitā</i> qu'on fait danser — <a href="#entry-nartita" class="skt-link">n.</a> danse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nas_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नस्</span> <i>nas_1</i> cl. pour <i>asmabhyam</i>, <i>asmākam</i>, <i>asmān</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nas_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नस्</span> <i>nas_2</i> <a href="#entry-nas_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> nez <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> du. <i>nāsā</i> narines, naseaux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>nāres</i>; fr. narine; ang. nose.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नस</span> <i>nasa</i> [<a href="#entry-nas_2" class="skt-link">nas_2</a>] ifc. <a href="#entry-nasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> nez de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nastas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nastas</i> [<a href="#entry--tas" class="skt-link">-tas</a>] adv. du nez.<br> <a href="#entry-nas_2" class="skt-link">nas_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नस्य</span> <i>nasya</i> [<a href="#entry-nasa" class="skt-link">nasa</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nasya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nasyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nasyā</i> nasal; qui concerne le nez — <a href="#entry-nasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> poils de nez <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méd. sternutatoire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नत</span> <i>nata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nam" class="skt-link">nam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>natā</i> courbé, tordu; penché, incliné; aplati, enfoncé; prosterné — <a href="#entry-nata" class="skt-link">n.</a> astr. inclinaison — <a href="#entry-nataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>natā</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Natā, fille de <a href="#entry-Uzukii_1" class="skt-link">Śukī_1</a>; <a href="#entry-Uvinataa" class="skt-link">Vinatā</a> est sa fille.<br> <i>natāśubhāśunāśaka</i> qui détruit rapidement les péchés de ceux qui se prosternent devant lui (se dit de <a href="#entry-Uga_neza" class="skt-link">Gaṇeśa</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नति</span> <i>nati</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nam" class="skt-link">nam</a>] <a href="#entry-nati" class="skt-link">f.</a> courbure; inclinaison, salut; humilité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-natu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नतु</span> <i>natu</i> [<a href="#entry-na" class="skt-link">na</a>-<a href="#entry-tu" class="skt-link">tu</a>] conj. plutôt que, et non pas; bien que, néanmoins.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nau_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नौ</span> <i>nau_1</i> <a href="#entry-nau_1" class="skt-link">f.</a> bateau, navire, barque — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(nāvyati)</i> souhaiter un bateau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. ναυς; lat. <i>navis</i>; fr. nef, navire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nau_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नौ</span> <i>nau_2</i> cl. pour <i>āvayos</i>, <i>āvābhyām</i>, <i>āvām</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naukaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>naukā</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-naukaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> (petit) bateau, barque <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pièce corr. à la tour aux échecs [<a href="#entry-caturafgaa" class="skt-link">caturaṅgā</a>]; parfois plutôt appelée <a href="#entry-ratha" class="skt-link">ratha</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-nau_1" class="skt-link">nau_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naulika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नौलिक</span> <i>naulika</i> <a href="#entry-naulika" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] austérité yogique — <a href="#entry-naulii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>naulī</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nava_sa_s_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navaṣaṣṭi</i> [<a href="#entry-_sa_s_ti" class="skt-link">ṣaṣṭi</a>] <a href="#entry-nava_sa_s_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> 69; syn. <a href="#entry-ekonasaptati" class="skt-link">ekonasaptati</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nava_sa_s_titama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navaṣaṣṭitama</i> [<a href="#entry--tama" class="skt-link">-tama</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nava_sa_s_titama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nava_sa_s_titama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nava_sa_s_titamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>navaṣaṣṭitamī</i> ord. 69⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩.<br> <a href="#entry-nava_sa_s_ti" class="skt-link">navaṣaṣṭi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nava_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नव</span> <i>nava_1</i> a. <a href="#entry-nava_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nava_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-navaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>navā</i> neuf, nouveau, récent, frais, jeune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> frais, non cuit (not. pot de terre) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. νεος; lat. <i>novus</i>; ang. new; all. neu; fr. neuf, nouveau.<br> <i>navīkṛ</i> rajeunir, rafraîchir, rénover; ranimer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nava_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नव</span> <i>nava_2</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-navan" class="skt-link">navan</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navaahnika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navāhnika</i> [<a href="#entry-aahnika" class="skt-link">āhnika</a>] <a href="#entry-navaahnika" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. section du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Mahābhāṣya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navaaziiti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navāśīti</i> [<a href="#entry-aziiti" class="skt-link">aśīti</a>] <a href="#entry-navaaziiti" class="skt-link">f.</a> 89.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navaaziititama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navāśītitama</i> [<a href="#entry--tama" class="skt-link">-tama</a>] a. <a href="#entry-navaaziititama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-navaaziititama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-navaaziititamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>navāśītitamī</i> ord. 89⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩.<br> <a href="#entry-navaaziiti" class="skt-link">navāśīti</a> <br> <a href="#entry-nava_2" class="skt-link">nava_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navacatvaari_mzat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navacatvāriṃśat</i> [<a href="#entry-catvaari_mzat" class="skt-link">catvāriṃśat</a>] <a href="#entry-navacatvaari_mzat" class="skt-link">f.</a> 49.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navadaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navadaśa</i> [<a href="#entry-daza" class="skt-link">daśa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-navadaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-navadaza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-navadazii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>navadaśī</i> 19⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navadazan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navadaśan</i> [<a href="#entry-dazan" class="skt-link">daśan</a>] num. <a href="#entry-navadazan" class="skt-link">*</a> 19.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navadvaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navadvāra</i> [<a href="#entry-dvaara" class="skt-link">dvāra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-navadvaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-navadvaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-navadvaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>navadvārā</i> qui a neuf orifices — <a href="#entry-navadvaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> le corps humain (comparé à une ville à 9 portes).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navadviipa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navadvīpa</i> [<a href="#entry-dviipa" class="skt-link">dvīpa</a>] <a href="#entry-navadviipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de la ville de Navadvīpa «Formée de neuf îles» sur une branche du Gange au Bengale, à 130 km au nord de Calcutta (district de <a href="#entry-Unadiiyaa" class="skt-link">Nadīyā</a>); elle fut la capitale de la dynastie <a href="#entry-Usena" class="skt-link">Sena</a> au 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, et un centre important d'enseignement; <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a> y naquit et y vécut dans sa jeunesse; c'est un lieu de pélerinage du <a href="#entry-gau_diiya" class="skt-link">gauḍīya</a> <a href="#entry-sampradaaya" class="skt-link">saṃpradāya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navagraha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navagraha</i> [<a href="#entry-graha" class="skt-link">graha</a>] <a href="#entry-navagraha" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. astr. les 9 influences astrales, comprenant le Soleil [<a href="#entry-Usuurya" class="skt-link">Sūrya</a>], la Lune [<a href="#entry-Ucandra" class="skt-link">Candra</a>], les 5 planètes Mars [<a href="#entry-Uafgaaraka" class="skt-link">Aṅgāraka</a>], Mercure [<a href="#entry-Ubudha" class="skt-link">Budha</a>], Jupiter [<a href="#entry-Ub_rhaspati" class="skt-link">Bṛhaspati</a>], Vénus [<a href="#entry-Uzukra" class="skt-link">Śukra</a>] et Saturne [<a href="#entry-Uzani" class="skt-link">Śani</a>], et les deux démons de l'éclipse <a href="#entry-Uraahu" class="skt-link">Rāhu</a> et de la comète <a href="#entry-Uketu" class="skt-link">Ketu</a>; on les représente souvent sculptés sur le linteau du sanctuaire d'un temple.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navakalevara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navakalevara</i> [<a href="#entry-kalevara" class="skt-link">kalevara</a>] <a href="#entry-navakalevara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-navakalevara" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. cérémonie du nouveau corps, rituel de renouvellement de l'idole de <a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha" class="skt-link">Jagannātha</a> à <a href="#entry-Upurii" class="skt-link">Purī</a> à l'occasion d'un double mois <a href="#entry-aa_saa_dha" class="skt-link">āṣāḍha</a>; l'arbre <a href="#entry-niima" class="skt-link">nīma</a> consacré est trouvé par divination d'un prêtre de <a href="#entry-Umafgalaa" class="skt-link">Maṅgalā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navakarmika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navakarmika</i> [<a href="#entry-karmika" class="skt-link">karmika</a>] <a href="#entry-navakarmika" class="skt-link">m.</a> architecte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navaku_njara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navakuñjara</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_njara" class="skt-link">kuñjara</a>] <a href="#entry-navaku_njara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] [«les neuf trésors»] chimère formée de 9 animaux (éléphant, serpent, taureau, cheval, paon, lion, tigre, coq et perroquet) en laquelle <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> s'est manifesté devant <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a> pour établir sa suprématie pendant l'épisode de <a href="#entry-khaa_n_davadahana" class="skt-link">khāṇḍavadahana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navama</i> [<a href="#entry--ma" class="skt-link">-ma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-navama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-navama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-navamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>navamī</i> ord. neuvième — <a href="#entry-navamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>navamī</i> soc. fête au 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour d'un demi-mois lunaire; cf. <a href="#entry-raamanavamii" class="skt-link">rāmanavamī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navamallikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navamallikā</i> [<a href="#entry-mallikaa" class="skt-link">mallikā</a>] <a href="#entry-navamallikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Jasminum sambac</i>, jasmin blanc, au parfum suave.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navamukha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navamukha</i> [<a href="#entry-mukha" class="skt-link">mukha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-navamukha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-navamukha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-navamukhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>navamukhī</i> qui a neuf orifices.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नवन्</span> <i>navan</i> num. <a href="#entry-navan" class="skt-link">*</a> le nombre neuf <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>novem</i>; ang. nine; fr. neuf.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navanavati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navanavati</i> [<a href="#entry-navati" class="skt-link">navati</a>] num. <a href="#entry-navanavati" class="skt-link">f.</a> 99 <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. [RV.] grand nombre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navanidhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navanidhi</i> [<a href="#entry-nidhi" class="skt-link">nidhi</a>] <a href="#entry-navanidhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] les 9 trésors cachés, gardés par <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a>: <a href="#entry-kacchapa" class="skt-link">kacchapa</a> la tortue, <a href="#entry-makara" class="skt-link">makara</a> le crocodile, <a href="#entry-mukunda" class="skt-link">mukunda</a> le joyau, <a href="#entry-nanda" class="skt-link">nanda</a> le bonheur, <a href="#entry-kharva" class="skt-link">kharva</a> l'innombrable, <a href="#entry-niila" class="skt-link">nīla</a> le saphir, <a href="#entry-zafkha" class="skt-link">śaṅkha</a> la conque, <a href="#entry-padma" class="skt-link">padma</a> le lotus ou <a href="#entry-padmaraaga" class="skt-link">padmarāga</a> le rubis et <a href="#entry-mahaapadma" class="skt-link">mahāpadma</a> le grand lotus, chacun gardé par un <a href="#entry-yak_sa" class="skt-link">yakṣa</a> ou un <a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navaniita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navanīta</i> [<a href="#entry-niita" class="skt-link">nīta</a>] <a href="#entry-navaniita" class="skt-link">n.</a> beurre (frais).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navaniitagola">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navanītagola</i> [<a href="#entry-gola" class="skt-link">gola</a>] <a href="#entry-navaniitagola" class="skt-link">m.</a> boule de beurre.<br> <a href="#entry-navaniita" class="skt-link">navanīta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navaniitagopaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navanītagopāla</i> [<a href="#entry-gopaala" class="skt-link">gopāla</a>] <a href="#entry-navaniitagopaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. représentation de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> enfant volant le beurre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navapallava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navapallava</i> [<a href="#entry-pallava" class="skt-link">pallava</a>] <a href="#entry-navapallava" class="skt-link">n.</a> bourgeon, nouvelle pousse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navapattrikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navapattrikā</i> [<a href="#entry-pattrikaa" class="skt-link">pattrikā</a>] <a href="#entry-navapattrikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. «les 9 feuilles», rite de célébration de la <a href="#entry-durgaapuujaa" class="skt-link">durgāpūjā</a>, où la Déesse est adorée sous la forme des feuilles de neuf plantes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navapa_ncaaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navapañcāśa</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_ncaaza" class="skt-link">pañcāśa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-navapa_ncaaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-navapa_ncaaza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-navapa_ncaazii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>navapañcāśī</i> 59⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navaraatra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navarātra</i> [<a href="#entry-raatra" class="skt-link">rātra</a>] <a href="#entry-navaraatra" class="skt-link">m.</a> période de neuf jours <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. sacrifice de <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a> avec 9 jours d'extraction [<a href="#entry-sutyaa" class="skt-link">sutyā</a>] — <a href="#entry-navaraatra" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. festival de <a href="#entry-durgaapuujaa" class="skt-link">durgāpūjā</a> de la région Bengale-Orissa; il célèbre la victoire de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a> sur l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>-buffle <a href="#entry-Umahi_sa" class="skt-link">Mahiṣa</a>; il se tient les 9 premiers jours de la quinzaine claire [<a href="#entry-zuklapak_sa" class="skt-link">śuklapakṣa</a>] du mois <a href="#entry-aazvina" class="skt-link">āśvina</a> (septembre-octobre); on construit des statues d'argile colorées montrant sa victoire sur <a href="#entry-Umahi_sa" class="skt-link">Mahiṣa</a>; le 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour a lieu la fête <a href="#entry-mahaanavamii" class="skt-link">mahānavamī</a>, où l'on célèbre <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a>-<a href="#entry-Uaparaajitaa" class="skt-link">Aparājitā</a>; le 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour [<a href="#entry-dazaharaa" class="skt-link">daśaharā</a>] on immerge son idole d'argile dans la rivière, symbolisant son retour auprès de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; c'est la fête de la Victoire de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a> [<a href="#entry-vijayadazamii" class="skt-link">vijayadaśamī</a>]; lors de ce festival est jouée la geste de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> [<a href="#entry-raamaliilaa" class="skt-link">rāmalīlā</a>], et le 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour on brûle des effigies de <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukumbhakar_na" class="skt-link">Kumbhakarṇa</a> et <a href="#entry-Umeghanaada" class="skt-link">Meghanāda</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hi. <i>navarātri</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navarafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navaraṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-rafga" class="skt-link">raṅga</a>] <a href="#entry-navarafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. astr. les neuf couleurs des gemmes associés à l'influence d'un astre; cf. <a href="#entry-navaratna" class="skt-link">navaratna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navaratna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navaratna</i> [<a href="#entry-ratna" class="skt-link">ratna</a>] <a href="#entry-navaratna" class="skt-link">n.</a> pl. <i>navaratnāni</i> astr. les neuf gemmes associés à l'influence d'un astre [<a href="#entry-navagraha" class="skt-link">navagraha</a>]: le rubis [<a href="#entry-maa_nikya" class="skt-link">māṇikya</a>] au Soleil [<a href="#entry-Usuurya" class="skt-link">Sūrya</a>], la perle [<a href="#entry-muktaa" class="skt-link">muktā</a>] à la Lune [<a href="#entry-Ucandra" class="skt-link">Candra</a>], le corail à Mars [<a href="#entry-Umafgala" class="skt-link">Maṅgala</a>], l'émeraude [<a href="#entry-marakata" class="skt-link">marakata</a>] à Mercure [<a href="#entry-Ubudha" class="skt-link">Budha</a>], la topaze [<a href="#entry-pu_syaraaga" class="skt-link">puṣyarāga</a>] à Jupiter [<a href="#entry-Uguru" class="skt-link">Guru</a>], le diamant [<a href="#entry-vajra" class="skt-link">vajra</a>] à Vénus [<a href="#entry-Uzukra" class="skt-link">Śukra</a>], le saphir [<a href="#entry-niila" class="skt-link">nīla</a>] à Saturne [<a href="#entry-Uzani" class="skt-link">Śani</a>], le lapis-lazuli [<a href="#entry-raajaavarta" class="skt-link">rājāvarta</a>] à l'éclipse [<a href="#entry-Uraahu" class="skt-link">Rāhu</a>], et le quartz hyacinthe [<a href="#entry-gomeda" class="skt-link">gomeda</a>] à la comète [<a href="#entry-Uketu" class="skt-link">Ketu</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. [«les neuf joyaux de la cour»] les neuf illustres poètes/savants traditionnellement associés à la cour du roi <a href="#entry-Uvikramaaditya" class="skt-link">Vikramāditya</a> à <a href="#entry-Uavanti" class="skt-link">Avanti</a>: <a href="#entry-Udhanvantari_2" class="skt-link">Dhanvantari_2</a>, <a href="#entry-Uk_sapa_naka" class="skt-link">Kṣapaṇaka</a>, <a href="#entry-Uamarasi_mha" class="skt-link">Amarasiṃha</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzafku" class="skt-link">Śaṅku</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukaalidaasa" class="skt-link">Kālidāsa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvetaalabha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Vetālabhaṭṭa</a>, <a href="#entry-Ugha_taparpara" class="skt-link">Ghaṭaparpara</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvararuci" class="skt-link">Vararuci</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvaraahamihira" class="skt-link">Varāhamihira</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. les neuf ouvrages principaux de <a href="#entry-Uraamaanuja" class="skt-link">Rāmānuja</a>: <a href="#entry-zrii" class="skt-link">śrī</a> <a href="#entry-bhaa_sya" class="skt-link">bhāṣya</a> (commentaire du <a href="#entry-Ubrahmasuutra" class="skt-link">Brahmasūtra</a>), Vaikuṇṭhagadya, Śrīraṅgagadya, Saranagatigadya, Vedārthasaṅgraha, <a href="#entry-Uvedaantasaara_1" class="skt-link">Vedāntasāra_1</a>, Vedāntadīpa, et Gītābhāṣya (commentaire de la <a href="#entry-Ugiitaa" class="skt-link">Gītā</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navasaptati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navasaptati</i> [<a href="#entry-saptati" class="skt-link">saptati</a>] <a href="#entry-navasaptati" class="skt-link">f.</a> 79.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navasaptatitama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navasaptatitama</i> [<a href="#entry--tama" class="skt-link">-tama</a>] a. <a href="#entry-navasaptatitama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-navasaptatitama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-navasaptatitamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>navasaptatitamī</i> ord. 79⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩.<br> <a href="#entry-navasaptati" class="skt-link">navasaptati</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-navataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> nouveauté; fraîcheur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navati</i> [<a href="#entry--ti" class="skt-link">-ti</a>] num. <a href="#entry-navati" class="skt-link">f.</a> sg. quatre-vingt-dix.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-navatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> nouveauté; fraîcheur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navav_rndaavana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navavṛndāvana</i> [<a href="#entry-v_rndaavana" class="skt-link">vṛndāvana</a>] <a href="#entry-navav_rndaavana" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. de Navavṛndāvana, lieu de mémoire, où reposent les cendres de 9 disciples de <a href="#entry-Umadhvaacaarya" class="skt-link">Madhvācārya</a> dans un parc de cénotaphes sur une île de la <a href="#entry-Utufgabhadraa" class="skt-link">Tuṅgabhadrā</a>, près de mod. Hampi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navazata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navaśata</i> [<a href="#entry-zata" class="skt-link">śata</a>] <a href="#entry-navazata" class="skt-link">n.</a> 109 — a. <a href="#entry-navazata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-navazata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-navazataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>navaśatā</i> pl. 900.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navi_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नविष्ठ</span> <i>naviṣṭha</i> [super. <a href="#entry-nava_1" class="skt-link">nava_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-navi_s_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-navi_s_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-navi_s_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>naviṣṭhā</i> le plus récent, le plus jeune; le dernier — <a href="#entry-navi_s_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> ou d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naviik_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नवीकृ</span> <i>navīkṛ</i> cf. <a href="#entry-nava_1" class="skt-link">nava_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naviik_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नवीकृत</span> <i>navīkṛta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-naviik_r" class="skt-link">navīkṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-naviik_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naviik_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naviik_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>navīkṛtā</i> rajeuni, rafraîchi, rénové.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naviina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नवीन</span> <i>navīna</i> [<a href="#entry-nava_1" class="skt-link">nava_1</a><a href="#entry--iina" class="skt-link">-īna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-naviina" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naviina" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naviinaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>navīnā</i> nouveau, neuf.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naviiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नवीय</span> <i>navīya</i> [<a href="#entry-nava_1" class="skt-link">nava_1</a><a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-naviiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naviiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naviiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>navīyā</i> véd. nouveau, neuf.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naviiyas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नवीयस्</span> <i>navīyas</i> [compar. <a href="#entry-nava_1" class="skt-link">nava_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-naviiyas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naviiyas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naviiyasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>navīyasī</i> nouveau, frais, jeune, récent — adv. récemment.<br> <i>navīyasā</i> adv. récemment.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navotthaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navotthāna</i> [<a href="#entry-utthaana" class="skt-link">utthāna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-navotthaana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-navotthaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-navotthaanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>navotthānā</i> nouvellement levé (astre) — <a href="#entry-navotthaana" class="skt-link">m.</a> renaissance.<br> <a href="#entry-nava_1" class="skt-link">nava_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नव्य</span> <i>navya</i> [<a href="#entry-naviiya" class="skt-link">navīya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-navya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-navya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-navyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>navyā</i> nouveau, neuf.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navyanyaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navyanyāya</i> [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] <a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. nouvelle logique (opp. <a href="#entry-praaciinanyaaya" class="skt-link">prācīnanyāya</a>); le fondateur de cette école [<a href="#entry-muulagranthakaara" class="skt-link">mūlagranthakāra</a>] est <a href="#entry-Ugafgeza" class="skt-link">Gaṅgeśa</a>; elle écarte les notions de philosophie générale pour se recentrer sur la dialectique et le raisonnement [<a href="#entry-tarkazaastra" class="skt-link">tarkaśāstra</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navyanyaayabhaa_saapradiipa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navyanyāyabhāṣāpradīpa</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_saa" class="skt-link">bhāṣā</a>-<a href="#entry-pradiipa" class="skt-link">pradīpa</a>] <a href="#entry-navyanyaayabhaa_saapradiipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. np. du Navyanyāyabhāṣāpradīpa «Lumière sur le langage du <a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a>», ouvrage didactique de Maheśacandra Nyāyaratna (fin 19⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).<br> <a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a> <br> <a href="#entry-navya" class="skt-link">navya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-navyanyaayaparibhaa_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>navyanyāyaparibhāṣā</i> [<a href="#entry-paribhaa_saa" class="skt-link">paribhāṣā</a>] <a href="#entry-navyanyaayaparibhaa_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. langage technique du <a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a>, calcul conceptuel basé sur l'élaboration de paraphrases non ambigües.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नय</span> <i>naya</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nii_1" class="skt-link">nī_1</a>] <a href="#entry-naya" class="skt-link">m.</a> qui mène (not. une armée) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Naya «Guide», fils de <a href="#entry-Udharma" class="skt-link">Dharma</a> et de <a href="#entry-Ukriyaa" class="skt-link">Kriyā</a> — <a href="#entry-naya" class="skt-link">m.</a> conduite, manière d'agir; gouvernance, politique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> prudence, sagesse, habileté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> prévoyance, dessein, plan; méthode, doctrine — iic. avec sagesse.<br> <i>nayeṣu</i> de manière prudente.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नयन</span> <i>nayana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nii_1" class="skt-link">nī_1</a>] <a href="#entry-nayana" class="skt-link">n.</a> conduite, comportement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait de diriger — <a href="#entry-nayana" class="skt-link">n.</a> œil — <a href="#entry-nayanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nayanā</i> <a href="#entry-nayanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nayanī</i> pupille de l'œil.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nayanonmiilana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nayanonmīlana</i> [<a href="#entry-unmiilana" class="skt-link">unmīlana</a>] <a href="#entry-nayanonmiilana" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. rite de consécration d'une idole par «ouverture de ses yeux».<br> <a href="#entry-nayana" class="skt-link">nayana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nayat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नयत्</span> <i>nayat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-nii_1" class="skt-link">nī_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nayat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nayat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nayantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nayantī</i> menant, conduisant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nayavaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nayavāda</i> [<a href="#entry-vaada" class="skt-link">vāda</a>] <a href="#entry-nayavaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. doctrine des points de vue; elle rend compte de la nature multiforme de la réalité [<a href="#entry-anekaantavaada" class="skt-link">anekāntavāda</a>] en proposant une logique modale selon différents modes de prédication [<a href="#entry-syaadvaada" class="skt-link">syādvāda</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-naya" class="skt-link">naya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nayavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नयवत्</span> <i>nayavat</i> [<a href="#entry-naya" class="skt-link">naya</a><a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nayavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nayavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nayavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nayavatī</i> habile en politique; avisé, sage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naz_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">नश्</span> <i>naś_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(naśyati)</i> pft. <i>(nanāśa)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>naṣṭa</i><i>)</i> inf. <i>(naṣṭum)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> périr, mourir; être détruit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> disparaître, s'enfuir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se perdre — ca. <i>(nāśayati)</i> faire périr, détruire; s'écarter de ⟨abl.⟩; oublier; renoncer à ⟨acc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>necis</i>; fr. évanescent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-naz_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नश्</span> <i>naś_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-naz_1" class="skt-link">naś_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-naz_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-naz_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-naz_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui périt.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-na_n">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नञ्</span> <i>nañ</i> nota. gram. marque de la négation ‘<a href="#entry-na" class="skt-link">na</a>’.<br> <i>dvau</i> <i>nañau</i> <i>prakṛtārthaṃ</i> <i>gamayataḥ</i> Deux négations valent affirmation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-na_nartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nañartha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] <a href="#entry-na_nartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. sens de ‘<a href="#entry-na" class="skt-link">na</a>’.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-na_nbahuvriihi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nañbahuvrīhi</i> [<a href="#entry-bahuvriihi" class="skt-link">bahuvrīhi</a>] <a href="#entry-na_nbahuvriihi" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. mot composé exocentrique privatif, comme <a href="#entry-aputra" class="skt-link">aputra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-na_ntatpuru_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nañtatpuruṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-tatpuru_sa" class="skt-link">tatpuruṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-na_ntatpuru_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. mot composé déterminatif privatif, comme <a href="#entry-abraahma_na" class="skt-link">abrāhmaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-na_nvaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nañvāda</i> [<a href="#entry-vaada" class="skt-link">vāda</a>] <a href="#entry-na_nvaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Nañvāda «Discussion sur la négation», ouvrage de <a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Uraghunaatha_1" class="skt-link">Raghunātha_1</a> <a href="#entry-Uziroma_ni" class="skt-link">Śiromaṇi</a>; aussi appelé Nañarthavivṛti, Nañvyutpatti et Nañviveka.<br> <a href="#entry-na_n" class="skt-link">nañ</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ne_s_t_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नेष्टृ</span> <i>neṣṭṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nii_1" class="skt-link">nī_1</a>] <a href="#entry-ne_s_t_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. un des 3 prêtres assistant l'<a href="#entry-adhvaryu" class="skt-link">adhvaryu</a> dans le sacrifice védique; il guide la femme du sacrifiant et prépare la <a href="#entry-suraa" class="skt-link">surā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nedya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नेद्य</span> <i>nedya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-nind" class="skt-link">nind</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nedya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nedya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nedyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nedyā</i> à blâmer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nej">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नेज्</span> <i>nej</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-nij" class="skt-link">nij</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(nejayati)</i> faire nettoyer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nejaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नेजक</span> <i>nejaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nij" class="skt-link">nij</a>] <a href="#entry-nejaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> nettoyeur, laveur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nejame_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नेजमेष</span> <i>nejameṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-me_sa" class="skt-link">meṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-nejame_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Nejameṣa «Croque-mitaine», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvizaakha" class="skt-link">Viśākha</a>, issu du flanc de <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a>, créature à tête de bélier; on l'invoque pour la fertilité, mais il peut aussi faire du mal aux enfants en tant que <a href="#entry-Uskandagraha" class="skt-link">Skandagraha</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Unaigame_sa" class="skt-link">Naigameṣa</a>, <a href="#entry-Unaigameya" class="skt-link">Naigameya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nejana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नेजन</span> <i>nejana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nij" class="skt-link">nij</a>] <a href="#entry-nejana" class="skt-link">n.</a> nettoyage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nema">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नेम</span> <i>nema</i> a. <a href="#entry-nema" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nema" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nemaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nemā</i> un; plusieurs — iic. demi — <a href="#entry-nema" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a> Nema <a href="#entry-Ubhaargava" class="skt-link">Bhārgava</a>, auteur d'un hymne du <a href="#entry-U_rgveda" class="skt-link">Ṛgveda</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> portion; limite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nemi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नेमि</span> <i>nemi</i> [<a href="#entry-nam" class="skt-link">nam</a>] <a href="#entry-nemi" class="skt-link">f.</a> jante de roue; circonférence; bord.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-neminaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>neminātha</i> [<a href="#entry-naatha" class="skt-link">nātha</a>] <a href="#entry-neminaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. np. de Neminātha «Maître de la roue (de la fortune)», 22⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-tiirthafkara" class="skt-link">tīrthaṅkara</a> mythique; son corps est rouge foncé, son symbole est une conque marine; on l'appelle aussi <a href="#entry-Uari_s_tanemi" class="skt-link">Ariṣṭanemi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-neminaathapuraa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nemināthapurāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-neminaathapuraa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Nemināthapurāṇa, recueil de tradition jaïne.<br> <a href="#entry-nemi" class="skt-link">nemi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nepaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नेपाल</span> <i>nepāla</i> <a href="#entry-nepaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du Nepāla ou Népal.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nepathya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नेपथ्य</span> <i>nepathya</i> <a href="#entry-nepathya" class="skt-link">n.</a> (théâtre) ornement, décoration, attirail de scène; parure, costume d'acteur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> arrière-scène (séparée de la scène [<a href="#entry-rafga" class="skt-link">raṅga</a>] par un rideau); coulisses — <a href="#entry-nepathyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nepathyā</i> ifc. costume de.<br> <i>nepathye</i> en coulisses.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nepathyag_rha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nepathyagṛha</i> [<a href="#entry-g_rha" class="skt-link">gṛha</a>] <a href="#entry-nepathyag_rha" class="skt-link">n.</a> salon de toilette, loge.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nepathyaprayoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nepathyaprayoga</i> [<a href="#entry-prayoga" class="skt-link">prayoga</a>] <a href="#entry-nepathyaprayoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> l'art de la toilette [<a href="#entry-nepathyaprayoga" class="skt-link">nepathyaprayoga</a>], l'un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-nepathya" class="skt-link">nepathya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-net_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नेतृ</span> <i>netṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nii_1" class="skt-link">nī_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-net_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-net_r" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-netrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>netrī</i> qui conduit, qui mène; conducteur, guide, chef.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-net_rtva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>netṛtva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-net_rtva" class="skt-link">n.</a> aptitude au commandement; qualité de guide <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bureau du commandant, quartier général.<br> <a href="#entry-net_r" class="skt-link">netṛ</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-netavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नेतव्य</span> <i>netavya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <a href="#entry-nii_1" class="skt-link">nī_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-netavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-netavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-netavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>netavyā</i> qui doit être conduit ou guidé; qui doit être reconduit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-neti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नेति</span> <i>neti</i> [<a href="#entry-na" class="skt-link">na</a>-<a href="#entry-iti" class="skt-link">iti</a>] adv. pas ainsi.<br> <i>neti</i> <i>neti</i> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] Ni ceci, ni cela (cette formule évoque l'incommunicabilité du Divin, qui ne peut être caractérisé qu'en niant qu'il ait aucune propriété particulière).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-netii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नेती</span> <i>netī</i> <a href="#entry-netii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] nettoyage des voies nasales (par un linge qu'on ressort par la bouche).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-netra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नेत्र</span> <i>netra</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-nii_1" class="skt-link">nī_1</a>] <a href="#entry-netra" class="skt-link">m.</a> ifc. conducteur, guide, chef — <a href="#entry-netra" class="skt-link">n.</a> œil <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 2 <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> corde (servant à faire tourner la baratte ou le bâton à feu) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> conduit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-netrakamala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>netrakamala</i> [<a href="#entry-kamala" class="skt-link">kamala</a>] <a href="#entry-netrakamala" class="skt-link">m.</a> œil de lotus (symbole de beauté).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-netrakoza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>netrakośa</i> [<a href="#entry-koza" class="skt-link">kośa</a>] <a href="#entry-netrakoza" class="skt-link">m.</a> globe oculaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-netratantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>netratantra</i> [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] <a href="#entry-netratantra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Netratantra, texte tantrique du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> du début du 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il traite de sorcellerie et de démonologie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-netrayoni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>netrayoni</i> [<a href="#entry-yoni" class="skt-link">yoni</a>] <a href="#entry-netrayoni" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Netrayoni, épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> maudit de devoir porter 1000 marques de vagin sur son corps (plus tard changées en 1000 yeux) après son adultère avec <a href="#entry-Uahalyaa" class="skt-link">Ahalyā</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-sahasraak_sa" class="skt-link">sahasrākṣa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-netrotpaa_tana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>netrotpāṭana</i> [<a href="#entry-utpaa_tana" class="skt-link">utpāṭana</a>] <a href="#entry-netrotpaa_tana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait d'arracher les yeux.<br> <a href="#entry-netra" class="skt-link">netra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-neya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नेय</span> <i>neya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-nii_1" class="skt-link">nī_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-neya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-neya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-neyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>neyā</i> à conduire, à apporter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नि</span> <i>ni</i> pf. à l'intérieur, en bas, sous <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> manque de, dépourvu de, cessation de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_hs_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निःसृ</span> <i>niḥsṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-s_r" class="skt-link">sṛ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(niḥsarati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niḥsṛta</i><i>)</i> sortir, se retirer de, couler hors de — ca. <i>(niḥsārayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niḥsārita</i><i>)</i> faire sortir, évacuer (de ⟨abl.⟩) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> conclure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_hs_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निःसृत</span> <i>niḥsṛta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ni_hs_r" class="skt-link">niḥsṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_hs_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_hs_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_hs_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niḥsṛtā</i> sorti, parti (hors de ⟨iic. abl.⟩) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> exorbité (œil).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_hsaarita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निःसारित</span> <i>niḥsārita</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-ni_hs_r" class="skt-link">niḥsṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_hsaarita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_hsaarita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_hsaaritaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niḥsāritā</i> évacué, renvoyé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_hsafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निःसङ्ग</span> <i>niḥsaṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-safga" class="skt-link">saṅga</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_hsafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_hsafga" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_hsafgaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niḥsaṅgā</i> désintéressé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_hsara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निःसर</span> <i>niḥsara</i> [<a href="#entry-ni_hs_r" class="skt-link">niḥsṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_hsara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_hsara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_hsaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niḥsarā</i> qui sort.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_hsara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निःसरण</span> <i>niḥsaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-ni_hsara" class="skt-link">niḥsara</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-ni_hsara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> sortie, évacuation; départ — ifc. moyen de se débarrasser de.<br> <i>āhāraniḥsaraṇamārga</i> anus.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_hsa_nj_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निःसंज्ञ</span> <i>niḥsaṃjña</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-sa_nj_naa_2" class="skt-link">saṃjñā_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_hsa_mj_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_hsa_mj_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_hsa_mj_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niḥsaṃjñā</i> [«qui ne se connaît plus»] inconscient, hors de soi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_hsp_rha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निःस्पृह</span> <i>niḥspṛha</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-sp_rhaa" class="skt-link">spṛhā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_hsp_rha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_hsp_rha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_hsp_rhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niḥspṛhā</i> exempt de désirs, indifférent, détaché.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_hsphur">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निःस्फुर्</span> <i>niḥsphur</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-sphur" class="skt-link">sphur</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(niḥsphurati)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(niḥṣphurati)</i> lancer au loin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_hzafka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निःशङ्क</span> <i>niḥśaṅka</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-zafkaa" class="skt-link">śaṅkā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_hzafka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_hzafka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_hzafkaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niḥśaṅkā</i> sans peur; intrépide <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sans risque, sûr — ifc. qui n'a pas peur de — adv. en sécurité; sans crainte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_hziitam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निःशीतम्</span> <i>niḥśītam</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-siitaa" class="skt-link">sītā</a>] adv. avec cessation du froid.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_hzreyasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निःश्रेयस</span> <i>niḥśreyasa</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-zreyas" class="skt-link">śreyas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_hzreyasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_hzreyasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_hzreyasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niḥśreyasī</i> insurpassable; excellent, meilleur — <a href="#entry-ni_hzreyasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> le meilleur; béatitude <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> émancipation, réalisation personnelle, foi; salut.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_hzvaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निःश्वास</span> <i>niḥśvāsa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ni_hzvas" class="skt-link">niḥśvas</a>] <a href="#entry-ni_hzvaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> expiration — <a href="#entry-ni_hzvaasaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niḥśvāsā</i> ifc. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_hzvaasaandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niḥśvāsāndha</i> [<a href="#entry-andha" class="skt-link">andha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_hzvaasaandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_hzvaasaandha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_hzvaasaandhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niḥśvāsāndhā</i> rendu opaque par (la buée de) la respiration (miroir).<br> <a href="#entry-ni_hzvaasa" class="skt-link">niḥśvāsa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_hzvas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निःश्वस्</span> <i>niḥśvas</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-zvas_1" class="skt-link">śvas_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(niḥśvasiti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niḥśvasita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> inspirer; renifler; soupirer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_hzvasita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निःश्वसित</span> <i>niḥśvasita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ni_hzvas" class="skt-link">niḥśvas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_hzvasita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_hzvasita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_hzvasitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niḥśvasitā</i> aspiré, inspiré — <a href="#entry-ni_hzvasita" class="skt-link">n.</a> expiration; soupir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_hzvasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निःश्वस्य</span> <i>niḥśvasya</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-ni_hzvas" class="skt-link">niḥśvas</a>] ind. ayant soupiré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_s_naa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्णा</span> <i>niṣṇā</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-snaa" class="skt-link">snā</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(niṣṇāti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niṣṇāta</i><i>)</i> être plongé ou absorbé dans ⟨loc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_s_naata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्णात</span> <i>niṣṇāta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ni_s_naa" class="skt-link">niṣṇā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_s_naata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_s_naata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_s_naataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣṇātā</i> versé dans, habile, expérimenté, instruit en ⟨loc. iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_s_tap">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्टप्</span> <i>niṣṭap</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-tap" class="skt-link">tap</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(niṣṭapati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niṣṭapta</i><i>)</i> brûler, chauffer; rôtir, frire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_s_tarkya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्टर्क्य</span> <i>niṣṭarkya</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-tarkya" class="skt-link">tarkya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_s_tarkya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_s_tarkya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_s_tarkyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣṭarkyā</i> véd. qui se laisse défaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्ठ</span> <i>niṣṭha</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ni_s_thaa_1" class="skt-link">niṣṭhā_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-ni_s_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_s_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_s_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣṭhā</i> situé sur, reposant sur ⟨loc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> relatif à, concernant; qui dépend de; adonné à, absorbé dans <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui conduit à, qui produit ⟨dat.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. objet cible d'une relation; opp. <a href="#entry-niruupita" class="skt-link">nirūpita</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_s_thaa_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्ठा</span> <i>niṣṭhā_1</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-sthaa_1" class="skt-link">sthā_1</a>] pp. <i>(</i><i>niṣṭhita</i><i>)</i> émettre, produire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_s_thaa_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्ठा</span> <i>niṣṭhā_2</i> [f. <a href="#entry-ni_s_tha" class="skt-link">niṣṭha</a>] <a href="#entry-ni_s_thaa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> état, condition; absorption dans ⟨loc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> certitude, connaissance parfaite, foi dans ⟨loc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> conclusion, point culminant; fin <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. suffixe d'adjectif verbal (<i>-ta</i> ou <i>-tavat</i>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. (théâtre) dénouement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_s_thaavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niṣṭhāvat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_s_thaavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_s_thaavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_s_thaavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣṭhāvatī</i> achevé, complet, consommé; parfait <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> religieux.<br> <a href="#entry-ni_s_thaa_2" class="skt-link">niṣṭhā_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_s_thiiv">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्ठीव्</span> <i>niṣṭhīv</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-_s_thiiv" class="skt-link">ṣṭhīv</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(niṣṭhīvati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niṣṭhīvita</i><i>)</i> cracher, expectorer; éjaculer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_s_thiivana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्ठीवन</span> <i>niṣṭhīvana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ni_s_thiiv" class="skt-link">niṣṭhīv</a>] <a href="#entry-ni_s_thiivana" class="skt-link">n.</a> crachat, salive.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_s_thita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्ठित</span> <i>niṣṭhita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ni_s_thaa_1" class="skt-link">niṣṭhā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_s_thita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_s_thita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_s_thitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣṭhitā</i> parfait, accompli <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> doué à, expert en, champion de ⟨loc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ferme, fixé; certain, déterminé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_s_tigrii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्टिग्री</span> <i>niṣṭigrī</i> <a href="#entry-ni_s_tigrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> véd. np. de Niṣṭigrī, mère d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_s_tya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्ट्य</span> <i>niṣṭya</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a><a href="#entry--tya" class="skt-link">-tya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_s_tya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_s_tya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_s_tyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣṭyā</i> étranger — <a href="#entry-ni_s_tya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. homme de basse caste; barbare; cf. <a href="#entry-ca_n_daala" class="skt-link">caṇḍāla</a>, <a href="#entry-mleccha" class="skt-link">mleccha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sa_n_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषण्ण</span> <i>niṣaṇṇa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ni_sad" class="skt-link">niṣad</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_sa_n_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_sa_n_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_sa_n_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣaṇṇā</i> assis <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. (<a href="#entry-sattra" class="skt-link">sattra</a>) performé assis.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_saada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषाद</span> <i>niṣāda</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ni_sad" class="skt-link">niṣad</a>] <a href="#entry-ni_saada" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. indigène non <a href="#entry-aarya" class="skt-link">ārya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. membre d'une tribu de chasseurs sauvages <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> chasseur; homme déchu de sa caste; voleur; homme endurçi dans le péché (tel le chasseur qui tua le mâle d'un couple d'oiseaux dans le <a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>); cf. <a href="#entry-Uekalavya" class="skt-link">Ekalavya</a>, <a href="#entry-Uguha_1" class="skt-link">Guha_1</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvena" class="skt-link">Vena</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-bhilla" class="skt-link">bhilla</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] pêcheurs d'une île, donnés en pâture à <a href="#entry-Ugaru_da" class="skt-link">Garuḍa</a> par sa mère <a href="#entry-Ukadruu" class="skt-link">Kadrū</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (mus.) 7⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ note de l'octave indienne — <a href="#entry-ni_saadii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣādī</i> soc. femme tribale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_saadin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषादिन्</span> <i>niṣādin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ni_sad" class="skt-link">niṣad</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_saadin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_saadin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_saadinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣādinī</i> qui s'assoit sur, qui repose sur — <a href="#entry-ni_saadin" class="skt-link">m.</a> conducteur d'éléphant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषद्</span> <i>niṣad</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-sad_1" class="skt-link">sad_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(niṣīdati)</i> pft. <i>(niṣasāda)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niṣaṇṇa</i><i>)</i> s'asseoir, être assis <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'étendre, être couché, reposer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषद</span> <i>niṣada</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ni_sad" class="skt-link">niṣad</a>] <a href="#entry-ni_sada" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-ni_saada" class="skt-link">niṣāda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sadha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषध</span> <i>niṣadha</i> <a href="#entry-ni_sadha" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. pays du nord-ouest de l'Inde <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du roi Niṣadha, titre de <a href="#entry-Uviirasena" class="skt-link">Vīrasena</a>, roi du pays <a href="#entry-ni_sadha" class="skt-link">niṣadha</a>; aussi son fils <a href="#entry-Unala" class="skt-link">Nala</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. np. du peuple des Niṣadhās — <a href="#entry-ni_sadhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣadhā</i> myth. np. de Niṣadhā, capitale du roi <a href="#entry-Unala" class="skt-link">Nala</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sedha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषेध</span> <i>niṣedha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ni_sidh" class="skt-link">niṣidh</a>] <a href="#entry-ni_sedha" class="skt-link">m.</a> prohibition, interdiction; contradiction, négation; exception <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. négation (avec particule <a href="#entry-na" class="skt-link">na</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsā</a>] injonction d'interdiction, prohibition; opp. <a href="#entry-vidhi" class="skt-link">vidhi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sedhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषेधन</span> <i>niṣedhana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ni_sidh" class="skt-link">niṣidh</a>] <a href="#entry-ni_sedhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> défense, interdiction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sedhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषेध्य</span> <i>niṣedhya</i> [pfp.
