[<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-ni_sidh" class="skt-link">niṣidh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_sedhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_sedhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_sedhyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣedhyā</i> à prohiber, à interdire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_seka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषेक</span> <i>niṣeka</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ni_sic" class="skt-link">niṣic</a>] <a href="#entry-ni_seka" class="skt-link">m.</a> arrosage; infusion; aspersion; fuite de gouttes <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. infusion séminale, imprégnation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> eau de lavage, rinçure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sektavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषेक्तव्य</span> <i>niṣektavya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <a href="#entry-ni_sic" class="skt-link">niṣic</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_sektavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_sektavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_sektavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣektavyā</i> ⟨loc.⟩ qu'il faut arroser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sev">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषेव्</span> <i>niṣev</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-sev" class="skt-link">sev</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(niṣevate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niṣevita</i><i>)</i> fréquenter, habiter; rechercher, visiter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> servir, honorer, se consacrer à; cultiver <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> suivre, pratiquer, exécuter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sic">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषिच्</span> <i>niṣic</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-sic" class="skt-link">sic</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(niṣiñcati)</i> pft. <i>(niṣiṣeca)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niṣikta</i><i>)</i> asperger; verser dans; infuser; irriger; tremper dans — ca. <i>(niṣecayati)</i> irriger; mouiller, imprégner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_siddha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषिद्ध</span> <i>niṣiddha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ni_sidh" class="skt-link">niṣidh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_siddha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_siddha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_siddhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣiddhā</i> restreint, défendu, prohibé; interdit (de ⟨inf.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sidh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषिध्</span> <i>niṣidh</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-sidh_2" class="skt-link">sidh_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(niṣedhati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niṣiddha</i><i>)</i> repousser, écarter; empêcher de ⟨abl.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> surpasser — ca. <i>(niṣedhayati)</i> prohiber, interdire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sikta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषिक्त</span> <i>niṣikta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ni_sic" class="skt-link">niṣic</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_sikta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_sikta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_siktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣiktā</i> arrosé, infusé, irrigué.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sk_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्कृ</span> <i>niṣkṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">8</span>] pr. <i>(niṣkaroti)</i> pr. md. <i>(niṣkurute)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niṣkṛta</i><i>)</i> extraire; supprimer, défaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> préparer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sk_r_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्कृष्</span> <i>niṣkṛṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-k_r_s" class="skt-link">kṛṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(niṣkṛṣati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niṣkṛṣṭa</i><i>)</i> extraire, tirer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sk_r_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्कृष्ट</span> <i>niṣkṛṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ni_sk_r_s" class="skt-link">niṣkṛṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_sk_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_sk_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_sk_r_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣkṛṣṭā</i> extrait.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sk_rpa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्कृप</span> <i>niṣkṛpa</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-k_rpa" class="skt-link">kṛpa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_sk_rpa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_sk_rpa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_sk_rpaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣkṛpā</i> sans pitié, cruel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sk_rt">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्कृत्</span> <i>niṣkṛt</i> var. <i>nikṛt</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-k_rt_1" class="skt-link">kṛt_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(niṣkṛntati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niṣkṛtta</i><i>)</i> tailler, trancher, retrancher, découper, diviser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sk_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्कृत</span> <i>niṣkṛta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ni_sk_r" class="skt-link">niṣkṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_sk_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_sk_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_sk_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣkṛtā</i> supprimé; expédié; banni; expié — <a href="#entry-ni_sk_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> expiation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_skaama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्काम</span> <i>niṣkāma</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-kaama" class="skt-link">kāma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_skaama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_skaama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_skaamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣkāmā</i> désintéressé, sans désir — <a href="#entry-ni_skaama" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. état de suppression des désirs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_skaamakarman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niṣkāmakarman</i> [<a href="#entry-karman" class="skt-link">karman</a>] <a href="#entry-ni_skaamakarman" class="skt-link">n.</a> acte désintéressé.<br> <a href="#entry-ni_skaama" class="skt-link">niṣkāma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_skampa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्कम्प</span> <i>niṣkampa</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-kampa" class="skt-link">kampa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_skampa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_skampa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_skampaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣkampā</i> qui ne tremble pas; immobile; immuable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_skar_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्कर्ष</span> <i>niṣkarṣa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ni_sk_r_s" class="skt-link">niṣkṛṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-ni_skar_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> extraction <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> essence; extrait; résumé; point principal — <a href="#entry-ni_skar_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait d'accabler ses sujets de taxes.<br> <i>niṣkarṣāt</i> ind. principalement en ce qui concerne ⟨iic⟩.<br> <i>eṣa</i> <i>tava</i> <i>vacaso</i> <i>niṣkarṣaḥ</i> ceci est l'essence de ton discours.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_skar_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्कर्षण</span> <i>niṣkarṣaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ni_sk_r_s" class="skt-link">niṣkṛṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-ni_skar_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> extraction; soustraction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_skarman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्कर्मन्</span> <i>niṣkarman</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-karman" class="skt-link">karman</a>] <a href="#entry-ni_skarman" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. arrêt des actes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_skram">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्क्रम्</span> <i>niṣkram</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-kram" class="skt-link">kram</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(niṣkrāmati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niṣkrānta</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>niṣkramya</i><i>)</i> sortir, partir, s'en aller.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_skrii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्क्री</span> <i>niṣkrī</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-krii" class="skt-link">krī</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(niṣkrīṇāti)</i> pr. md. <i>(niṣkrīṇīte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niṣkrīta</i><i>)</i> racheter; se racheter; rembourser; rançonner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_skriya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्क्रिय</span> <i>niṣkriya</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-kriyaa" class="skt-link">kriyā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_skriya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_skriya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_skriyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣkriyā</i> inactif <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. qui néglige ses devoirs religieux, non pratiquant — <a href="#entry-ni_skriya" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. l'Être suprême [«absolument impassible»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_skriyataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niṣkriyatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-ni_skriyataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> inactivité — ifc. négligence de.<br> <a href="#entry-ni_skriya" class="skt-link">niṣkriya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sku_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्कुट</span> <i>niṣkuṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-ku_ta" class="skt-link">kuṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-ni_sku_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> jardin ou champ hors de la maison <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> harem.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_spaa_n_ditya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्पाण्डित्य</span> <i>niṣpāṇḍitya</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-paa_n_ditya" class="skt-link">pāṇḍitya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_spaa_n_ditya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_spaa_n_ditya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_spaa_n_dityaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣpāṇḍityā</i> ignorant; non éduqué.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_spad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्पद्</span> <i>niṣpad</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-pad_1" class="skt-link">pad_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. md. <i>(niṣpadyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niṣpanna</i><i>)</i> sortir, apparaître, se produire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> naître, provenir de ⟨abl.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_spatti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्पत्ति</span> <i>niṣpatti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ni_spad" class="skt-link">niṣpad</a>] <a href="#entry-ni_spatti" class="skt-link">f.</a> complétion; génération, production <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. dérivation (not. à partir d'une racine [<a href="#entry-dhaatu" class="skt-link">dhātu</a>]).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sphala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्फल</span> <i>niṣphala</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-phala" class="skt-link">phala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_sphala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_sphala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_sphalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣphalā</i> sans fruit, sans profit, stérile.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_spi_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्पिष्</span> <i>niṣpiṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-pi_s" class="skt-link">piṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(niṣpinaṣṭi)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niṣpiṣṭa</i><i>)</i> broyer, écraser; cogner; battre (le linge).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sprapa_nca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्प्रपञ्च</span> <i>niṣprapañca</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-prapa_nca" class="skt-link">prapañca</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_sprapa_nca" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_sprapa_nca" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_sprapa_ncaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣprapañcā</i> sans développement, non manifesté; fictif <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> de pure essence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>nippapañca</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_sprayojana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्प्रयोजन</span> <i>niṣprayojana</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-prayojana" class="skt-link">prayojana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_sprayojana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_sprayojana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_sprayojanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣprayojanā</i> sans motif; impartial, indifférent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> inoffensif <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sans objet, inutile, non nécessaire.<br> <i>niṣprayojanam</i> inutilement; sans nécessité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_suud">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषूद्</span> <i>niṣūd</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-suud" class="skt-link">sūd</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(niṣūdayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niṣūdita</i><i>)</i> tuer, détruire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_suuda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषूद</span> <i>niṣūda</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ni_suud" class="skt-link">niṣūd</a>] <a href="#entry-ni_suuda" class="skt-link">m.</a> destruction; meurtre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_suudaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषूदक</span> <i>niṣūdaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ni_suud" class="skt-link">niṣūd</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-ni_suudaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_suudaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_suudikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣūdikā</i> tueur de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_suudana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषूदन</span> <i>niṣūdana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ni_suud" class="skt-link">niṣūd</a>] ifc. <a href="#entry-ni_suudana" class="skt-link">m.</a> tueur de — <a href="#entry-ni_suudana" class="skt-link">n.</a> destruction de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_suudita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निषूदित</span> <i>niṣūdita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ni_suud" class="skt-link">niṣūd</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ni_suudita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_suudita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ni_suuditaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niṣūditā</i> tué, massacré; détruit.<br> <i>śūlāyāṃ</i> <i>niṣūdita</i> empalé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_syand">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्यन्द्</span> <i>niṣyand</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-syand" class="skt-link">syand</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(niṣyandate)</i> couler goutte à goutte; s'égoutter dans ⟨loc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ni_syanda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निष्यन्द</span> <i>niṣyanda</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ni_syand" class="skt-link">niṣyand</a>] <a href="#entry-ni_syanda" class="skt-link">m.</a> écoulement, suintement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nib_rh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निबृह्</span> <i>nibṛh</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-b_rh_2" class="skt-link">bṛh_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nibṛhati)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> renverser, détruire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nibandh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निबन्ध्</span> <i>nibandh</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-bandh" class="skt-link">bandh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(nibadhnāti)</i> pr. md. <i>(nibadhnīte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nibaddha</i><i>)</i> attacher, lier, fixer à ⟨loc.⟩; ligoter, enchaîner, entraver; resserrer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> arrêter, capturer, prendre; retenir, restreindre; obstruer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fixer par écrit, rédiger, composer — ca. <i>(nibandhayati)</i> faire attacher, faire capturer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nibandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निबन्ध</span> <i>nibandha</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-nibandh" class="skt-link">nibandh</a>] <a href="#entry-nibandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> lien, chaîne, entrave <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> origine, cause <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. attachement au monde des sens, esclavage de ses désirs <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> composition, œuvre littéraire [«feuillets liés ensemble»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. not. np. du Nibandha d'<a href="#entry-Uudayanaacaarya" class="skt-link">Udayanācārya</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Unyaayavaarttikataatparyaparizuddhi" class="skt-link">Nyāyavārttikatātparyapariśuddhi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nibandhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निबन्धन</span> <i>nibandhana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nibandh" class="skt-link">nibandh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nibandhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nibandhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nibandhanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nibandhanā</i> qui lie, qui attache; qui entrave — <a href="#entry-nibandhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> lien, chaîne, entrave <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> support, appui; cause, origine, fondement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> œuvre littéraire, traité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nibarha_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निबर्हण</span> <i>nibarhaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nib_rh" class="skt-link">nibṛh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nibarha_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nibarha_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nibarha_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nibarhaṇā</i> qui détruit, qui écrase — <a href="#entry-nibarha_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> destruction; extermination.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nibh_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निभृत</span> <i>nibhṛta</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-bh_rta" class="skt-link">bhṛta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nibh_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nibh_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nibh_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nibhṛtā</i> porté ou placé sous; caché; secret <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> modeste; silencieux, solitaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ferme, fixe; fixé, réglé, décidé; fermé (porte) — ifc. rempli de — <a href="#entry-nibh_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> humilité, modestie.<br> <i>nibhṛtam</i> adv. tranquillement, silencieusement; secrètement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nibha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निभ</span> <i>nibha</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-bha" class="skt-link">bha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nibha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nibha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nibhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nibhā</i> semblable à, similaire à ⟨iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-nibha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nibha" class="skt-link">n.</a> apparence; prétexte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nibudh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निबुध्</span> <i>nibudh</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-budh_1" class="skt-link">budh_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nibodhati)</i> pr. md. <i>(nibudhyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nibuddha</i><i>)</i> faire attention à ⟨acc.⟩, apprendre de.<br> <i>nibodha</i> imp. fais attention, écoute attentivement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nid_rz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निदृश्</span> <i>nidṛś</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-d_rz_1" class="skt-link">dṛś_1</a>] ca. <i>(nidarśayati)</i> faire voir, montrer, indiquer; conseiller, enseigner; démontrer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> annoncer, proclamer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nidaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निदा</span> <i>nidā</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-daa_2" class="skt-link">dā_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(nidyati)</i> lier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nidaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निदान</span> <i>nidāna</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-nidaa" class="skt-link">nidā</a>] <a href="#entry-nidaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> lien, corde <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cause, origine (not. d'une maladie); symptôme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méd. diagnostic; thérapeutique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. lien entre microcosme et macrocosme, <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a> efficace; cf. <a href="#entry-bandhu" class="skt-link">bandhu</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. un des 12 facteurs de la chaîne des causalités ou occasions de production de l'existence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. introduction; exposé liminaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nidaanasthaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nidānasthāna</i> [<a href="#entry-sthaana" class="skt-link">sthāna</a>] <a href="#entry-nidaanasthaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> méd. étude du diagnostic; pathologie.<br> <a href="#entry-nidaana" class="skt-link">nidāna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nidarzana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निदर्शन</span> <i>nidarśana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nid_rz" class="skt-link">nidṛś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nidarzana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nidarzana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nidarzanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nidarśanā</i> qui indique, qui enseigne — <a href="#entry-nidarzana" class="skt-link">n.</a> aspect, apparence; vision; preuve, évidence; exemple; signe, indication, indice, symptôme — <a href="#entry-nidarzanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nidarśanā</i> lit. rhét. figure de style [<a href="#entry-alafkaara" class="skt-link">alaṃkāra</a>] exhibant un objet et sa réflexion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nideza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निदेश</span> <i>nideśa</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-nidiz" class="skt-link">nidiś</a>] <a href="#entry-nideza" class="skt-link">m.</a> ordre, commande; instruction, directive <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> voisinage.<br> <i>nideśaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> var. <i>nideśaṃvṛt</i> var. <i>nideśaṃsthā</i> exécuter les ordres, obéir.<br> <i>nideśe</i> adv. à proximité, près de ⟨g.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nidezatas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nideśatas</i> [<a href="#entry--tas" class="skt-link">-tas</a>] adv. sur l'ordre de, selon les instructions de ⟨iic. g.⟩.<br> <a href="#entry-nideza" class="skt-link">nideśa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nidhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निधा</span> <i>nidhā</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-dhaa_1" class="skt-link">dhā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. <i>(nidadhāti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nihita</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>nidhāya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pra</i>, <i>prati</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> déposer, poser, placer; placer dans ⟨loc.⟩; réserver, cacher <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> offrir, confier à ⟨dat. loc.⟩ — ps. <i>(nidhīyate)</i> être contenu ou absorbé dans ⟨loc.⟩.<br> <i>ātmānaṃ</i> <i>nidhā</i> se confier à ⟨loc.⟩.<br> <i>kriyāṃ</i> <i>nidhā</i> se donner de la peine pour ⟨loc.⟩.<br> <i>dṛśaṃ</i> <i>nidhā</i> fixer les yeux sur ⟨loc.⟩.<br> <i>bhūmau</i> <i>nidhā</i> enterrer.<br> <i>maulau</i> <i>nidhā</i> placer sur la tête; recevoir avec respect.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nidhaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निधान</span> <i>nidhāna</i> [<a href="#entry-nidhaa" class="skt-link">nidhā</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-nidhaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> réceptacle, dépôt; réserve, trésor.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nidhaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निधाय</span> <i>nidhāya</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-nidhaa" class="skt-link">nidhā</a>] ind. ayant déposé.<br> <i>nidhāya</i> <i>manasi</i> ayant à l'esprit, réfléchissant à ⟨acc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nidhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निधन</span> <i>nidhana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nidhaa" class="skt-link">nidhā</a>] <a href="#entry-nidhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> réceptacle; conclusion, fin; finale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> destruction, mort; trépas <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-rudra" class="skt-link">rudra</a> Nidhana ou Nidhaneṣa «le Seigneur qui détruit», aspect de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> qui délivre l'âme en détruisant la cause et l'effet du <a href="#entry-sa_msaara" class="skt-link">saṃsāra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>oṅṅendo</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nidhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निधि</span> <i>nidhi</i> [<a href="#entry-nidhaa" class="skt-link">nidhā</a>] <a href="#entry-nidhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> distribution de nourriture <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> réceptacle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> trésor <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. l'un des 9 trésors de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-navanidhi" class="skt-link">navanidhi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nidhyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निध्या</span> <i>nidhyā</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-dhyaa" class="skt-link">dhyā</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nidhyāyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nidhyāta</i><i>)</i> observer, percevoir; contempler; méditer — dés. md. <i>(nididhyāsate)</i> méditer avec attention.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nididhyaas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निदिध्यास्</span> <i>nididhyās</i> [dés. <a href="#entry-nidhyaa" class="skt-link">nidhyā</a>] v. pr. md. <i>(nididhyāsate)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>nididhyāsitavya</i><i>)</i> désirer méditer; méditer avec attention.<br> <i>ātmā</i> <i>vā</i> <i>are</i> <i>draṣṭavyaḥ</i> <i>śrotavyo</i> <i>mantavyo</i> <i>nididhyāsitavyaḥ</i> [BU.] En vérité, fidèle, c'est le Soi, qu'il faut regarder, qu'il faut écouter, qu'il faut contempler, sur lequel on doit désirer méditer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nididhyaasana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निदिध्यासन</span> <i>nididhyāsana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nididhyaas" class="skt-link">nididhyās</a>] <a href="#entry-nididhyaasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] technique de méditation intense.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nidiz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निदिश्</span> <i>nidiś</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-diz_1" class="skt-link">diś_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(nidiśati)</i> ordonner, donner des instructions.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nidraa_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निद्रा</span> <i>nidrā_1</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-draa_2" class="skt-link">drā_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(nidrāyate)</i> s'endormir, dormir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nidraa_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निद्रा</span> <i>nidrā_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nidraa_1" class="skt-link">nidrā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-nidraa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> sommeil, somnolence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] la perte d'attention, un <a href="#entry-cittav_rtti" class="skt-link">cittavṛtti</a>.<br> <i>nidrāṃ</i> <i>gam</i> s'endormir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nidraamagna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nidrāmagna</i> [<a href="#entry-magna" class="skt-link">magna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nidraamagna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nidraamagna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nidraamagnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nidrāmagnā</i> profondément endormi.<br> <a href="#entry-nidraa_2" class="skt-link">nidrā_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nig_rr">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निगॄ</span> <i>nigṝ</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-g_rr_1" class="skt-link">gṝ_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(nigirati)</i> avaler, ingurgiter, engloutir — ca. <i>(nigārayati)</i> ca. <i>(nigālayati)</i> faire avaler — int. md. <i>(nijegilyate)</i> se goinfrer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nigam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निगम्</span> <i>nigam</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-gam" class="skt-link">gam</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nigacchati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nigata</i><i>)</i> aller auprès de, aller vers ⟨acc. loc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> parvenir à tel état — ca. <i>(nigamayati)</i> conclure, déduire; dériver.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nigama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निगम</span> <i>nigama</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nigam" class="skt-link">nigam</a>] <a href="#entry-nigama" class="skt-link">m.</a> tradition, doctrine; savoir; certitude <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> texte religieux, not. véd.; tradition sacrée; paroles vénérées <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. <a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a> shivaïte (sous la forme d'un dialogue où <a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a> répond à des questions de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>); opp. <a href="#entry-aagama" class="skt-link">āgama</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. racine; ifc. dérivé de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> accès, route; commerce, marché; caravane de marchands.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nigamaj_naana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nigamajñāna</i> [<a href="#entry-j_naana" class="skt-link">jñāna</a>] <a href="#entry-nigamaj_naana" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Nigamajñāna_1, philosophe du 16⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, qui propagea la doctrine <a href="#entry-zaivasiddhaanta" class="skt-link">śaivasiddhānta</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Nigamajñāna_2, neveu et disciple du précédent.<br> <a href="#entry-nigama" class="skt-link">nigama</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nigamana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निगमन</span> <i>nigamana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nigam" class="skt-link">nigam</a>] <a href="#entry-nigamana" class="skt-link">n.</a> déduction; conclusion logique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] la conclusion du syllogisme [<a href="#entry-pa_ncaavayava" class="skt-link">pañcāvayava</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nigh_r_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निघृष्</span> <i>nighṛṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-gh_r_s" class="skt-link">ghṛṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nigharṣati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nighṛṣṭa</i><i>)</i> effacer en frottant; moudre, broyer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nigh_r_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निघृष्ट</span> <i>nighṛṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nigh_r_s" class="skt-link">nighṛṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nigh_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nigh_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nigh_r_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nighṛṣṭā</i> effacé; usé; broyé; soumis.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nigha_n_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निघण्ट</span> <i>nighaṇṭa</i> <a href="#entry-nigha_n_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. lexique; vocabulaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nigha_n_tu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निघण्टु</span> <i>nighaṇṭu</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-nigha_n_ta" class="skt-link">nighaṇṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-nigha_n_tu" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. glossaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. du Nighaṇṭu, glossaire de mots védiques dû à <a href="#entry-Uyaaska" class="skt-link">Yāska</a>; il ajouta les gloses à un lexique de synonymes védiques préexistant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>nighaṇṭavaḥ</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nigha_n_turatnaakara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nighaṇṭuratnākara</i> [<a href="#entry-ratnaakara" class="skt-link">ratnākara</a>] <a href="#entry-nigha_n_turatnaakara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Nighaṇṭuratnākara «Mine lexicale», ouvrage médical [<a href="#entry-aayurveda" class="skt-link">āyurveda</a>] tardif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nigha_n_tuze_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nighaṇṭuśeṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-ze_sa" class="skt-link">śeṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-nigha_n_tuze_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Nighaṇṭuśeṣa «Supplément au <a href="#entry-Unigha_n_tu" class="skt-link">Nighaṇṭu</a>», dictionnaire de termes botaniques dû à <a href="#entry-Uhemacandra" class="skt-link">Hemacandra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-nigha_n_tu" class="skt-link">nighaṇṭu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nighar_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निघर्ष</span> <i>nigharṣa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nigh_r_s" class="skt-link">nighṛṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-nighar_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> effacement, grattage; broyage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nighna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निघ्न</span> <i>nighna</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nihan" class="skt-link">nihan</a>] <a href="#entry-nighna" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du roi Nighna «Tueur», père de <a href="#entry-Usatraajit" class="skt-link">Satrājit</a> et <a href="#entry-Uprasena" class="skt-link">Prasena</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nigrah">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निग्रह्</span> <i>nigrah</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-grah" class="skt-link">grah</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(nigṛhṇāti)</i> pr. md. <i>(nigṛhṇīte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nigṛhīta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> retenir, restreindre; refréner, réprimer, contraindre; arrêter.<br> <i>krodhaṃ</i> <i>nigṛhāṇa</i> réprime ta colère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nigraha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निग्रह</span> <i>nigraha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nigrah" class="skt-link">nigrah</a>] <a href="#entry-nigraha" class="skt-link">m.</a> restriction, répression, punition; opp. <a href="#entry-anugraha" class="skt-link">anugraha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> coercion, contrainte; subjugation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] possession par une <a href="#entry-yoginii" class="skt-link">yoginī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> défaite, déroute, destruction <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> arrêt, capture, immobilisation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méd. guérison d'une maladie, cure <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> confinement, emprisonnement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> réprimande, blâme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aversion, dégoût <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] faute de raisonnement; réfutation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> poignée; dispositif de préhension — <a href="#entry-nigrahaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nigrahā</i> ifc. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nigraha_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निग्रहण</span> <i>nigrahaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nigraha" class="skt-link">nigraha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nigraha_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nigraha_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nigraha_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nigrahaṇā</i> ifc. qui retient, qui supprime — <a href="#entry-nigraha_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> contrainte; rétention, répression, suppression <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> capture; punition, emprisonnement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nigrahasthaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nigrahasthāna</i> [<a href="#entry-sthaana" class="skt-link">sthāna</a>] <a href="#entry-nigrahasthaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] rhét. impossibilité d'argumentation par non accord sur les principes de base; pierre d'achoppement; situation d'échec d'un opposant dans un débat.<br> <a href="#entry-nigraha" class="skt-link">nigraha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niguh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निगुह्</span> <i>niguh</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-guh" class="skt-link">guh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nigūhati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nigūḍha</i><i>)</i> couvrir, cacher, dissimuler — ca. <i>(nigūhayati)</i> cacher, dissimuler; mettre au secret.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niguu_dha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निगूढ</span> <i>nigūḍha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-niguh" class="skt-link">niguh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niguu_dha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niguu_dha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niguu_dhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nigūḍhā</i> caché, dissimulé; secret <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> obscur, ésotérique.<br> <i>nigūḍha</i> en secret.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niguu_dhaartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nigūḍhārtha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niguu_dhaartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niguu_dhaartha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niguu_dhaarthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nigūḍhārthā</i> au sens caché ou mystérieux; occulte <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. se dit des textes ésotériques [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] dont on fait une lecture allégorique.<br> <a href="#entry-niguu_dha" class="skt-link">nigūḍha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nihaakaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निहाका</span> <i>nihākā</i> <a href="#entry-nihaakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> tempête, tornade <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. alligator du Gange.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nihan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निहन्</span> <i>nihan</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-han_1" class="skt-link">han_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(nihanti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nihata</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>nihatya</i><i>)</i> frapper, abattre; tuer, détruire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nihata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निहत</span> <i>nihata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nihan" class="skt-link">nihan</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nihata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nihata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nihataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nihatā</i> lancé; touché <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> blessé, abattu; tué, annihilé; disparu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phon. sans accent; syn. <a href="#entry-anudaatta" class="skt-link">anudātta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nihataari">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nihatāri</i> [<a href="#entry-ari" class="skt-link">ari</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nihataari" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nihataari" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nihataari" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui supprime ses ennemis.<br> <a href="#entry-nihata" class="skt-link">nihata</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nihatya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निहत्य</span> <i>nihatya</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-nihan" class="skt-link">nihan</a>] ind. ayant tué, ayant abattu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nihita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निहित</span> <i>nihita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nidhaa" class="skt-link">nidhā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nihita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nihita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nihitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nihitā</i> placé, déposé, fixé; donné, confié.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nii_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नीड</span> <i>nīḍa</i> <a href="#entry-nii_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nii_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> abri; nid <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fr. nid.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nii_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">नी</span> <i>nī_1</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-nii_3" class="skt-link">nī_3</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nayati)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>neṣyati</i><i>)</i> pft. <i>(nināya)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] <i>(anaiṣīt)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nīta</i><i>)</i> ppr. <i>(nayat)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>neya</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>nītvā</i>, <i>-nīya</i><i>)</i> inf. <i>(netum)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>anu</i>, <i>apa</i>, <i>abhi</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>ut</i>, <i>upa</i>, <i>nis</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>prati</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> mener, conduire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> amener qqn. ⟨acc.⟩ (à ⟨acc.⟩) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> porter, apporter, emporter, emmener <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mener à son terme, accomplir; passer (le temps) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ⟨acc. nom abstrait⟩ faire (sens causal) — ps. <i>(nīyate)</i> être conduit — ca. <i>(nāyayati)</i> ps. <i>(nāyyate)</i> faire mener, faire conduire — dés. <i>(ninīṣati)</i> dés. md. <i>(ninīṣate)</i> désirer mener — int. md. <i>(nenīyate)</i> amener de force.<br> <i>kriyāṃ</i> <i>nī</i> accomplir un rite.<br> <i>daṇḍaṃ</i> <i>nī</i> châtier, exercer l'autorité.<br> <i>duḥkhaṃ</i> <i>nī</i> faire de la peine.<br> <i>paritoṣaṃ</i> <i>nī</i> satisfaire, contenter.<br> <i>prasādaṃ</i> <i>nī</i> rendre favorable.<br> <i>bhasmasān</i> <i>nī</i> réduire en cendres.<br> <i>vaśaṃ</i> <i>nī</i> soumettre.<br> <i>śamaṃ</i> <i>nī</i> apaiser.<br> <i>samatāṃ</i> <i>nī</i> partager équitablement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nii_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नी</span> <i>nī_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nii_1" class="skt-link">nī_1</a>] ifc. <a href="#entry-nii_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nii_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui guide, qui conduit, qui dirige.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nii_3">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नी</span> <i>nī_3</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-i" class="skt-link">i</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(nyeti)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>nyāyya</i><i>)</i> entrer dans.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nii_4">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नी</span> <i>nī_4</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niica">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नीच</span> <i>nīca</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-ac" class="skt-link">ac</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niica" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niica" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niicaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nīcā</i> bas, insignifiant; vil, mesquin, méchant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> court (cheveux, ongles).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niicakula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nīcakula</i> [<a href="#entry-kula" class="skt-link">kula</a>] <a href="#entry-niicakula" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. race inférieure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niicataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nīcatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-niicataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> infériorité; condition de serviteur.<br> <i>nīcatayā</i> <i>yā</i> s'accroupir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niicatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nīcatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-niicatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> infériorité; condition de serviteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niicavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nīcavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niicavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niicavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niicavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nīcavatī</i> à la manière d'un esclave.<br> <a href="#entry-niica" class="skt-link">nīca</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niihaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नीहार</span> <i>nīhāra</i> [<a href="#entry-nii_4" class="skt-link">nī_4</a>-<a href="#entry-haara_1" class="skt-link">hāra_1</a>] <a href="#entry-niihaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> brouillard — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(nīhārāyate)</i> faire du brouillard.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नील</span> <i>nīla</i> a. <a href="#entry-niila" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niila" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nīlā</i> <a href="#entry-niilii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nīlī</i> bleu; de couleur foncée; teint d'indigo; bleu nuit, violet ou noir — <a href="#entry-niila" class="skt-link">m.</a> saphir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du démon-éléphant [<a href="#entry-gajaasura" class="skt-link">gajāsura</a>] Nīla «le Noir»; il attaqua <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> et fut détruit par <a href="#entry-Uga_neza" class="skt-link">Gaṇeśa</a> — <a href="#entry-niilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nīlā</i> natu. bot. <i>Indigofera tinctoria</i>, indigo — <a href="#entry-niilii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nīlī</i> id. — <a href="#entry-niila" class="skt-link">n.</a> <i>nīla</i> couleur sombre; obscurité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> substance sombre; indigo, etc. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fr. lilas.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niilaacala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nīlācala</i> [<a href="#entry-acala" class="skt-link">acala</a>] <a href="#entry-niilaacala" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de Nīlācala «Mont Bleu», ancien nom d'une colline près du bord de mer en Orissa, sur laquelle a été édifié le temple de <a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha" class="skt-link">Jagannātha</a>; mod. <a href="#entry-Upurii" class="skt-link">Purī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niilaacalakha_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nīlācalakhaṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-kha_n_da" class="skt-link">khaṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-niilaacalakha_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niilaacalakha_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Nīlācalakhaṇḍa, l'un des suppléments [<a href="#entry-parizi_s_ta" class="skt-link">pariśiṣṭa</a>] du <a href="#entry-Uskandapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Skandapurāṇa</a>, traitant de <a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha" class="skt-link">Jagannātha</a>-<a href="#entry-Upurii" class="skt-link">Purī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-niilaacala" class="skt-link">nīlācala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niilaa_njana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nīlāñjana</i> [<a href="#entry-a_njana" class="skt-link">añjana</a>] <a href="#entry-niilaa_njana" class="skt-link">n.</a> onguent noir — <a href="#entry-niilaa_njanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nīlāñjanā</i> var. <i>nīlājanā</i> géo. np. de la rivière Nīlāñjanā, qui rejoint la <a href="#entry-Umohanaa" class="skt-link">Mohanā</a> pour former la <a href="#entry-Uphalgu" class="skt-link">Phalgu</a> près de <a href="#entry-Ugayaa" class="skt-link">Gayā</a> au <a href="#entry-Ubihaara" class="skt-link">Bihāra</a>; [<a href="#entry-Ubuddhacarita" class="skt-link">Buddhacarita</a>] c'est sur ses rives que le futur <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> pratiquait ses austérités avec cinq compagnons ascètes avant de les quitter et de renoncer à l'ascétisme; il accepta un repas de <a href="#entry-Usujaataa" class="skt-link">Sujātā</a>, et alla méditer sous l'arbre où il connut l'Éveil [<a href="#entry-bodhitaru" class="skt-link">bodhitaru</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>neranjanā</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niilaa_njanagiri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nīlāñjanagiri</i> [<a href="#entry-giri" class="skt-link">giri</a>] <a href="#entry-niilaa_njanagiri" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Nīlāñjanagiri, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uzani" class="skt-link">Śani</a> «Pareil à une montagne d'onguent noir».<br> <a href="#entry-niilaa_njana" class="skt-link">nīlāñjana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niilagiri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nīlagiri</i> [<a href="#entry-giri" class="skt-link">giri</a>] <a href="#entry-niilagiri" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de la colline de Nīlagiri, au <a href="#entry-Upuru_sottamak_setra" class="skt-link">Puruṣottamakṣetra</a>, consacrée à <a href="#entry-Uniilamaadhava" class="skt-link">Nīlamādhava</a>; en son centre s'étendait un immense arbre d'abondance [<a href="#entry-kalpav_rk_sa" class="skt-link">kalpavṛkṣa</a>]; la fontaine de libération <a href="#entry-Urohi_niiku_n_da" class="skt-link">Rohiṇīkuṇḍa</a> y était située; le roi <a href="#entry-Uindradyumna" class="skt-link">Indradyumna</a> y fonda la ville de <a href="#entry-Upurii" class="skt-link">Purī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. des Monts Bleus Nīlagiri_1, au sud des Ghats occidentaux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niilaka_n_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nīlakaṇṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_n_tha" class="skt-link">kaṇṭha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niilaka_n_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niilaka_n_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niilaka_n_thii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nīlakaṇṭhī</i> à la gorge bleue — <a href="#entry-niilaka_n_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> paon; geai bleu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Nīlakaṇṭha «Gorge Bleue», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>, dont la gorge a bleui après qu'il ait bu le poison [<a href="#entry-halaahala" class="skt-link">halāhala</a>] issu du barattage de la mer de lait [<a href="#entry-k_siirodamathana" class="skt-link">kṣīrodamathana</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Nīlakaṇṭha_1, nom de famille de <a href="#entry-Ubhavabhuuti" class="skt-link">Bhavabhūti</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du lettré Nīlakaṇṭha_2 <a href="#entry-Udiik_sita" class="skt-link">Dīkṣita</a> (17⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle), auteur de la <a href="#entry-Uparibhaa_saav_rtti" class="skt-link">Paribhāṣāvṛtti</a>, du <a href="#entry-Ukalivi_dambana" class="skt-link">Kaliviḍambana</a> et de l'<a href="#entry-Uanyaapadezazataka" class="skt-link">Anyāpadeśaśataka</a>; il vivait à Madurai <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du mathématicien et astronome Nīlakaṇṭha_3 <a href="#entry-Usomayaajii" class="skt-link">Somayājī</a>, auteur du <a href="#entry-Utantrasafgraha" class="skt-link">Tantrasaṃgraha</a> (1445–1545) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du légiste Nīlakaṇṭha_4 <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a> (17⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle), petit-fils de <a href="#entry-Unaaraaya_na_3" class="skt-link">Nārāyaṇa_3</a> <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a>, auteur du <a href="#entry-Uvyavahaaramayuukha" class="skt-link">Vyavahāramayūkha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Nīlakaṇṭha_5, auteur de la <a href="#entry-Umaatafgaliilaa" class="skt-link">Mātaṃgalīlā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niilamaadhava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nīlamādhava</i> [<a href="#entry-maadhava" class="skt-link">mādhava</a>] <a href="#entry-niilamaadhava" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Nīlamādhava «le bleu descendant de <a href="#entry-Umadhu" class="skt-link">Madhu</a>», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niilamata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nīlamata</i> [<a href="#entry-mata" class="skt-link">mata</a>] <a href="#entry-niilamata" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Nīlamata <a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>; antérieur à <a href="#entry-Ukalha_na" class="skt-link">Kalhaṇa</a>, il décrit l'histoire ancienne du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> et les légendes fondant sa culture.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niilasukhaadi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nīlasukhādi</i> [<a href="#entry-sukha" class="skt-link">sukha</a>-<a href="#entry-aadi" class="skt-link">ādi</a>] <a href="#entry-niilasukhaadi" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [tantr.] catégorie des expériences ressenties, comprenant les perceptions ou expériences externes (comme la perception du bleu) et les sensations ou expériences internes (comme le plaisir).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niilotpala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nīlotpala</i> [<a href="#entry-utpala" class="skt-link">utpala</a>] <a href="#entry-niilotpala" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. bot. <i>Nymphæa cærulea</i>, lotus bleu (qui s'ouvre au lever de la lune).<br> <a href="#entry-niila" class="skt-link">nīla</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niima">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नीम</span> <i>nīma</i> <a href="#entry-niima" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-nimba" class="skt-link">nimba</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niimaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nīmāya</i> [<a href="#entry-aya" class="skt-link">aya</a>] <a href="#entry-niimaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Nīmāya, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a> «né sous un margousier».<br> <a href="#entry-niima" class="skt-link">nīma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niipa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नीप</span> <i>nīpa</i> a. <a href="#entry-niipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niipa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niipaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nīpā</i> situé en bas — <a href="#entry-niipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> région au pied de la montagne, piémont.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नीर</span> <i>nīra</i> <a href="#entry-niira" class="skt-link">n.</a> eau — <a href="#entry-niiraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nīrā</i> géo. np. de la rivière Nīrā au <a href="#entry-Umahaaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">Mahārāṣṭra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niiraaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नीराग</span> <i>nīrāga</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-raaga" class="skt-link">rāga</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niiraaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niiraaga" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niiraagaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nīrāgā</i> qui est sans passion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niiraaj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नीराज्</span> <i>nīrāj</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-raaj_1" class="skt-link">rāj_1</a>] ca. <i>(nīrājayati)</i> illuminer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. effectuer le rite <a href="#entry-niiraajana" class="skt-link">nīrājana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niiraajana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नीराजन</span> <i>nīrājana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-niiraaj" class="skt-link">nīrāj</a>] <a href="#entry-niiraajana" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. cérémonie de lustration des armes par un roi; consécration d'un cheval <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. rite d'adoration d'une divinité par illumination avec une lampe de camphre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niirak_siiraviveka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nīrakṣīraviveka</i> [<a href="#entry-k_siira" class="skt-link">kṣīra</a>-<a href="#entry-viveka" class="skt-link">viveka</a>] <a href="#entry-niirak_siiraviveka" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. pouvoir de séparer le lait de l'eau (c'est-à-dire de discerner la vérité du mensonge); il est incarné par le <a href="#entry-ha_msa" class="skt-link">haṃsa</a>.<br> <i>nīrakṣīravivekam</i> <i>ca</i> <i>haṃso</i> <i>vetti</i> <i>na</i> <i>ca</i> <i>aparaḥ</i> [GP.] Seul le cygne sacré sait séparer le lait de l'eau.<br> <a href="#entry-niira" class="skt-link">nīra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niirasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नीरस</span> <i>nīrasa</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niirasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niirasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niirasaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nīrasā</i> insipide.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niiruj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नीरुज्</span> <i>nīruj</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-ruj_2" class="skt-link">ruj_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niiruj" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niiruj" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niiruj" class="skt-link">f.</a> bien-portant, en bonne santé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नीत</span> <i>nīta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nii_1" class="skt-link">nī_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nītā</i> conduit, amené; emmené, emporté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niiti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नीति</span> <i>nīti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nii_1" class="skt-link">nī_1</a>] <a href="#entry-niiti" class="skt-link">f.</a> guidage, conduite; éducation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sagesse, bon sens <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> comportement; disposition naturelle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> usage, coutume; méthode, manière <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> niveau de langue, manière de s'exprimer; style de diction ou d'écriture <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> éthique, politique, morale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> laiton; bronze de cloche <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> oxydation d'un métal; rouille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niitij_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nītijña</i> [<a href="#entry-j_na" class="skt-link">jña</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niitij_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niitij_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niitij_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nītijñā</i> bien élevé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niitimat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nītimat</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niitimat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niitimat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niitimatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nītimatī</i> sage, prudent; avisé, sagace.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niitisaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nītisāra</i> [<a href="#entry-saara" class="skt-link">sāra</a>] <a href="#entry-niitisaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niitisaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du <a href="#entry-Ukaamandakiiya" class="skt-link">Kāmandakīya</a> Nītisāra «Essence de la morale», ouvrage de <a href="#entry-niitizaastra" class="skt-link">nītiśāstra</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Ukaamandaki" class="skt-link">Kāmandaki</a>, exposant les doctrines de l'<a href="#entry-Uarthazaastra" class="skt-link">Arthaśāstra</a> de son maître <a href="#entry-Ucaa_nakya" class="skt-link">Cāṇakya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-niiti" class="skt-link">nīti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niitivaakya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nītivākya</i> [<a href="#entry-vaakya" class="skt-link">vākya</a>] <a href="#entry-niitivaakya" class="skt-link">n.</a> pl. paroles de sagesse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niitivaakyaam_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nītivākyāmṛta</i> [<a href="#entry-am_rta" class="skt-link">amṛta</a>] <a href="#entry-niitivaakyaam_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Nītivākyāmṛta, ouvrage de morale politique de <a href="#entry-Usomadeva_1" class="skt-link">Somadeva_1</a> <a href="#entry-Usuuri" class="skt-link">Sūri</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-niitivaakya" class="skt-link">nītivākya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niitivarman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nītivarman</i> [<a href="#entry-varman" class="skt-link">varman</a>] <a href="#entry-niitivarman" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du poète Nītivarmā; il vivait à l'Est de l'Inde, vers le 7⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il est l'auteur du <a href="#entry-Ukiicakavadha" class="skt-link">Kīcakavadha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niitividyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nītividyā</i> [<a href="#entry-vidyaa" class="skt-link">vidyā</a>] <a href="#entry-niitividyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> savoir-vivre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niitizaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nītiśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-niitizaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. traité de politique, de savoir-vivre ou de morale; cf. <a href="#entry-Upa_ncatantra" class="skt-link">Pañcatantra</a>, <a href="#entry-Uhitopadeza" class="skt-link">Hitopadeśa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niitizataka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nītiśataka</i> [<a href="#entry-zataka" class="skt-link">śataka</a>] <a href="#entry-niitizataka" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Nītiśataka, recueil de 100 poèmes sur la morale de <a href="#entry-Ubhart_rhari_1" class="skt-link">Bhartṛhari_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niivaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नीवार</span> <i>nīvāra</i> [<a href="#entry-nii_4" class="skt-link">nī_4</a>-<a href="#entry-vaara" class="skt-link">vāra</a>] <a href="#entry-niivaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> bot. <i>Hygroryza aristata</i>, riz sauvage; graminée aquatique flottante, médicinale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>nīvārās</i> grains de riz sauvage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नीय</span> <i>nīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-nii_1" class="skt-link">nī_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nīyā</i> à conduire, à emmener, etc.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nij">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">निज्</span> <i>nij</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. md. <i>(niṅkte)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. <i>(nenekti)</i> pr. md. <i>(nenikte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nikta</i><i>)</i> laver, nettoyer; se laver, se purifier — ps. <i>(nijyate)</i> être nettoyé — ca. <i>(nejayati)</i> faire nettoyer — int. <i>(nenijīti)</i> nettoyer à fond — dés. <i>(nenikṣati)</i> vouloir nettoyer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nija">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निज</span> <i>nija</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-ja" class="skt-link">ja</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nija" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nija" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nijaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nijā</i> inné; sien, propre; natif <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> du même pays, concitoyen; du même parti; parent.<br> <i>nijoripuḥ</i> traître dans son propre camp.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nik_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निकृ</span> <i>nikṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-k_r_1" class="skt-link">kṛ_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">8</span>] pr. <i>(nikaroti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nikṛta</i><i>)</i> abaisser, humilier; dominer, vaincre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nik_r_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निकृष्</span> <i>nikṛṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-k_r_s" class="skt-link">kṛṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(nikṛṣati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nikṛṣṭa</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> tirer vers le bas, retirer — ps. <i>(nikṛṣyate)</i> être emporté par le courant d'une rivière.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nik_r_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निकृष्ट</span> <i>nikṛṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nik_r_s" class="skt-link">nikṛṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nik_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nik_r_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nik_r_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nikṛṣṭā</i> abaissé, dégradé; avili, méprisé; hors-caste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nik_rt">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निकृत्</span> <i>nikṛt</i> var. <i>niṣkṛt</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-k_rt_1" class="skt-link">kṛt_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(nikṛntati)</i> pr. md. <i>(nikṛntate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nikṛtta</i><i>)</i> tailler, trancher, retrancher, découper, diviser.<br> <i>nakhāni</i> <i>nikṛntate</i> il se coupe les ongles.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nik_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निकृत</span> <i>nikṛta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nik_r" class="skt-link">nikṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nik_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nik_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nik_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nikṛtā</i> humilié, offensé, insulté; trompé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bas, vil, méchant — <a href="#entry-nik_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> humiliation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nik_rti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निकृति</span> <i>nikṛti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nik_r" class="skt-link">nikṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nik_rti" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nik_rti" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nik_rti" class="skt-link">f.</a> malhonnête, déloyal — <a href="#entry-nik_rti" class="skt-link">f.</a> bassesse, vilenie, déloyauté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Nikṛti «Tromperie», fille d'<a href="#entry-Uadharma" class="skt-link">Adharma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nik_rtta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निकृत्त</span> <i>nikṛtta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nik_rt" class="skt-link">nikṛt</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nik_rtta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nik_rtta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nik_rttaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nikṛttā</i> tranché, retranché, taillé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nik_sepa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निक्षेप</span> <i>nikṣepa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nik_sip" class="skt-link">nikṣip</a>] <a href="#entry-nik_sepa" class="skt-link">m.</a> dépôt; consignation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> abandon.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nik_sepavaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nikṣepavāda</i> [<a href="#entry-vaada" class="skt-link">vāda</a>] <a href="#entry-nik_sepavaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. doctrine du renoncement à l'unicité de la réalité; syn. <a href="#entry-nyaasavaada" class="skt-link">nyāsavāda</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-nik_sepa" class="skt-link">nikṣepa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nik_sip">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निक्षिप्</span> <i>nikṣip</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-k_sip" class="skt-link">kṣip</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(nikṣipati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nikṣipta</i><i>)</i> jeter, renverser; écarter, abandonner, répudier; jeter dans, verser dans <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> confier; stipuler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nika_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निकष्</span> <i>nikaṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-ka_s" class="skt-link">kaṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nikaṣati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nikaṣita</i><i>)</i> frotter, gratter — pr. md. <i>(nikaṣate)</i> se frotter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nika_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निकष</span> <i>nikaṣa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nika_s" class="skt-link">nikaṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-nika_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de frotter, d'oindre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pierre de touche — <a href="#entry-nika_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> trace de l'or sur la pierre de touche — <a href="#entry-nika_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nikaṣā</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de la <a href="#entry-raak_sasii" class="skt-link">rākṣasī</a> Nikaṣā, mère de <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukaikasii" class="skt-link">Kaikasī</a> — adv. près de ⟨acc.⟩; au milieu, entre — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(nikaṣāyate)</i> servir de pierre de touche; servir d'épreuve.<br> <i>nikaṣāyamāṇa</i> a. m. n. (pprm.) qui établit le standard, qui définit la norme — ifc. qui sert de test à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nika_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निकषण</span> <i>nikaṣaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nika_s" class="skt-link">nikaṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-nika_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> effaçage, polissage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nika_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निकट</span> <i>nikaṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-ka_ta" class="skt-link">kaṭa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nika_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nika_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nika_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nikaṭā</i> proche, près de — <a href="#entry-nika_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nika_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> proximité.<br> <i>nikaṭe</i> adv. auprès de.<br> <i>nikaṭāt</i> adv. loin de.<br> <i>nikaṭībhū</i> se rapprocher.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nikaama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निकाम</span> <i>nikāma</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nikam" class="skt-link">nikam</a>] <a href="#entry-nikaama" class="skt-link">m.</a> désir; convoitise.<br> <i>nikāmam</i> adv. selon son plaisir, à son bon vouloir; à volonté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> excessivement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nikaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निकाय</span> <i>nikāya</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-kaaya" class="skt-link">kāya</a>] <a href="#entry-nikaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> tas, amas, accumulation; groupe, association, confrérie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. congrégation, école <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. lit. les 5 listes de préceptes [<a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>] (canon bd. <a href="#entry-paali" class="skt-link">pāli</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> maison, résidence; bûcher funèbre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nikam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निकम्</span> <i>nikam</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-kam" class="skt-link">kam</a>] pft. <i>(nicakame)</i> désirer, convoiter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nikar_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निकर्ष</span> <i>nikarṣa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nik_r_s" class="skt-link">nikṛṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-nikar_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> abaissement; réduction; décroissance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nikar_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निकर्षण</span> <i>nikarṣaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nik_r_s" class="skt-link">nikṛṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-nikar_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> alentours d'un village; cour à l'entrée d'une habitation; voisinage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nikara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निकर</span> <i>nikara</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-kara_2" class="skt-link">kara_2</a>] <a href="#entry-nikara" class="skt-link">m.</a> tas; pile; collection; grande quantité; multitude <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> honoraires, cadeau conséquent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> trésor; not. trésor de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>nikāra</i> fait d'entasser ou de vanner du grain.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niketa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निकेत</span> <i>niketa</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-keta" class="skt-link">keta</a>] <a href="#entry-niketa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niketa" class="skt-link">n.</a> habitation, maison.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nikhan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निखन्</span> <i>nikhan</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-khan" class="skt-link">khan</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nikhanati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nikhāta</i><i>)</i> enfouir, enfoncer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nikharva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निखर्व</span> <i>nikharva</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-kharva" class="skt-link">kharva</a>] <a href="#entry-nikharva" class="skt-link">n.</a> math. cent milliards.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nikhila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निखिल</span> <i>nikhila</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-khila" class="skt-link">khila</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nikhila" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nikhila" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nikhilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nikhilā</i> complet, entier, intègre.<br> <i>nikhilena</i> adv. complètement, totalement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nikta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निक्त</span> <i>nikta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nij" class="skt-link">nij</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nikta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nikta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niktā</i> lavé, nettoyé, purifié <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> propre, pur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nikumbha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निकुम्भ</span> <i>nikumbha</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-kumbha" class="skt-link">kumbha</a>] <a href="#entry-nikumbha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth.
