[HV.] np. de Nikumbha, membre du <a href="#entry-ga_na" class="skt-link">gaṇa</a>, envoyé par <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> à <a href="#entry-Uvaaraa_nasii" class="skt-link">Vārāṇasī</a> pour provoquer <a href="#entry-Udivodaasa" class="skt-link">Divodāsa</a>; il accordait tous les vœux, sauf celui de <a href="#entry-Udivodaasa" class="skt-link">Divodāsa</a> d'avoir un fils; celui-ci voulut l'expulser, mais <a href="#entry-Unikumbha" class="skt-link">Nikumbha</a> maudit la ville pour 2000 ans, afin que <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> puisse en profiter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Nikumbha_1, <a href="#entry-raak_sasa" class="skt-link">rākṣasa</a> fils de <a href="#entry-Ukumbhakar_na" class="skt-link">Kumbhakarṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nikumbhilaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निकुम्भिला</span> <i>nikumbhilā</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-kumbhila" class="skt-link">kumbhila</a>] <a href="#entry-nikumbhilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du bosquet Nikumbhilā, à la porte ouest de <a href="#entry-Ulafkaa" class="skt-link">Laṅkā</a>, lieu de rites sacrificiels des <a href="#entry-raak_sasaas" class="skt-link">rākṣasās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nilambita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निलम्बित</span> <i>nilambita</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-lambita" class="skt-link">lambita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nilambita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nilambita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nilambitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nilambitā</i> détaché; radié de ⟨i. iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nilaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निलय</span> <i>nilaya</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nilii" class="skt-link">nilī</a>] <a href="#entry-nilaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> séjour; demeure, résidence; refuge.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nilii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निली</span> <i>nilī</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-lii" class="skt-link">lī</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. md. <i>(nilīyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nilīna</i><i>)</i> se réfugier, se tapir, se cacher.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimaa_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निमा</span> <i>nimā_1</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-maa_1" class="skt-link">mā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. md. <i>(nimimīte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nimita_1</i><i>)</i> mesurer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimaa_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निमा</span> <i>nimā_2</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-maa_4" class="skt-link">mā_4</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(nimayate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nimita_2</i><i>)</i> échanger, troquer (contre ⟨i.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimagna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निमग्न</span> <i>nimagna</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nimajj" class="skt-link">nimajj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nimagna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nimagna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nimagnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nimagnā</i> plongé, immergé; absorbé; noyé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimajj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निमज्ज्</span> <i>nimajj</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-majj" class="skt-link">majj</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(nimajjati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nimagna</i><i>)</i> plonger, submerger <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être immergé, couler; périr — ca. <i>(nimajjayati)</i> immerger, noyer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निमन्त्र</span> <i>nimantra</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(nimantrayate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nimantrita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>upa</i><i>)</i> inviter à, enjoindre de ⟨loc. inf.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimantra_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निमन्त्रण</span> <i>nimantraṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nimantra" class="skt-link">nimantra</a>] <a href="#entry-nimantra_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> injonction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निमय</span> <i>nimaya</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nimaa_2" class="skt-link">nimā_2</a>] <a href="#entry-nimaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> échange, troc.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimba">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निम्ब</span> <i>nimba</i> var. <i>nīma</i> <a href="#entry-nimba" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Azadirachta indica</i>, margousier ou arbre <i>nīm</i>, une méliacée; ses fleurs sont violettes; ses feuilles doublement pennées; de ses fruits amers on fait une huile médicinale [<a href="#entry-taila" class="skt-link">taila</a>]; son écorce et ses feuilles ont aussi des utilisations médicinales; ses jeunes rameaux servent de brosse à dents; on mâche ses feuilles aux cérémonies funéraires; l'idole de <a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha" class="skt-link">Jagannātha</a> est faite de son bois; cf. <a href="#entry-prabhadra" class="skt-link">prabhadra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hi. <i>nīm</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimbaarka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nimbārka</i> [<a href="#entry-arka" class="skt-link">arka</a>] <a href="#entry-nimbaarka" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du maître [<a href="#entry-aacaarya" class="skt-link">ācārya</a>] Nimbārka «Qui fit paraître le Soleil du margousier [<a href="#entry-nimba" class="skt-link">nimba</a>] (pour recevoir son opposant jaïne refusant son hospitalité après le coucher du soleil)», né en pays dravidien au 11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il était ascète à Nandagrāma près de <a href="#entry-Ugovardhana" class="skt-link">Govardhana</a>; il prêcha la doctrine de dualité dans la non-dualité [<a href="#entry-dvaitaadvaita" class="skt-link">dvaitādvaita</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-nimba" class="skt-link">nimba</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimbadala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nimbadala</i> [<a href="#entry-dala" class="skt-link">dala</a>] <a href="#entry-nimbadala" class="skt-link">n.</a> inflorescences de <a href="#entry-nimba" class="skt-link">nimba</a>.<br> <i>nimbadalaṃ</i> <i>guḍaṃ</i> <i>ca</i> <i>svīkarotu</i> Veuillez prendre des fleurs de <a href="#entry-nimba" class="skt-link">nimba</a> et du sucre brun (formule rituelle du jour de l'an [<a href="#entry-yugaadi" class="skt-link">yugādi</a>]).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimbataila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nimbataila</i> [<a href="#entry-taila" class="skt-link">taila</a>] <a href="#entry-nimbataila" class="skt-link">n.</a> huile de margousier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimbuu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निम्बू</span> <i>nimbū</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-nimba" class="skt-link">nimba</a>] <a href="#entry-nimbuu" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Citrus acida</i>, citron <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>nimbūka</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nime_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निमेष</span> <i>nimeṣa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nimi_s" class="skt-link">nimiṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-nime_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> clignement; clin d'œil.<br> <i>nimeṣād</i> <i>iva</i> adv. en un clin d'œil.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निमि</span> <i>nimi</i> <a href="#entry-nimi" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de l'empereur Nimi «Clin d'œil», fils cadet d'<a href="#entry-Uik_svaaku" class="skt-link">Ikṣvāku</a>; il voulut célébrer un sacrifice [<a href="#entry-yaj_na" class="skt-link">yajña</a>] de 500 ans, et demanda à son précepteur <a href="#entry-Uvasi_s_tha" class="skt-link">Vasiṣṭha</a> de le diriger; celui-ci, s'étant engagé auprès d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> pour le même service, lui demanda d'attendre; mais <a href="#entry-Unimi" class="skt-link">Nimi</a> fit exécuter le sacrifice par <a href="#entry-Ugautama" class="skt-link">Gautama</a> à sa place; au retour de <a href="#entry-Uvasi_s_tha" class="skt-link">Vasiṣṭha</a>, celui-ci le maudit, le condamnant à perdre son corps [<a href="#entry-videha" class="skt-link">videha</a>] et à ne plus vivre que dans les yeux des mortels; il y dirige à jamais le clignement des yeux [<a href="#entry-nimi_sa" class="skt-link">nimiṣa</a>]; son corps fut embaumé, et les sages [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] barattèrent sa cuisse pour donner naissance au roi <a href="#entry-Umithi" class="skt-link">Mithi</a> <a href="#entry-Ujanaka" class="skt-link">Janaka</a>, fondateur de la dynastie de <a href="#entry-Umithilaa" class="skt-link">Mithilā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimi_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निमिष्</span> <i>nimiṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-mi_s" class="skt-link">miṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nimiṣati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nimiṣita</i><i>)</i> cligner de l'œil.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimi_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निमिष</span> <i>nimiṣa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nimi_s" class="skt-link">nimiṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-nimi_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> clignement d'œil <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> instant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimiil">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निमील्</span> <i>nimīl</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-miil" class="skt-link">mīl</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nimīlati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nimīlita</i><i>)</i> fermer les yeux, s'endormir; se fermer (yeux, fleurs); mourir, disparaître.<br> <i>sā</i> <i>locane</i> <i>nyamīlayat</i> Elle ferma les yeux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niminaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निमिनाथ</span> <i>niminātha</i> cf. <a href="#entry-naminaatha" class="skt-link">naminātha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimita_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निमित</span> <i>nimita_1</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nimaa_1" class="skt-link">nimā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nimita_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nimita_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nimitaa_1" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nimitā_1</i> mesuré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimita_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निमित</span> <i>nimita_2</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nimaa_2" class="skt-link">nimā_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nimita_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nimita_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nimitaa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nimitā_2</i> échangé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimitta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निमित्त</span> <i>nimitta</i> <a href="#entry-nimitta" class="skt-link">n.</a> marque, signe; présage, augure <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> but, cible <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cause, moyen, motif <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (cas obliques) à cause de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] cause efficace (opp. <a href="#entry-upaadaana" class="skt-link">upādāna</a>, cause matérielle) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. cf. <a href="#entry-prav_rtti" class="skt-link">pravṛtti</a>, <a href="#entry-vyutpatti" class="skt-link">vyutpatti</a> — a. <a href="#entry-nimitta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nimitta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nimittaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nimittā</i> ifc. causé par — adv. var. <i>nimittas</i> pour telle raison.<br> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>nimitta</i> pour quoi faire?<br> <i>nimittaṃ</i> <i>yā</i> être la cause de.<br> <i>nimittīkṛ</i> considérer comme la cause; utiliser comme moyen.<br> <i>nimittīkṛtya</i> en conséquence, à cause de ⟨acc.⟩.<br> <i>nimittībhū</i> devenir la cause de ⟨loc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimittakaara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nimittakāraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-kaara_na" class="skt-link">kāraṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-nimittakaara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. cause efficace (dit not. de Dieu en tant que Créateur); cause instrumentale (par ex. le bâton [<a href="#entry-da_n_da" class="skt-link">daṇḍa</a>] du potier pour fabriquer le pot [<a href="#entry-gha_ta" class="skt-link">ghaṭa</a>]).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimittanaimittika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nimittanaimittika</i> [<a href="#entry-naimittika" class="skt-link">naimittika</a>] <a href="#entry-nimittanaimittika" class="skt-link">n.</a> du. phil. la cause et l'effet.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimittasaptamii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nimittasaptamī</i> [<a href="#entry-saptamii" class="skt-link">saptamī</a>] <a href="#entry-nimittasaptamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> gram. locatif de motif.<br> <a href="#entry-nimitta" class="skt-link">nimitta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निम्न</span> <i>nimna</i> a. <a href="#entry-nimna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nimna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nimnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nimnā</i> profond, bas, creux — ifc. incliné vers — <a href="#entry-nimna" class="skt-link">n.</a> dépression du sol, cavité; profondeur.<br> <i>nimnais</i> vers le bas.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nimnonnata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nimnonnata</i> [<a href="#entry-unnata" class="skt-link">unnata</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nimnonnata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nimnonnata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nimnonnataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nimnonnatā</i> modelé (creux et en relief); bosselé (haut et bas).<br> <a href="#entry-nimna" class="skt-link">nimna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ninad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निनद्</span> <i>ninad</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-nad" class="skt-link">nad</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ninadati)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pra</i><i>)</i> bruire, résonner; crier — ca. <i>(ninādayati)</i> faire résonner, remplir de bruit ou de cris.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nind">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">निन्द्</span> <i>nind</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nindati)</i> pft. <i>(nininda)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nindita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>nedya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>prati</i><i>)</i> blâmer; reprocher; critiquer, accuser; dénigrer, railler <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> punir; mépriser, humilier; maudire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nindaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निन्दा</span> <i>nindā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nind" class="skt-link">nind</a>] <a href="#entry-nindaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> blâme, reproche; censure; controverse, diffamation, injure; outrage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nindaastuti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nindāstuti</i> [<a href="#entry-stuti" class="skt-link">stuti</a>] <a href="#entry-nindaastuti" class="skt-link">f.</a> fausse louange, louange ironique; ironie.<br> <a href="#entry-nindaa" class="skt-link">nindā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nindita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निन्दित</span> <i>nindita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nind" class="skt-link">nind</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nindita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nindita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ninditaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ninditā</i> blâmé; blâmable, méprisable; prohibé, interdit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nipaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निपा</span> <i>nipā</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-paa_1" class="skt-link">pā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nipibati)</i> boire dans; sucer; absorber, assécher.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nipaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निपान</span> <i>nipāna</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nipaa" class="skt-link">nipā</a>] <a href="#entry-nipaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de boire, d'imbiber <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> abreuvoir; réservoir, étang.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nipaat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निपात्</span> <i>nipāt</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-nipat" class="skt-link">nipat</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(nipātayati)</i> faire tomber, abattre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nipaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निपात</span> <i>nipāta</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nipat" class="skt-link">nipat</a>] <a href="#entry-nipaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> descente, chute; déchéance, ruine; mort <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait de se ruer, attaque; renversement, destruction, meurtre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. irrégularité, exception; particule.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nipaatana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निपातन</span> <i>nipātana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nipat" class="skt-link">nipat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nipaatana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nipaatana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nipaatanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nipātanā</i> qui renverse, qui détruit — <a href="#entry-nipaatana" class="skt-link">n.</a> renversement, destruction, meurtre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. exception; expression figée, forme idiomatique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nipat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निपत्</span> <i>nipat</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-pat_1" class="skt-link">pat_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nipatati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nipatita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>upa</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> descendre, fondre sur, se jeter sur; tomber <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> déchoir, être perdu — ca. <i>(nipātayati)</i> faire tomber, abattre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nipatana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निपतन</span> <i>nipatana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nipat" class="skt-link">nipat</a>] <a href="#entry-nipatana" class="skt-link">n.</a> chute, descente <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vol.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nipatita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निपतित</span> <i>nipatita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nipat" class="skt-link">nipat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nipatita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nipatita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nipatitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nipatitā</i> tombé, descendu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dégradé, corrompu.<br> <i>nabhonipatita</i> descendu des cieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nipu_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निपुण</span> <i>nipuṇa</i> a. <a href="#entry-nipu_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nipu_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nipu_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nipuṇā</i> intelligent, instruit; habile, subtil, malin; adroit, précis, exact.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nir">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्</span> <i>nir</i> cf. <a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nir_naya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्णय</span> <i>nirṇaya</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nir_nii" class="skt-link">nirṇī</a>] <a href="#entry-nir_naya" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait d'emporter, d'enlever <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> décision, détermination <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. déduction, inférence; conclusion; démonstration <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. sentence, verdict; décret.<br> <i>tava</i> <i>nirṇaye</i> <i>sthāsyāmi</i> je m'en remets à ta décision.<br> <i>tava</i> <i>nirṇayaḥ</i> <i>pramāṇam</i> ta décision fait autorité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nir_nii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्णी</span> <i>nirṇī</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-nii_1" class="skt-link">nī_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nirṇayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirṇīta</i><i>)</i> emmener, emporter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> déduire; conclure, déterminer, décider, établir, fixer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nir_niita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्णीत</span> <i>nirṇīta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nir_nii" class="skt-link">nirṇī</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nir_niita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nir_niita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nir_niitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirṇītā</i> déterminé, déduit, établi; décidé, conclu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nir_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरृ</span> <i>nirṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-_r" class="skt-link">ṛ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nirṛcchati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirṛta</i><i>)</i> tomber, être privé de; se démonter, se désagréger, pourrir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nir_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरृत</span> <i>nirṛta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nir_r" class="skt-link">nirṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nir_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nir_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nir_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirṛtā</i> détruit, démoli, corrompu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nir_rti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरृति</span> <i>nirṛti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nir_r" class="skt-link">nirṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-nir_rti" class="skt-link">f.</a> destruction, dissolution, déclin, corruption, décomposition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> calamité, malédiction; abîme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. le Mal; syn. <a href="#entry-a_mhas" class="skt-link">aṃhas</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Nirṛti «Calamité», force universelle de destruction, épouse d'<a href="#entry-Uadharma" class="skt-link">Adharma</a>, associée à <a href="#entry-Um_rtyu" class="skt-link">Mṛtyu</a>; elle est mère des ogres-vampires [<a href="#entry-raak_sasaas" class="skt-link">rākṣasās</a>] — <a href="#entry-nir_rti" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du souffle divin [<a href="#entry-marut" class="skt-link">marut</a>] Nirṛti_1; il est l'une des 11 formes de <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a> [<a href="#entry-ekaadazarudraas" class="skt-link">ekādaśarudrās</a>]; gardien [<a href="#entry-dikpaala" class="skt-link">dikpāla</a>] du Sud-Ouest, il règne sur l'esprit des morts [<a href="#entry-bhuuta" class="skt-link">bhūta</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niraahaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निराहार</span> <i>nirāhāra</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-aahaara" class="skt-link">āhāra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niraahaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niraahaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niraahaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirāhārā</i> qui jeûne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niraak_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निराकृ</span> <i>nirākṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-aak_r" class="skt-link">ākṛ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">8</span>] pr. <i>(nirākaroti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirākṛta</i><i>)</i> défaire, séparer, détacher; écarter, expulser, répudier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rejeter, refuser, négliger; contredire, démentir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niraakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निराकार</span> <i>nirākāra</i> [<a href="#entry-nir" class="skt-link">nir</a>-<a href="#entry-aakaara_1" class="skt-link">ākāra_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niraakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niraakaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niraakaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirākārā</i> informe; incorporel (se dit du <a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a>); indécelable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> insignifiant, sans importance; sans objet; opp. <a href="#entry-saakaara" class="skt-link">sākāra</a> — <a href="#entry-niraakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. les Cieux; l'Être suprême.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niraakara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निराकरण</span> <i>nirākaraṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-niraak_r" class="skt-link">nirākṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-niraakara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> rejet; expulsion; répudiation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> opposition; contradiction, négation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> oubli; négligence (not. des devoirs religieux).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niraakari_s_nu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निराकरिष्णु</span> <i>nirākariṣṇu</i> [<a href="#entry-niraak_r" class="skt-link">nirākṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niraakari_s_nu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niraakari_s_nu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niraakari_s_nu" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui rejette, qui répudie; qui chasse, qui bannit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui obstrue, qui empêche de ⟨abl.⟩; envieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niraalambana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरालम्बन</span> <i>nirālambana</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-aalambana" class="skt-link">ālambana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niraalambana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niraalambana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niraalambanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirālambanā</i> privé d'appui, sans support <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [«sans support dans le réel»] imaginaire, illusoire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niraaloka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरालोक</span> <i>nirāloka</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-aaloka" class="skt-link">āloka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niraaloka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niraaloka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niraalokaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirālokā</i> sombre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niraamaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरामय</span> <i>nirāmaya</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-aamaya" class="skt-link">āmaya</a>] <a href="#entry-niraamaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> bonne santé — a. <a href="#entry-niraamaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niraamaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niraamayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirāmayā</i> en bonne santé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> intact; en état de marche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niraasa_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरास</span> <i>nirāsa_1</i> [act. <a href="#entry-niras" class="skt-link">niras</a>] <a href="#entry-niraasa_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> rejet, exclusion, refus; expulsion <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait de vomir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> élision (d'un son); prononciation fautive.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niraasa_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरास</span> <i>nirāsa_2</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-aasa_2" class="skt-link">āsa_2</a>] <a href="#entry-niraasa_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> sans siège; sans abri.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niraatman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरात्मन्</span> <i>nirātman</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">ātman</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niraatman" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niraatman" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niraatmanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirātmanī</i> sans âme; sans individualité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niraaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निराश</span> <i>nirāśa</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-aazaa_2" class="skt-link">āśā_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niraaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niraaza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niraazaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirāśā</i> indifférent, sans désir; déprimé, désespéré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirabhilaa_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरभिलाष</span> <i>nirabhilāṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-abhilaa_sa" class="skt-link">abhilāṣa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirabhilaa_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirabhilaa_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirabhilaa_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirabhilāṣā</i> ne désirant pas, ne recherchant pas; sans convoitise, indifférent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirahafkaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरहंकार</span> <i>nirahaṃkāra</i> var. <i>nirahaṅkāra</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-ahafkaara" class="skt-link">ahaṃkāra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niraha_mkaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niraha_mkaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niraha_mkaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirahaṃkārā</i> sans ego.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirak_sara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरक्षर</span> <i>nirakṣara</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-ak_sara" class="skt-link">akṣara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirak_sara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirak_sara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirak_saraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirakṣarā</i> illettré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niram">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरम्</span> <i>niram</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-ram" class="skt-link">ram</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(niramate)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(niramati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirata</i><i>)</i> cesser, s'arrêter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> trouver son plaisir à, être attaché à — ca. <i>(niramayati)</i> arrêter; contenter, faire plaisir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirantara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरन्तर</span> <i>nirantara</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-antara" class="skt-link">antara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirantara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirantara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirantaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirantarā</i> compact, dense; ininterrompu, continu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> perpétuel; constant, fidèle; sincère — ifc. plein de; rempli de.<br> <i>nirantaram</i> adv. étroitement, fermement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sans interruption, continuellement; immédiatement, aussitôt.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirantaraaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरन्तराय</span> <i>nirantarāya</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-antaraaya" class="skt-link">antarāya</a>] <a href="#entry-nirantaraaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> absence d'obstacle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirantaraayatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirantarāyatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-nirantaraayatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. état de conscience où l'on est libre de tout obstacle.<br> <a href="#entry-nirantaraaya" class="skt-link">nirantarāya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirapek_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरपेक्ष</span> <i>nirapekṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-apek_saa" class="skt-link">apekṣā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirapek_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirapek_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirapek_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirapekṣā</i> indifférent à, sans égard pour, indépendant de ⟨iic. loc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> indifférent; désintéressé; impartial; opp. <a href="#entry-saapek_sa" class="skt-link">sāpekṣa</a> — <a href="#entry-nirapek_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirapekṣā</i> indifférence.<br> <i>nirapekṣam</i> adv. avec indifférence; nonobstant; sans faire attention, accidentellement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirapek_sataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirapekṣatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-nirapek_sataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> indifférence, désintérêt; impartialité; tolérance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirapek_satva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirapekṣatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-nirapek_satva" class="skt-link">n.</a> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirapek_sin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirapekṣin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirapek_sin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirapek_sin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirapek_si_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirapekṣiṇī</i> indifférent.<br> <a href="#entry-nirapek_sa" class="skt-link">nirapekṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरर्थ</span> <i>nirartha</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirartha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirarthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirarthā</i> [«sans fortune»] pauvre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> inutile, vain; absurde.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirarthaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirarthaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirarthaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirarthaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirarthikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirarthikā</i> inutile, vain; absurde.<br> <i>nirarthakam</i> adv. en vain, inutilement.<br> <a href="#entry-nirartha" class="skt-link">nirartha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niras">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरस्</span> <i>niras</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-as_2" class="skt-link">as_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(nirasyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirasta</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>nirasya</i><i>)</i> rejeter; chasser, expulser, bannir; renoncer à ⟨acc.⟩; réfuter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> arracher, détruire, anéantir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirasta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरस्त</span> <i>nirasta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-niras" class="skt-link">niras</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirasta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirasta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirastaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirastā</i> rejeté, banni, refusé; à quoi on a renoncé; omis <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lancé (flèche, arme de jet) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vomi — <a href="#entry-nirasta" class="skt-link">n.</a> mauvaise prononciation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरस्य</span> <i>nirasya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-niras" class="skt-link">niras</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirasya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirasyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirasyā</i> à rejeter; à bannir; à réfuter — ind. ayant rejeté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरत</span> <i>nirata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-niram" class="skt-link">niram</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niratā</i> content, satisfait; enchanté par <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> voué à, engagé dans, absorbé par ⟨loc. i. iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरति</span> <i>nirati</i> [act. <a href="#entry-niram" class="skt-link">niram</a>] ifc. <a href="#entry-nirati" class="skt-link">f.</a> jouissance de; attachement à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niratizaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरतिशय</span> <i>niratiśaya</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-atizaya" class="skt-link">atiśaya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niratizaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niratizaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niratizayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niratiśayā</i> insurpassable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niravacchinna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरवच्छिन्न</span> <i>niravacchinna</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-avacchinna" class="skt-link">avacchinna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niravacchinna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niravacchinna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niravacchinnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niravacchinnā</i> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] non délimité; non déterminé, non discriminé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niravalamba">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरवलम्ब</span> <i>niravalamba</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-avalamba" class="skt-link">avalamba</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niravalamba" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niravalamba" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niravalambaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niravalambā</i> sans appui, sans support.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niravalambana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरवलम्बन</span> <i>niravalambana</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-avalambana" class="skt-link">avalambana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niravalambana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niravalambana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niravalambanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niravalambanā</i> sans appui, sans support <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui n'appartient à personne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niravasaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरवसा</span> <i>niravasā</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-avasaa" class="skt-link">avasā</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(niravasyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niravasita</i><i>)</i> rejeter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niravasita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरवसित</span> <i>niravasita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-niravasaa" class="skt-link">niravasā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niravasita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niravasita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niravasitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niravasitā</i> exclu, rejeté (se dit d'un hors-caste ou d'un <a href="#entry-zuudra" class="skt-link">śūdra</a> intouchable, par ex. un <a href="#entry-caa_n_daala" class="skt-link">cāṇḍāla</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niraya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरय</span> <i>niraya</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-aya" class="skt-link">aya</a>] <a href="#entry-niraya" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«mauvaise chance»] enfer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Niraya, l'Enfer personnifié, fils de la peur et de la mort.<br> <i>vedānāṃ</i> <i>lekhakāścaiva</i> <i>te</i> <i>vai</i> <i>nirayagāminaḥ</i> [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] Celui qui écrit le <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> en vérité va en enfer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरयन</span> <i>nirayana</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-ayana" class="skt-link">ayana</a>] <a href="#entry-nirayana" class="skt-link">n.</a> astr. zodiaque sidéral [«sans mouvement»], lié aux constellations; opp. <a href="#entry-saayana" class="skt-link">sāyana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nira_njana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरञ्जन</span> <i>nirañjana</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-a_njana" class="skt-link">añjana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nira_njana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nira_njana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nira_njanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirañjanā</i> immaculé; pur, non mélangé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sans émotions, dispassionné — <a href="#entry-nira_njana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Nirañjana, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «l'Immaculé» — <a href="#entry-nira_njanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirañjanā</i> pleine lune; syn. <a href="#entry-puur_nimaa" class="skt-link">pūrṇimā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Nirañjanā, épith. de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a> «l'Immaculée».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirbandh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्बन्ध्</span> <i>nirbandh</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-bandh" class="skt-link">bandh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(nirbadhnāti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirbaddha</i><i>)</i> insister, persister.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirbandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्बन्ध</span> <i>nirbandha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirbandh" class="skt-link">nirbandh</a>] <a href="#entry-nirbandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> insistance, persévérance; ténacité, obstination.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirbhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्भर</span> <i>nirbhara</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-bhara" class="skt-link">bhara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirbhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirbhara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirbharaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirbharā</i> [«sans mesure»] excessif, violent; profond (sommeil); étroit (étreinte) — ifc. plein de.<br> <i>nirbharam</i> adv. excessivement, violemment; complètement, à fond; profondément.<br> <i>nirbharaṃ</i> <i>prasupta</i> a. m. n. profondément endormi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirbharts">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्भर्त्स्</span> <i>nirbharts</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-bharts" class="skt-link">bharts</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(nirbhartsayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirbhartsita</i><i>)</i> menacer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirbhartsana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्भर्त्सन</span> <i>nirbhartsana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirbharts" class="skt-link">nirbharts</a>] <a href="#entry-nirbhartsana" class="skt-link">n.</a> menace.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirbhaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्भय</span> <i>nirbhaya</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-bhaya" class="skt-link">bhaya</a>] <a href="#entry-nirbhaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> absence de peur; sécurité — a. <a href="#entry-nirbhaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirbhaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirbhayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirbhayā</i> sans peur, intrépide; tranquille; en sécurité.<br> <i>nirbhayam</i> adv. sans peur, de manière intrépide.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirbhid">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्भिद्</span> <i>nirbhid</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-bhid_1" class="skt-link">bhid_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(nirbhinatti)</i> pr. md. <i>(nirbhintte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirbhinna</i><i>)</i> fendre, briser, diviser, ouvrir, percer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> blesser; détruire — ps. <i>(nirbhidyate)</i> être fendu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirbhinna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्भिन्न</span> <i>nirbhinna</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nirbhid" class="skt-link">nirbhid</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirbhinna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirbhinna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirbhinnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirbhinnā</i> ouvert, éclos (fleur) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> divisé, séparé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> percé, pénétré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> découvert, trahi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirbiija">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्बीज</span> <i>nirbīja</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-biija" class="skt-link">bīja</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirbiija" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirbiija" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirbiijaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirbījā</i> [«sans germe»] infécond, stérile (opp. <a href="#entry-sabiija" class="skt-link">sabīja</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirdah">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्दह्</span> <i>nirdah</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-dah_1" class="skt-link">dah_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nirdahati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirdagdha</i><i>)</i> brûler entièrement, se consumer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirdaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्दय</span> <i>nirdaya</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-dayaa" class="skt-link">dayā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirdaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirdaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirdayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirdayā</i> cruel, sans pitié.<br> <i>nirdayataram</i> adv. très cruellement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirdeva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्देव</span> <i>nirdeva</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirdeva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirdeva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirdevaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirdevā</i> abandonné des dieux; sans dieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirdevatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirdevatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-nirdevatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> absence de dieux.<br> <a href="#entry-nirdeva" class="skt-link">nirdeva</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirdeza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्देश</span> <i>nirdeśa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirdiz" class="skt-link">nirdiś</a>] <a href="#entry-nirdeza" class="skt-link">m.</a> indication, instruction (pour ⟨iic.⟩); ordre, commande <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> description, spécification; indication spéciale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirdezya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्देश्य</span> <i>nirdeśya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-nirdiz" class="skt-link">nirdiś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirdezya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirdezya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirdezyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirdeśyā</i> à déterminer, à définir; à établir, à prouver — <a href="#entry-nirdezya" class="skt-link">m.</a> math. énoncé d'un problème (trouver ... tel que).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirdh_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्धृ</span> <i>nirdhṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-dh_r" class="skt-link">dhṛ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nirdharati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirdhṛta</i><i>)</i> évaluer; déterminer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirdhaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्धार</span> <i>nirdhāra</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirdh_r" class="skt-link">nirdhṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-nirdhaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de définir, de déterminer; évaluation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirdhaara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्धारण</span> <i>nirdhāraṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirdh_r" class="skt-link">nirdhṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-nirdhaara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de définir, de déterminer; évaluation
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirdhaav">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्धाव्</span> <i>nirdhāv</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-dhaav_1" class="skt-link">dhāv_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nirdhāvati)</i> jaillir de, s'échapper de, se sauver de ⟨abl.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirdharma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्धर्म</span> <i>nirdharma</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-dharma" class="skt-link">dharma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirdharma" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirdharma" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirdharmaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirdharmā</i> impie; injuste, inique; contre-nature.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirdhuuma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्धूम</span> <i>nirdhūma</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-dhuuma" class="skt-link">dhūma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirdhuuma" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirdhuuma" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirdhuumaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirdhūmā</i> sans fumée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirdi_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्दिष्ट</span> <i>nirdiṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nirdiz" class="skt-link">nirdiś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirdi_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirdi_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirdi_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirdiṣṭā</i> indiqué, annoncé; désigné, spécifié.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirdiz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्दिश्</span> <i>nirdiś</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-diz_1" class="skt-link">diś_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nirdiśati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirdiṣṭa</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> désigner, stipuler, prescrire; spécifier, définir, nommer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirdo_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्दोष</span> <i>nirdoṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-do_sa" class="skt-link">doṣa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirdo_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirdo_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirdo_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirdoṣā</i> sans faute, impeccable; sans péché, innocent.<br> <i>nirdoṣīkṛ</i> innocenter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirdvandva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्द्वन्द्व</span> <i>nirdvandva</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-dvandva" class="skt-link">dvandva</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirdvandva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirdvandva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirdvandvaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirdvandvā</i> phil. qui a transcendé les contraires.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirgam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्गम्</span> <i>nirgam</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-gam" class="skt-link">gam</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nirgacchati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirgata</i><i>)</i> s'en aller, sortir, disparaître.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirgama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्गम</span> <i>nirgama</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirgam" class="skt-link">nirgam</a>] <a href="#entry-nirgama" class="skt-link">m.</a> départ, disparition; cessation, fin; sortie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirgamana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्गमन</span> <i>nirgamana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirgam" class="skt-link">nirgam</a>] <a href="#entry-nirgamana" class="skt-link">n.</a> sortie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirgata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्गत</span> <i>nirgata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nirgam" class="skt-link">nirgam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirgata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirgata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirgataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirgatā</i> allé, parti; disparu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirgh_r_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्घृण</span> <i>nirghṛṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-gh_r_naa" class="skt-link">ghṛṇā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirgh_r_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirgh_r_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirgh_r_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirghṛṇā</i> cruel, sans pitié.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirgh_r_nataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirghṛṇatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-nirgh_r_nataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> cruauté.<br> <a href="#entry-nirgh_r_na" class="skt-link">nirghṛṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirgrantha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्ग्रन्थ</span> <i>nirgrantha</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-grantha" class="skt-link">grantha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirgrantha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirgrantha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirgranthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirgranthā</i> sans attachements, libre de liens — <a href="#entry-nirgrantha" class="skt-link">m.</a> ermite retiré du monde, ascète errant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mendiant jaïne nu — <a href="#entry-nirgrantha" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. jaïnisme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirgu_n_dii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्गुण्डी</span> <i>nirguṇḍī</i> <a href="#entry-nirgu_n_dii" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Vitex negundo</i>; cf. <a href="#entry-sinduvaara" class="skt-link">sinduvāra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>nirguṇḍi</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirgu_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्गुण</span> <i>nirguṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirgu_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirgu_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirgu_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirguṇā</i> sans corde (arc) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dépourvu de qualités, mauvais, vicieux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. sans attributs, indifférencié (opp. <a href="#entry-sagu_na" class="skt-link">saguṇa</a>).<br> <i>nirguṇa</i> <i>brahman</i> phil. le Divin transcendant, non manifesté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirgu_nagu_nii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirguṇaguṇī</i> [red.] a. <a href="#entry-nirgu_nagu_nii" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirgu_nagu_nii" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirgu_nagu_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. [«avec et sans qualités»] qualifie la nature divine duale, à la fois immanente et transcendante.<br> <a href="#entry-nirgu_na" class="skt-link">nirguṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirh_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्हृ</span> <i>nirhṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-h_r_1" class="skt-link">hṛ_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nirharati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirhṛta</i><i>)</i> emporter; tirer, extraire (de ⟨abl.⟩); enlever, détruire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> exporter (marchandises); transporter (cadavre) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> saigner; évacuer; purger — pr. md. <i>(nirharate)</i> exclure ou délivrer de ⟨abl.⟩; se débarrasser de; changer (vêtement).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirhaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्हार</span> <i>nirhāra</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirh_r" class="skt-link">nirhṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-nirhaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> butin <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> évacuation (excréments); transport (cadavre) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> déduction <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> odeur pénétrante.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirhaarin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्हारिन्</span> <i>nirhārin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nirh_r" class="skt-link">nirhṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirhaarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirhaarin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirhaari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirhāriṇī</i> diffusant (odeur) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui va et vient <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> riche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirhara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्हरण</span> <i>nirharaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirh_r" class="skt-link">nirhṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-nirhara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> extraction; évacuation; expulsion; destruction; transport de cadavre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirhara_niiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्हरणीय</span> <i>nirharaṇīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-nirh_r" class="skt-link">nirhṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirhara_niiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirhara_niiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirhara_niiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirharaṇīyā</i> qui doit être extrait ou évacué.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirhraada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्ह्राद</span> <i>nirhrāda</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-hraada" class="skt-link">hrāda</a>] <a href="#entry-nirhraada" class="skt-link">m.</a> défécation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niriik_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरीक्ष्</span> <i>nirīkṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-iik_s" class="skt-link">īkṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(nirīkṣate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirīkṣita</i><i>)</i> regarder attentivement, examiner, scruter; contempler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niriik_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरीक्षण</span> <i>nirīkṣaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-niriik_s" class="skt-link">nirīkṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niriik_sa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niriik_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niriik_sa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirīkṣaṇā</i> qui regarde, qui voit ⟨iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-niriik_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> vue; regard, observation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tour d'horizon; investigation, étude <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. aspect des planètes — <a href="#entry-niriik_sa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirīkṣaṇā</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niriik_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरीक्षा</span> <i>nirīkṣā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-niriik_s" class="skt-link">nirīkṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-niriik_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> examen, observation (not. des astres).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niriik_saka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरीक्षक</span> <i>nirīkṣaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-niriik_s" class="skt-link">nirīkṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niriik_saka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niriik_saka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niriik_sikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirīkṣikā</i> qui regarde, qui voit; qui visite — <a href="#entry-niriik_saka" class="skt-link">m.</a> inspecteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niriizvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरीश्वर</span> <i>nirīśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niriizvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niriizvara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niriizvaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirīśvarā</i> phil. athée; opp. <a href="#entry-sezvara" class="skt-link">seśvara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirjala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्जल</span> <i>nirjala</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-jala" class="skt-link">jala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirjala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirjala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirjalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirjalā</i> sec, aride; désertique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirjalaikaadazii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirjalaikādaśī</i> [<a href="#entry-ekaadazii" class="skt-link">ekādaśī</a>] <a href="#entry-nirjalaikaadazii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. 11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour de la quinzaine claire du mois <a href="#entry-jyai_s_tha" class="skt-link">jyaiṣṭha</a>; on y jeûne sans nourriture ni eau.<br> <a href="#entry-nirjala" class="skt-link">nirjala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirjana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्जन</span> <i>nirjana</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-jana" class="skt-link">jana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirjana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirjana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirjanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirjanā</i> [«sans hommes»] solitaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirjara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्जर</span> <i>nirjara</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-jara" class="skt-link">jara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirjara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirjara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirjaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirjarā</i> qui ne vieillit pas; toujours jeune; inaltérable, impérissable; immortel — a. <a href="#entry-nirjaras" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirjaras" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirjaras" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirjaras</i> id. — <a href="#entry-nirjara" class="skt-link">m.</a> dieu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirjaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्जय</span> <i>nirjaya</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirji" class="skt-link">nirji</a>] <a href="#entry-nirjaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> conquête; victoire complète, triomphe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirjhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्झर</span> <i>nirjhara</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-jhara" class="skt-link">jhara</a>] <a href="#entry-nirjhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> cascade, cataracte; torrent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirji">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्जि</span> <i>nirji</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-ji" class="skt-link">ji</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nirjayati)</i> pr. md. <i>(nirjayate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirjita</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>nirjitya</i><i>)</i> vaincre, conquérir; soumettre, subjuguer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirlajja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्लज्ज</span> <i>nirlajja</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-lajjaa" class="skt-link">lajjā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirlajja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirlajja" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirlajjaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirlajjā</i> qui n'a pas honte.<br> <i>nirlajjam</i> adv. sans vergogne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirlu_n_th">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्लुण्ठ्</span> <i>nirluṇṭh</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-lu_n_th" class="skt-link">luṇṭh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nirluṇṭati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirluṭhita</i><i>)</i> piller, voler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirmaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्मा</span> <i>nirmā</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-maa_1" class="skt-link">mā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. md. <i>(nirmimīte)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(nirmāti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirmita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> mesurer; construire, façonner, fabriquer; créer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> produire, causer, déterminer; établir.<br> <i>citraṃ</i> <i>nirmā</i> peindre un tableau.<br> <i>dharmaṃ</i> <i>nirmā</i> établir une loi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirmaa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्माण</span> <i>nirmāṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirmaa" class="skt-link">nirmā</a>] <a href="#entry-nirmaa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> mesure; portée, étendue <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> construction, création, formation, composition; forme; œuvre — ifc. fait de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirmaa_nakaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirmāṇakāya</i> [<a href="#entry-kaaya" class="skt-link">kāya</a>] <a href="#entry-nirmaa_nakaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. le «corps créé», symbolisant la forme physique de l'Être présente dans la nature sensuelle de l'homme.<br> <a href="#entry-nirmaa_na" class="skt-link">nirmāṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirmaalya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्माल्य</span> <i>nirmālya</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-maalya" class="skt-link">mālya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirmaalya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirmaalya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirmaalyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirmālyā</i> écarté d'une guirlande; rejeté, périmé; (vêtement) porté la veille — <a href="#entry-nirmaalya" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. guirlande offerte en sacrifice; reste d'un sacrifice, nourriture consacrée; not. préparation sacrée du temple de <a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha" class="skt-link">Jagannātha</a> à <a href="#entry-Upurii" class="skt-link">Purī</a>, consistant en un filet contenant des grains de riz consacrés; on doit en manger un par jour.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirmaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्मान</span> <i>nirmāna</i> [<a href="#entry-nir" class="skt-link">nir</a>-<a href="#entry-maana_1" class="skt-link">māna_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirmaana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirmaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirmaanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirmānā</i> sans assurance; sans orgueil.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirmaanu_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्मानुष</span> <i>nirmānuṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-nir" class="skt-link">nir</a>-<a href="#entry-maanu_sa" class="skt-link">mānuṣa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirmaanu_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirmaanu_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirmaanu_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirmānuṣā</i> inhabité; désert, désolé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirmak_sikam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्मक्षिकम्</span> <i>nirmakṣikam</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-mak_sikaa" class="skt-link">makṣikā</a>] adv. sans mouches.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirmala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्मल</span> <i>nirmala</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-mala" class="skt-link">mala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirmala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirmala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirmalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirmalā</i> sans tache, pur, immaculé; brillant; vertueux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirmita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्मित</span> <i>nirmita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nirmaa" class="skt-link">nirmā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirmita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirmita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirmitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirmitā</i> fait, construit, façonné, fabriqué, formé, créé; fait par ⟨i. iic.⟩ avec ⟨abl. i. iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> établi, fixé; (jugement) rendu; (rite) accompli <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> choisi, déterminé, réservé (animal pour le sacrifice) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>nirmitās</i> bd. [<a href="#entry-Umahaayaana" class="skt-link">Mahāyāna</a>] classe de divinités <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>nimitta</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirmitavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्मितवत्</span> <i>nirmitavat</i> [ppa. <a href="#entry-nirmaa" class="skt-link">nirmā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirmitavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirmitavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirmitavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirmitavatī</i> auteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirmocana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्मोचन</span> <i>nirmocana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirmuc" class="skt-link">nirmuc</a>] <a href="#entry-nirmocana" class="skt-link">n.</a> libération; délivrance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirmok_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्मोक्ष</span> <i>nirmokṣa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirmuc" class="skt-link">nirmuc</a>] <a href="#entry-nirmok_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> libération, délivrance (de ⟨g. iic.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirmuc">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्मुच्</span> <i>nirmuc</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-muc_1" class="skt-link">muc_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(nirmuñcati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirmukta</i><i>)</i> libérer; délivrer de ⟨abl.⟩ — ps. <i>(nirmucyate)</i> être libéré de; se libérer, se débarrasser de ⟨abl.⟩; être privé de ⟨i.⟩; muer (serpent) — ca. <i>(nirmocayati)</i> rendre libre; libérer de ⟨abl.⟩; désengager.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirmukta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्मुक्त</span> <i>nirmukta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nirmuc" class="skt-link">nirmuc</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirmukta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirmukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirmuktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirmuktā</i> rendu libre; libéré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> privé de ⟨abl. i. iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> abandonné; perdu; disparu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> privé de tout; sans aucune possession <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (serpent) venant de muer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirmuktadeha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirmuktadeha</i> [<a href="#entry-deha" class="skt-link">deha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirmuktadeha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirmuktadeha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirmuktadehaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirmuktadehā</i> libéré de ses désirs sensuels.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirmuktasafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirmuktasaṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-safga" class="skt-link">saṅga</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirmuktasafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirmuktasafga" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirmuktasafgaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirmuktasaṅgā</i> libre des attachements au monde.<br> <a href="#entry-nirmukta" class="skt-link">nirmukta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirmukti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्मुक्ति</span> <i>nirmukti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirmuc" class="skt-link">nirmuc</a>] <a href="#entry-nirmukti" class="skt-link">f.</a> libération, délivrance (de ⟨abl. iic.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirnimitta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्निमित्त</span> <i>nirnimitta</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-nimitta" class="skt-link">nimitta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirnimitta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirnimitta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirnimittaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirnimittā</i> sans raison, sans motif; sans cause <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> désintéressé.<br> <i>nirnimittam</i> adv. sans cause.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirodha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरोध</span> <i>nirodha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirudh" class="skt-link">nirudh</a>] <a href="#entry-nirodha" class="skt-link">m.</a> emprisonnement, détention; restriction, restreinte, obstruction; répression, suppression <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. Noble Vérité [<a href="#entry-aaryasatya" class="skt-link">āryasatya</a>] de la cessation de la souffrance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirodhaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरोधक</span> <i>nirodhaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nirudh" class="skt-link">nirudh</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-nirodhaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirodhaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirodhikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirodhikā</i> qui obstrue; qui empêche, qui supprime.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirodhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirodhana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nirudh" class="skt-link">nirudh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirodhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirodhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirodhanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirodhanā</i> qui obstrue; qui empêche, qui supprime — <a href="#entry-nirodhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> détention; restriction, restreinte, obstruction; répression, suppression<br> <a href="#entry-nirodhaka" class="skt-link">nirodhaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niruddha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरुद्ध</span> <i>niruddha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nirudh" class="skt-link">nirudh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niruddha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niruddha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niruddhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niruddhā</i> obstrué, arrêté, retenu, bloqué; assiégé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> empêché, écarté, rejeté, exclu; supprimé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirudh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
