    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरुध्</span> <i>nirudh</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-rudh_2" class="skt-link">rudh_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(niruṇaddhi)</i> pr. md. <i>(nirunddhe)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niruddha</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> obstruer, bloquer, enfermer; restreindre; cacher <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> exclure, réprimer, tenir en échec.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirukta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरुक्त</span> <i>nirukta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nirvac" class="skt-link">nirvac</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirukta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niruktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niruktā</i> prononcé, exprimé, expliqué — <a href="#entry-nirukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> explication, interprétation, étymologie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. l'herméneutique, ou sémantique phonologique, interprétation du glossaire des termes védiques, un des 6 traités annexes du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> [<a href="#entry-vedaafga" class="skt-link">vedāṅga</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. du Nirukta, ouvrage d'herméneutique védique dû à <a href="#entry-Uyaaska" class="skt-link">Yāska</a>; c'est un commentaire de son glossaire <a href="#entry-Unigha_n_tu" class="skt-link">Nighaṇṭu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niruktatara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niruktatara</i> [<a href="#entry--tara" class="skt-link">-tara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niruktatara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niruktatara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niruktataraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niruktatarā</i> articulé, expliqué.<br> <a href="#entry-nirukta" class="skt-link">nirukta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirupadrava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरुपद्रव</span> <i>nirupadrava</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-upadrava" class="skt-link">upadrava</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirupadrava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirupadrava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirupadravaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirupadravā</i> hors de danger, en sécurité; paisible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirupama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरुपम</span> <i>nirupama</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-upama" class="skt-link">upama</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirupama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirupama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirupamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirupamā</i> inégalé; insurpassable; suprême.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niruu_dha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरूढ</span> <i>nirūḍha</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-ruu_dha" class="skt-link">rūḍha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niruu_dha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niruu_dha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niruu_dhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirūḍhā</i> développé, mur, adulte <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. de sens acquis conventionnel — <a href="#entry-niruu_dha" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. usage des mots selon leur sens naturel ou conventionnel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niruu_dhalak_sa_naa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirūḍhalakṣaṇā</i> [<a href="#entry-lak_sa_naa" class="skt-link">lakṣaṇā</a>] <a href="#entry-niruu_dhalak_sa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> gram. sens secondaire d'un mot devenu principal.<br> <a href="#entry-niruu_dha" class="skt-link">nirūḍha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niruup">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरूप्</span> <i>nirūp</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-ruup" class="skt-link">rūp</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(nirūpayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirūpita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>nirūpya</i><i>)</i> observer, examiner, considérer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> déterminer, statuer; assigner à ⟨dat. loc. inf.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> représenter (not. sur la scène), exprimer par gestes <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] déterminer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niruupa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरूपण</span> <i>nirūpaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-niruup" class="skt-link">nirūp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niruupa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niruupa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niruupa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirūpaṇā</i> ifc. qui détermine, qui définit — <a href="#entry-niruupa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niruupa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirūpaṇā</i> fait de statuer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> recherche, investigation, examen <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> forme, apparence — ifc. lit. forme des titres d'ouvrage «investigation de».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niruupa_niiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरूपणीय</span> <i>nirūpaṇīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-niruup" class="skt-link">nirūp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niruupa_niiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niruupa_niiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niruupa_niiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirūpaṇīyā</i> qui doit être investigué, qui doit être examiné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niruupaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरूपक</span> <i>nirūpaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-niruup" class="skt-link">nirūp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niruupaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niruupaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niruupikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirūpikā</i> observateur, examinateur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] descripteur, restricteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niruupayitavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरूपयितव्य</span> <i>nirūpayitavya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <a href="#entry-niruup" class="skt-link">nirūp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niruupayitavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niruupayitavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niruupayitavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirūpayitavyā</i> à déterminer; à estimer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niruupita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरूपित</span> <i>nirūpita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-niruup" class="skt-link">nirūp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niruupita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niruupita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niruupitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirūpitā</i> décrit, observé, examiné, évalué; déterminé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> assigné, désigné, choisi, élu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. objet source d'une relation; opp. <a href="#entry-ni_s_tha" class="skt-link">niṣṭha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a>] déterminé par, décrit par, restreint par ⟨iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-niruupita" class="skt-link">n.</a> état d'avoir été examiné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niruupya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निरूप्य</span> <i>nirūpya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-niruup" class="skt-link">nirūp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niruupya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niruupya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niruupyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirūpyā</i> à considérer, à examiner; à déterminer, à choisir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ce qui est décrit, ce qui est observé.<br> <i>nirūpya</i> <i>nirūpaka</i> <i>bhāva</i> phil. relation entre l'observateur et l'observé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirv_r">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirv_rt">

[<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(nirvartate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirvṛtta</i><i>)</i> se produire, avoir lieu; être accompli, être terminé; s'arrêter — ca. <i>(nirvartayati)</i> accomplir; arrêter, terminer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirv_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वृत</span> <i>nirvṛta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nirv_r" class="skt-link">nirvṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirv_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirv_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirv_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirvṛtā</i> calme, apaisé; content, satisfait; cessé, terminé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirv_rti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वृति</span> <i>nirvṛti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirv_r" class="skt-link">nirvṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-nirv_rti" class="skt-link">f.</a> plaisir, contentement, bonheur; sérénité, béatitude <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> extinction, accomplissement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirv_rtta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वृत्त</span> <i>nirvṛtta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nirv_rt" class="skt-link">nirvṛt</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirv_rtta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirv_rtta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirv_rttaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirvṛttā</i> développé, devenu, accompli, fait, prêt — ifc. qui provient de.<br> <i>kuśāmbena</i> <i>nirvṛttā</i> <i>nagarī</i> Une ville construite par <a href="#entry-Ukuzaamba" class="skt-link">Kuśāmba</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirv_rtti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirva_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वा</span> <i>nirvā</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-vaa_2" class="skt-link">vā_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(nirvāti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirvāta</i><i>)</i> cesser de souffler; s'éteindre, être éteint <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être calmé, être réconforté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvaa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वाण</span> <i>nirvāṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nirvaa" class="skt-link">nirvā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirvaa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvaa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvaa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirvāṇā</i> éteint, mort, disparu; apaisé — <a href="#entry-nirvaa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> extinction, disparition, repos, dissolution, anéantissement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [«extinction du feu des passions»] libération; cf. <a href="#entry-mok_sa" class="skt-link">mokṣa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. état de libération définitive; émancipation finale; béatitude, félicité suprême <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>nibbāna</i>.<br> <i>nirvāṇam</i> <i>kṛ</i> réduire à néant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvaa_na_tiikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirvāṇaṭīkā</i> [<a href="#entry-_tiikaa" class="skt-link">ṭīkā</a>] <a href="#entry-nirvaa_na_tiikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Nirvāṇaṭīkā, commentaire par <a href="#entry-Ubhaaskaraka_n_tha" class="skt-link">Bhāskarakaṇṭha</a> du <a href="#entry-Unirvaa_naprakara_na" class="skt-link">Nirvāṇaprakaraṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvaa_nadiik_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvaa_naprakara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirvāṇaprakaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-prakara_na" class="skt-link">prakaraṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-nirvaa_naprakara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Nirvāṇaprakaraṇa, 6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ section du <a href="#entry-Umok_sopaaya" class="skt-link">Mokṣopāya</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Unirvaa_na_tiikaa" class="skt-link">Nirvāṇaṭīkā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-nirvaa_na" class="skt-link">nirvāṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvaacya_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वाच्य</span> <i>nirvācya_1</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-vaacya" class="skt-link">vācya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirvaacya_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvaacya_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvaacyaa_1" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirvācyā_1</i> [«dont on ne doit pas médire»] irréprochable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dont il ne faut pas parler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvaacya_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वाच्य</span> <i>nirvācya_2</i> [ca. pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-nirvac" class="skt-link">nirvac</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirvaacya_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvaacya_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvaacyaa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirvācyā_2</i> à expliquer, à préciser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvaaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वाह</span> <i>nirvāha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirvah" class="skt-link">nirvah</a>] <a href="#entry-nirvaaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> accomplissement, complétion; persévérance; subsistance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvaahaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirvāhaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirvaahaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvaahaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvaahikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirvāhikā</i> qui effectue, qui accomplit — <a href="#entry-nirvaahikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirvāhikā</i> méd. diarrhée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvaahakaikya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

[<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] cohérence de terminaison de deux sections adjacentes.<br> <a href="#entry-nirvaahaka" class="skt-link">nirvāhaka</a> <br> <a href="#entry-nirvaaha" class="skt-link">nirvāha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvaaraa_nasi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvac">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वच्</span> <i>nirvac</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-vac" class="skt-link">vac</a>] pfp. <i>(</i><i>nirvacanīya</i>, <i>nirvācya_2</i><i>)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirukta</i><i>)</i> ps. <i>(nirucyate)</i> déclarer, énoncer; interpréter, expliquer; dériver <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dériver de ⟨abl.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> défendre de, avertir contre, ordonner de sortir de ⟨abl.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvacana_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वचन</span> <i>nirvacana_1</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-vacana" class="skt-link">vacana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirvacana_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvacana_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvacanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirvacanā</i> silencieux, qui ne dit rien <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> irréprochable, dont il n'y a rien à redire.<br> <i>nirvacanam</i> adv. en silence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvacana_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वचन</span> <i>nirvacana_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirvac" class="skt-link">nirvac</a>] <a href="#entry-nirvacana_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> dicton, proverbe; explication <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. interprétation, étymologie; cf. <a href="#entry-nirukta" class="skt-link">nirukta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvacanazaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirvacanaśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-nirvacanazaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. science traditionnelle de l'étymologie et de l'interprétation sémantique; syn. <a href="#entry-nirukta" class="skt-link">nirukta</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-nirvacana_2" class="skt-link">nirvacana_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvacaniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वचनीय</span> <i>nirvacanīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-nirvac" class="skt-link">nirvac</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirvacaniiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvacaniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvacaniiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirvacanīyā</i> à expliquer, à préciser; syn. <a href="#entry-nirvaacya_2" class="skt-link">nirvācya_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvah">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वह्</span> <i>nirvah</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-vah_1" class="skt-link">vah_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nirvahati)</i> conduire hors de, sauver de ⟨abl.⟩; couler hors de ⟨abl.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> accomplir; réussir, surmonter les obstacles <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> survivre par ⟨i.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être à la hauteur de la tache — ca. <i>(nirvāhayati)</i> accomplir, s'acquitter de.<br> <i>te</i> <i>svakarma</i> <i>sādhu</i> <i>niravāhayanācaran</i> ils se sont bien acquittés de leur tâche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvaha_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वहण</span> <i>nirvahaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirvah" class="skt-link">nirvah</a>] <a href="#entry-nirvaha_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> complétion, accomplissement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [théâtre] dénouement ou catastrophe d'un drame; syn. <a href="#entry-upasa_mh_rti" class="skt-link">upasaṃhṛti</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> récit rétrospectif par l'acteur de l'origine de son caractère <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mod. gestion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvahni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वह्नि</span> <i>nirvahni</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-vahni" class="skt-link">vahni</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirvahni" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvahni" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvahni" class="skt-link">f.</a> sans fumée; cf. <a href="#entry-nirdhuuma" class="skt-link">nirdhūma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वन</span> <i>nirvana</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-vana" class="skt-link">vana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirvana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirvanā</i> sans forêt; hors du bois; en rase campagne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvap">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वप्</span> <i>nirvap</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-vap_2" class="skt-link">vap_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nirvapati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirupta</i><i>)</i> verser, répandre; asperger <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> offrir (not. une libation) (à ⟨dat⟩).<br> <i>agnaye</i> <i>tvā</i> <i>juṣṭam</i> <i>nirvapāmi</i> [<a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a>] Ô Agni je verse la libation pour te satisfaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvapa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वपण</span> <i>nirvapaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirvap" class="skt-link">nirvap</a>] <a href="#entry-nirvapa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de verser, de répandre; arrosage, dispersion <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. offrande; not. libation aux mânes <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> don; donation — ifc. dispersion de, versement de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvar_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वर्ण</span> <i>nirvarṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-var_na" class="skt-link">varṇa</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(nirvarṇayati)</i> regarder, observer, contempler, examiner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvart">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वर्त्</span> <i>nirvart</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-nirv_rt" class="skt-link">nirvṛt</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(nirvartayati)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>nirvartya</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>nirvartya</i><i>)</i> accomplir; arrêter, terminer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvartin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वर्तिन्</span> <i>nirvartin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nirvart" class="skt-link">nirvart</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirvartin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvartin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvartinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirvartinī</i> qui accomplit, qui fait ⟨iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui se conduit ou qui agit mal.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvartya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वर्त्य</span> <i>nirvartya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-nirvart" class="skt-link">nirvart</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirvartya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvartya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvartyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirvartyā</i> à accomplir, à faire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. qualifie un objet [<a href="#entry-karman" class="skt-link">karman</a>] créé par l'action.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirveda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वेद</span> <i>nirveda</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nirvid" class="skt-link">nirvid</a>] <a href="#entry-nirveda" class="skt-link">m.</a> indifférence; dégoût; état de dépression.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvi_n_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्विण्ण</span> <i>nirviṇṇa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nirvid" class="skt-link">nirvid</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirvi_n_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvi_n_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvi_n_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirviṇṇā</i> découragé; peiné, déprimé; dégoûté (de ⟨abl. i. g. loc. iic.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvi_saya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्विषय</span> <i>nirviṣaya</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-vi_saya" class="skt-link">viṣaya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirvi_saya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvi_saya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvi_sayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirviṣayā</i> détaché des voluptés.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvicaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्विचार</span> <i>nirvicāra</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-vicaara" class="skt-link">vicāra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirvicaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvicaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvicaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirvicārā</i> sans réflexion, sans hésitation (opp. <a href="#entry-savicaara" class="skt-link">savicāra</a>).<br> <i>nirvicāram</i> adv. sans réfléchir, de manière inconsidérée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvid">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्विद्</span> <i>nirvid</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-vid_2" class="skt-link">vid_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. md. <i>(nirvitte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nirviṇṇa</i><i>)</i> se débarrasser de, jeter ⟨g. acc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être déprimé; être écœuré de ⟨abl. i.⟩; désespérer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvighna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्विघ्न</span> <i>nirvighna</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-vighna" class="skt-link">vighna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirvighna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvighna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvighnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirvighnā</i> qui n'est pas empêché.<br> <i>nirvighnam</i> adv. sans obstruction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirviirya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वीर्य</span> <i>nirvīrya</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-viirya" class="skt-link">vīrya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirviirya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirviirya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirviiryaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirvīryā</i> non viril, impuissant; lâche, faible — <a href="#entry-nirviirya" class="skt-link">m.</a> femmelette, faiblard, lâche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirviiryataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirvīryatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-nirviiryataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> affaiblissement, épuise­ment; impuissance.<br> <i>nirvīryatām</i> <i>i</i> devenir impuissant.<br> <a href="#entry-nirviirya" class="skt-link">nirvīrya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvikalpa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्विकल्प</span> <i>nirvikalpa</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-vikalpa" class="skt-link">vikalpa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirvikalpa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvikalpa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvikalpaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirvikalpā</i> indifférencié <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui n'admet pas d'alternative; indiscutable <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] qualifie le <a href="#entry-samaadhi" class="skt-link">samādhi</a> où l'identification à l'objet se fait en perdant conscience de son identité; opp. <a href="#entry-savikalpa" class="skt-link">savikalpa</a>.<br> <i>nirvikalpam</i> adv. sans aucun doute.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvikalpaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirvikalpaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirvikalpaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvikalpaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvikalpakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirvikalpakā</i> phil. évident, indiscutable — <a href="#entry-nirvikalpaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. connaissance ne reposant pas sur la perception <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] connaissance non discriminante; opp. <a href="#entry-savikalpaka" class="skt-link">savikalpaka</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-nirvikalpa" class="skt-link">nirvikalpa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvitarka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्वितर्क</span> <i>nirvitarka</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-vitarka" class="skt-link">vitarka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirvitarka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvitarka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvitarkaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirvitarkā</i> irraisonné, sans réflexion <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] qualifie le second stade de <a href="#entry-samaadhi" class="skt-link">samādhi</a>, où le <a href="#entry-yogin" class="skt-link">yogin</a> ressent directement l'objet de sa méditation, sans recours à la pensée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvize_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्विशेष</span> <i>nirviśeṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-vize_sa" class="skt-link">viśeṣa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirvize_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvize_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvize_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirviśeṣā</i> non différent; qui ne fait pas de différence — ifc. identique à — <a href="#entry-nirvize_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> absence de distinction, identité.<br> <i>nirviśeṣam</i> var. <i>nirviśeṣeṇa</i> adv. sans distinction, indifféremment; de même que ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvize_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निर्विशेषण</span> <i>nirviśeṣaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-vize_sa_na" class="skt-link">viśeṣaṇa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nirvize_sa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvize_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nirvize_sa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nirviśeṣaṇā</i> sans attributs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nirvize_satva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nirviśeṣatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-nirvize_satva" class="skt-link">n.</a> identité.<br> <a href="#entry-nirvize_sa" class="skt-link">nirviśeṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nis">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निस्</span> <i>nis</i> var. <i>nir</i>, <i>niś</i>, <i>niṣ</i>, <i>niḥ</i> pf. au delà, vers le dehors, hors de, disparition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> privé de, sans, négation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nisarga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निसर्ग</span> <i>nisarga</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-sarga" class="skt-link">sarga</a>] <a href="#entry-nisarga" class="skt-link">m.</a> évacuation; défécation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> abandon; don, récompense <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> condition naturelle, nature innée.<br> <i>nisargeṇa</i> var. <i>nisargāt</i> naturellement, spontanément.<br> <i>striyo</i> <i>hi</i> <i>nāma</i> <i>khalvetāḥ</i> <i>nisargādeva</i> <i>paṇḍitāḥ</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>puruṣāṇāṃ</i> <i>tu</i> <i>pāṇḍityaṃ</i> <i>śāstrair</i> <i>evopadiśyate</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<a href="#entry-Um_rcchaka_tikaa" class="skt-link">Mṛcchakaṭikā</a>] Les femmes, en fait, sont instruites par nature, alors que les hommes doivent acquérir la connaissance par l'étude.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nisargatas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nisargatas</i> [<a href="#entry--tas" class="skt-link">-tas</a>] adv. par nature; naturellement; spontanément.<br> <a href="#entry-nisarga" class="skt-link">nisarga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nistejas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निस्तेजस्</span> <i>nistejas</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-tejas" class="skt-link">tejas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nistejas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nistejas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nistejas" class="skt-link">f.</a> sans énergie, sans passion; faible; triste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nitamba">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नितम्ब</span> <i>nitamba</i> <a href="#entry-nitamba" class="skt-link">m.</a> bassin, hanches, fesses (de la femme).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nitambinii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nitambinī</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] <a href="#entry-nitambinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> jolie femme [«qui a un joli fessier»], callipyge; syn. <a href="#entry-suzro_nii" class="skt-link">suśroṇī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-nitamba" class="skt-link">nitamba</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nitaraam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नितराम्</span> <i>nitarām</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-tara" class="skt-link">tara</a>] adv. vers le bas <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> beaucoup, entièrement, complètement; spécialement; excessivement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> en tous cas, de toutes façons.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nitya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नित्य</span> <i>nitya</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a><a href="#entry--tya" class="skt-link">-tya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nitya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nitya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nityaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nityā</i> constant, fixe; invariable, permanent, continu; essentiel; perpétuel, éternel, indestructible <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> quotidien, régulier, ordinaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. (rite) obligatoire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. une règle A est <a href="#entry-nitya" class="skt-link">nitya</a> vis-à-vis d'une règle B si A est applicable que B le soit ou pas, mais pas l'inverse; elle a précédence sur une règle <a href="#entry-anitya" class="skt-link">anitya</a> — ifc. constamment engagé dans; voué à; habitué à — <a href="#entry-nityaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nityā</i> myth. np. de Nityā, épith. de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a> l'Éternelle.<br> <i>nityam</i> adv. toujours, éternellement; continuellement, régulièrement, tous les jours.<br> <i>na</i> <i>nityam</i> adv. jamais.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nityaananda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nityānanda</i> [<a href="#entry-aananda" class="skt-link">ānanda</a>] <a href="#entry-nityaananda" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de <a href="#entry-zrii" class="skt-link">śrī</a> Nityānanda Prabhu «Joie éternelle», mystique <a href="#entry-vai_s_nava" class="skt-link">vaiṣṇava</a> du Bengale [<a href="#entry-gau_diiya" class="skt-link">gauḍīya</a>], né en 1474, ami et disciple de <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a>; il est considéré dans la tradition <a href="#entry-gau_diiya" class="skt-link">gauḍīya</a> comme réincarnation de <a href="#entry-Ubalaraama" class="skt-link">Balarāma</a>; son nom est abrégé en Nitai, et avec <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a>-<a href="#entry-Uniimaaya" class="skt-link">Nīmāya</a> ils forment le couple de frères éternels Nimai-Nitai.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nityaanitya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nityānitya</i> [<a href="#entry-anitya" class="skt-link">anitya</a>] iic. opposition permanent/impermanent, éternel/éphémère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nityaanityavastuviveka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nityānityavastuviveka</i> [<a href="#entry-vastu_1" class="skt-link">vastu_1</a>-<a href="#entry-viveka" class="skt-link">viveka</a>] <a href="#entry-nityaanityavastuviveka" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] le discernement dans le réel entre le permanent et l'éphémère, première des qualifications de l'aspirant à la libération [<a href="#entry-saadhanacatu_s_taya" class="skt-link">sādhanacatuṣṭaya</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-nityaanitya" class="skt-link">nityānitya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nityaanumeya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nityānumeya</i> [<a href="#entry-anumeya" class="skt-link">anumeya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nityaanumeya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nityaanumeya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nityaanumeyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nityānumeyā</i> phil. [«toujours (seulement) inférable»] se dit d'un <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> caché, implicite par son influence présumée dans des textes révélés.<br> <a href="#entry-nitya" class="skt-link">nitya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nityabrahmacaarin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nityabrahmacārin</i> [<a href="#entry-brahmacaarin" class="skt-link">brahmacārin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nityabrahmacaarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nityabrahmacaarin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nityabrahmacaari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nityabrahmacāriṇī</i> qui n'éprouve pas de désir charnel — <a href="#entry-nityabrahmacaarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> «éternellement chaste».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nityagrantha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nityagrantha</i> [<a href="#entry-grantha" class="skt-link">grantha</a>] <a href="#entry-nityagrantha" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Nityagrantha, texte réglant les rites de purification, d'adoration et de méditation dû à <a href="#entry-Uraamaanuja" class="skt-link">Rāmānuja</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nityakarman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nityakarman</i> [<a href="#entry-karman" class="skt-link">karman</a>] <a href="#entry-nityakarman" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. observance rituelle obligatoire, quotidienne ou occasionnelle [<a href="#entry-naimittika" class="skt-link">naimittika</a>]; elle inclut les cinq rites majeurs [<a href="#entry-mahaayaj_na" class="skt-link">mahāyajña</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nityamukta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nityamukta</i> [<a href="#entry-mukta" class="skt-link">mukta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nityamukta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nityamukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nityamuktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nityamuktā</i> phil. éternellement libre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nityanarta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nityanarta</i> [<a href="#entry-narta" class="skt-link">narta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nityanarta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nityanarta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nityanartaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nityanartā</i> qui danse sans cesse — <a href="#entry-nityanarta" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nityasamaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nityasamāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-samaasa" class="skt-link">samāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-nityasamaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> composé autonome, dont le sens n'est pas la composition du sens de ses constituents.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nityasumafgalii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nityasumaṅgalī</i> [<a href="#entry-sumafgalii" class="skt-link">sumaṅgalī</a>] <a href="#entry-nityasumafgalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. prostituée sacrée [syn. <a href="#entry-devadaasii" class="skt-link">devadāsī</a>] de l'Inde du Sud.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nityatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nityatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-nityatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> perpétuité, éternité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nityavaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nityavāda</i> [<a href="#entry-vaada" class="skt-link">vāda</a>] <a href="#entry-nityavaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] éternalisme, doctrine de la permanence de la réalité; elle s'oppose à la doctrine bd. du transitoire de toutes choses [<a href="#entry-k_sa_nikavaada" class="skt-link">kṣaṇikavāda</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nityavastu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nityavastu</i> [<a href="#entry-vastu_1" class="skt-link">vastu_1</a>] <a href="#entry-nityavastu" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] la réalité permanente, le <a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a> éternel.<br> <a href="#entry-nityamukta" class="skt-link">nityamukta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nityazas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nityaśas</i> [<a href="#entry--zas" class="skt-link">-śas</a>] adv. éternellement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niv_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवृ</span> <i>nivṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-v_r_1" class="skt-link">vṛ_1</a>] ca. <i>(nivārayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nivārita</i><i>)</i> retenir, refouler, écarter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niv_rt">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवृत्</span> <i>nivṛt</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-v_rt_1" class="skt-link">vṛt_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(nivartate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nivṛtta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>prati</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> retourner, revenir de ⟨abl.⟩ vers ⟨acc.⟩; revenir sur ses pas, s'en retourner, revenir en arrière, retourner chez soi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> revivre, renaître, redevenir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cesser, disparaître, se retirer; manquer, ne pas exister, ne pas se produire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niv_rtta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवृत्त</span> <i>nivṛtta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-niv_rt" class="skt-link">nivṛt</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niv_rtta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niv_rtta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niv_rttaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nivṛttā</i> revenu à ⟨acc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> disparu, en fuite; passé, révolu, cessé; couché (soleil) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> indifférent; qui a renoncé à ⟨abl. iic.⟩; débarrassé de, privé de ⟨abl.⟩; retiré du monde; désintéressé (acte).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niv_rttamaa_msa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nivṛttamāṃsa</i> [<a href="#entry-maa_msa" class="skt-link">māṃsa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niv_rttamaa_msa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niv_rttamaa_msa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niv_rttamaa_msaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nivṛttamāṃsā</i> qui a renoncé à la viande.<br> <a href="#entry-niv_rtta" class="skt-link">nivṛtta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niv_rtti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवृत्ति</span> <i>nivṛtti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-niv_rt" class="skt-link">nivṛt</a>] <a href="#entry-niv_rtti" class="skt-link">f.</a> cessation, disparition; abandon, abstinence; abstention de ⟨abl.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> repos, inactivité, contemplation; voie contemplative (opp. <a href="#entry-prav_rtti" class="skt-link">pravṛtti</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-zaivasiddhaanta" class="skt-link">śaivasiddhānta</a>] aspect du <a href="#entry-bindu" class="skt-link">bindu</a> associé à <a href="#entry-Usadaaziva" class="skt-link">Sadāśiva</a>-<a href="#entry-Usadyojaata" class="skt-link">Sadyojāta</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Nivṛtti «Arrêt de la pensée» personnifiant l'énergie de l'esprit ou le Mental au repos, épouse de <a href="#entry-Umanas" class="skt-link">Manas</a>, mère de <a href="#entry-Uviveka" class="skt-link">Viveka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivaaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवाह</span> <i>nivāha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nivah" class="skt-link">nivah</a>] <a href="#entry-nivaaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de mener vers le bas.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivaapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवाप</span> <i>nivāpa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nivap" class="skt-link">nivap</a>] <a href="#entry-nivaapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> champ ensemencé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. libation d'eau aux mânes <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. dispersion des cendres.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवार</span> <i>nivāra</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-niv_r" class="skt-link">nivṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nivaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nivaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nivaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nivārā</i> qui retient, qui refoule, qui maintient à distance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivaarita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवारित</span> <i>nivārita</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-niv_r" class="skt-link">nivṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nivaarita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nivaarita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nivaaritaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nivāritā</i> empêché, retenu, écarté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivaarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवार्य</span> <i>nivārya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-niv_r" class="skt-link">nivṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nivaarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nivaarya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nivaaryaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nivāryā</i> à empêcher, à écarter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivaasa_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवास</span> <i>nivāsa_1</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nivas_1" class="skt-link">nivas_1</a>] <a href="#entry-nivaasa_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait d'habiter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> demeure, résidence, lieu de séjour; halte nocturne, refuge.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivaasa_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवास</span> <i>nivāsa_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nivas_2" class="skt-link">nivas_2</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-nivaasa_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nivaasa_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nivaasaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nivāsā</i> vêtu de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivaasin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवासिन्</span> <i>nivāsin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nivas_1" class="skt-link">nivas_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nivaasin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nivaasin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nivaasinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nivāsinī</i> habitant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवात</span> <i>nivāta</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-vaata" class="skt-link">vāta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nivaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nivaata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nivaataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nivātā</i> abrité du vent, calme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivaatakavaca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nivātakavaca</i> [<a href="#entry-kavaca" class="skt-link">kavaca</a>] <a href="#entry-nivaatakavaca" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [«dont l'armure protège du vent»] catégorie de titants [<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>] combattant les dieux; la capitale des <i>nivātakavacās</i> est la riche <a href="#entry-Uma_nimatii" class="skt-link">Maṇimatī</a>, objet de la convoitise de <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a> qui les combattit pendant des siècles, avant que <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> ne les réconcilie.<br> <a href="#entry-nivaata" class="skt-link">nivāta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivah">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवह्</span> <i>nivah</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-vah_1" class="skt-link">vah_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nivahati)</i> conduire en bas; amener à ⟨dat. loc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> couler <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> transporter — ca. <i>(nivāhayati)</i> mettre en mouvement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवह</span> <i>nivaha</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nivah" class="skt-link">nivah</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nivaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nivaha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nivahaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nivahā</i> qui amène — <a href="#entry-nivaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> multitude, quantité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Nivaha, l'un des 7 Vents célestes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivap">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवप्</span> <i>nivap</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-vap_2" class="skt-link">vap_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nivapati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nivapta</i><i>)</i> renverser, répandre; disperser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> offrir une libation aux mânes; répandre les cendres.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivas_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवस्</span> <i>nivas_1</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-vas_1" class="skt-link">vas_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nivasati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nivasita</i><i>)</i> habiter, séjourner, vivre, choisir pour résidence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cesser d'habiter — ca. <i>(nivāsayati)</i> expulser (de ⟨abl.⟩); bannir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivas_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवस्</span> <i>nivas_2</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-vas_2" class="skt-link">vas_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. md. <i>(nivaste)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nivasita</i><i>)</i> s'habiller — ca. <i>(nivāsayati)</i> revêtir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivasita_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवसित</span> <i>nivasita_1</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nivas_1" class="skt-link">nivas_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nivasita_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nivasita_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nivasitaa_1" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nivasitā_1</i> expulsé, banni.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivasita_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवसित</span> <i>nivasita_2</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nivas_2" class="skt-link">nivas_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nivasita_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nivasita_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nivasitaa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nivasitā_2</i> vêtu de ⟨i.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवश</span> <i>nivaśa</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-vaza" class="skt-link">vaśa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nivaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nivaza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nivazaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nivaśā</i> faible, sans volonté, qui n'est pas maître de soi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivedana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवेदन</span> <i>nivedana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nivid_1" class="skt-link">nivid_1</a>] <a href="#entry-nivedana" class="skt-link">n.</a> proclamation, publication; information, communication <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> offre; dédicace.<br> <i>ātmanivedana</i> fait de se présenter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivedin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवेदिन्</span> <i>nivedin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nivid_1" class="skt-link">nivid_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nivedin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nivedin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nivedinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nivedinī</i> qui sait, informé de ⟨iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui informe, qui communique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivedita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवेदित</span> <i>nivedita</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-nivid_1" class="skt-link">nivid_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nivedita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nivedita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niveditaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niveditā</i> divulgé, annoncé; publié, communiqué <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> présenté; offert.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivedya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवेद्य</span> <i>nivedya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-nivid_1" class="skt-link">nivid_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nivedya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nivedya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nivedyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nivedyā</i> qui doit être annoncé; qui doit être offert — <a href="#entry-nivedya" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. nourriture offerte à une idole.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niveza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवेश</span> <i>niveśa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-niviz" class="skt-link">niviś</a>] <a href="#entry-niveza" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait d'entrer; installation; campement, halte; habitation, résidence.<br> <i>niveśaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> s'installer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivezana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवेशन</span> <i>niveśana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-niviz" class="skt-link">niviś</a>] <a href="#entry-nivezana" class="skt-link">n.</a> installation; campement; abri, cachette, repaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> habitation, maison, résidence; pièce d'habitation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> peuplement (d'un pays).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivezita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवेशित</span> <i>niveśita</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-niviz" class="skt-link">niviś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nivezita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nivezita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nivezitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niveśitā</i> introduit, placé dans ou sur, installé; situé à ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivid_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निविद्</span> <i>nivid_1</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-vid_1" class="skt-link">vid_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(nivetti)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nivedati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nividita</i><i>)</i> dire, annoncer — ca. <i>(nivedayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nivedita</i><i>)</i> inf. <i>(nivedayitum)</i> dire, annoncer, faire savoir, communiquer; informer ⟨dat.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> offrir, présenter.<br> <i>ātmānaṃ</i> <i>nivid</i> se présenter, s'offrir.<br> <i>doṣaṃ</i> <i>nivid</i> imputer la faute à ⟨dat.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivid_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निविद्</span> <i>nivid_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nivid_1" class="skt-link">nivid_1</a>] <a href="#entry-nivid_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> instruction; information <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. forme d'invocation liturgique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niviita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवीत</span> <i>nivīta</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-viita_2" class="skt-link">vīta_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niviita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niviita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niviitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nivītā</i> porté autour du cou — <a href="#entry-niviita" class="skt-link">n.</a> port en collier, not. du cordon sacré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ce cordon; syn. <a href="#entry-upaviita" class="skt-link">upavīta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niviz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निविश्</span> <i>niviś</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-viz_1" class="skt-link">viś_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. md. <i>(niviśate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niviṣṭa</i><i>)</i> ppr. ps. <i>(niviśamāna)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>abhi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> faire halte, camper; s'installer; fonder un foyer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être concentré, être attentif à — ca. <i>(niviśyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niveśita</i><i>)</i> déposer, laisser en un lieu; amener, apporter, transférer; confier à ⟨loc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> introduire, installer; faire asseoir ou coucher.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivizamaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निविशमान</span> <i>niviśamāna</i> [ppr. ps. <a href="#entry-niviz" class="skt-link">niviś</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nivizamaana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nivizamaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nivizamaanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niviśamānā</i> qui se repose.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nivyadh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निव्यध्</span> <i>nivyadh</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-vyadh" class="skt-link">vyadh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(nividhyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nividdha</i><i>)</i> frapper, atteindre (par une flèche); percer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyaama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नियाम</span> <i>niyāma</i> [act. <a href="#entry-niyam" class="skt-link">niyam</a>] <a href="#entry-niyaama" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-niyama" class="skt-link">niyama</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> marin, navigateur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyaamaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नियामक</span> <i>niyāmaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-niyam" class="skt-link">niyam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niyaamaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niyaamaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niyaamikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niyāmikā</i> qui restreint, qui contrôle, qui réprime <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> restrictif, limitant, définissant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] qui délimite; syn. <a href="#entry-avacchedaka" class="skt-link">avacchedaka</a> — <a href="#entry-niyaamaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> guide, dirigeant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> conducteur de char; navigateur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] principe directeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नियम्</span> <i>niyam</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-yam" class="skt-link">yam</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(niyacchati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niyata</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> retenir, réprimer, restreindre; régir, discipliner, régler, fixer — ca. <i>(niyamayati)</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नियम</span> <i>niyama</i> [act. <a href="#entry-niyam" class="skt-link">niyam</a>] <a href="#entry-niyama" class="skt-link">m.</a> répression, restriction, limitation, abstinence; retenue, réserve; discipline, observance, austérité; pratique résultant d'un vœu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. la discipline morale, deuxième étape du <a href="#entry-raajayoga" class="skt-link">rājayoga</a>, consistant en la pratique d'exercices spirituels pour acquérir 5 vertus: la pureté [<a href="#entry-zauca" class="skt-link">śauca</a>], la modération (contentement de peu) [<a href="#entry-santo_sa" class="skt-link">saṃtoṣa</a>], la force d'âme (acquise par l'ascèse) [<a href="#entry-tapas" class="skt-link">tapas</a>], la connaissance (acquise par la lecture des textes sacrés) [<a href="#entry-svaadhyaaya" class="skt-link">svādhyāya</a>], et la foi (acquise par la méditation) [<a href="#entry-iizvarapra_nidhaana" class="skt-link">īśvarapraṇidhāna</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-a_s_taafgayoga" class="skt-link">aṣṭāṅgayoga</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>niyamās</i> règles; réglement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyamavidhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niyamavidhi</i> [<a href="#entry-vidhi" class="skt-link">vidhi</a>] <a href="#entry-niyamavidhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsā</a>] injonction rituelle restrictive.<br> <a href="#entry-niyama" class="skt-link">niyama</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyamita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नियमित</span> <i>niyamita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-niyam" class="skt-link">niyam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niyamita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niyamita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niyamitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niyamitā</i> contrôlé, réprimé; limité, confiné; attaché à ⟨iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> arrêté, supprimé; déterminé, fixé; destiné à ⟨inf.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gouverné, guidé; régulé, prescrit; stipulé; convenu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyamya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नियम्य</span> <i>niyamya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-niyam" class="skt-link">niyam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niyamya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niyamya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niyamyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niyamyā</i> à contrôler, à réprimer; que l'on doit limiter; à définir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyant_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नियन्तृ</span> <i>niyantṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-niyam" class="skt-link">niyam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niyant_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niyant_r" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niyantrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niyantrī</i> qui régit, qui réprime, qui restreint; gouverneur — <a href="#entry-niyant_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] un régent d'une faculté [<a href="#entry-indriya" class="skt-link">indriya</a>]; les 5 régents des sens [<a href="#entry-buddhiindriya" class="skt-link">buddhīndriya</a>] sont les <a href="#entry-Udizas" class="skt-link">Diśas</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvaayu" class="skt-link">Vāyu</a>, <a href="#entry-Uaaditya" class="skt-link">Āditya</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a> et la paire des <a href="#entry-Uazvinau" class="skt-link">Aśvinau</a>; les 5 régents des facultés d'action [<a href="#entry-karmendriya" class="skt-link">karmendriya</a>] sont <a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a>, <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>, <a href="#entry-Uprajaapati" class="skt-link">Prajāpati</a> et <a href="#entry-Umitra" class="skt-link">Mitra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नियन्त्र</span> <i>niyantra</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-yantra" class="skt-link">yantra</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(niyantrayati)</i> restreindre, réprimer; contrôler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyantra_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नियन्त्रण</span> <i>niyantraṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-niyantra" class="skt-link">niyantra</a>] <a href="#entry-niyantra_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> restriction, limitation, contrainte <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. restriction à un sens particulier; définition.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyantrita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नियन्त्रित</span> <i>niyantrita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-niyantra" class="skt-link">niyantra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niyantrita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niyantrita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niyantritaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niyantritā</i> restreint, contrôlé; attaché, confiné; mis en berges (rivière) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. restreint à un sens particulier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gouverné par, dépendant de ⟨i. iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नियत</span> <i>niyata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-niyam" class="skt-link">niyam</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niyata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niyata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niyataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niyatā</i> retenu, réprimé, empêché; limité, discipliné, maîtrisé, contrôlé; tempéré, modéré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fixé, déterminé, invariable, permanent, usuel; certain, établi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyataapti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niyatāpti</i> [<a href="#entry-aapti" class="skt-link">āpti</a>] <a href="#entry-niyataapti" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. [théâtre] fait pour le héros de surmonter un obstacle, laissant espérer l'obtention du but; ce ressort dramatique est l'un des 5 étapes de déroulement [<a href="#entry-kaaryaavasthaa" class="skt-link">kāryāvasthā</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyataatman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niyatātman</i> [<a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">ātman</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niyataatman" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niyataatman" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niyataatmanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niyatātmanī</i> [«qui contrôle son caractère»] maître de soi-même, discipliné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyataa_njali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niyatāñjali</i> [<a href="#entry-a_njali" class="skt-link">añjali</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niyataa_njali" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niyataa_njali" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niyataa_njali" class="skt-link">f.</a> [«qui observe l'<a href="#entry-a_njali" class="skt-link">añjali</a>»] qui salue en joignant les mains.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyatabhojana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niyatabhojana</i> [<a href="#entry-bhojana" class="skt-link">bhojana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niyatabhojana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niyatabhojana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niyatabhojanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niyatabhojanā</i> qui mange avec modération — <a href="#entry-niyatabhojana" class="skt-link">n.</a> diète.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyatamaanasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niyatamānasa</i> [<a href="#entry-maanasa" class="skt-link">mānasa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niyatamaanasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niyatamaanasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niyatamaanasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niyatamānasī</i> qui contrôle ses pensées — <a href="#entry-niyatamaanasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> répression du mental.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyatamaithuna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niyatamaithuna</i> [<a href="#entry-maithuna" class="skt-link">maithuna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niyatamaithuna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niyatamaithuna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niyatamaithunaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niyatamaithunā</i> qui s'abstient de rapports charnels — <a href="#entry-niyatamaithuna" class="skt-link">n.</a> abstinence sexuelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyatavrata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niyatavrata</i> [<a href="#entry-vrata" class="skt-link">vrata</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niyatavrata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niyatavrata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niyatavrataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niyatavratā</i> qui observe ses vœux; pieux — <a href="#entry-niyatavrata" class="skt-link">n.</a> piété; respect des vœux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyatayukta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niyatayukta</i> [<a href="#entry-yukta" class="skt-link">yukta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niyatayukta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niyatayukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niyatayuktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niyatayuktā</i> qui contrôle la méditation — <a href="#entry-niyatayukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. <a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a> continu ou contrôlé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyatendriya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niyatendriya</i> [<a href="#entry-indriya" class="skt-link">indriya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niyatendriya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niyatendriya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niyatendriyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niyatendriyā</i> qui contrôle ses sens — <a href="#entry-niyatendriya" class="skt-link">n.</a> contrôle des sens.<br> <a href="#entry-niyata" class="skt-link">niyata</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नियति</span> <i>niyati</i> [act. <a href="#entry-niyam" class="skt-link">niyam</a>] <a href="#entry-niyati" class="skt-link">f.</a> nécessité; fatalité, destin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyativaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niyativāda</i> [<a href="#entry-vaada" class="skt-link">vāda</a>] <a href="#entry-niyativaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. doctrine de prédestination ou fatalisme, enseignée not. par <a href="#entry-Ugozaala" class="skt-link">Gośāla</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-niyati" class="skt-link">niyati</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नियोग</span> <i>niyoga</i> [act. <a href="#entry-niyuj" class="skt-link">niyuj</a>] <a href="#entry-niyoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> assignation, emploi, charge, instruction; ordre, mission <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. devoir d'une veuve d'épouser le frère de son mari.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyogin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नियोगिन्</span> <i>niyogin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-niyuj" class="skt-link">niyuj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niyogin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niyogin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niyoginii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niyoginī</i> nommé, assigné; chargé de mission — <a href="#entry-niyogin" class="skt-link">m.</a> fonctionnaire, officiel; ministre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyojya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नियोज्य</span> <i>niyojya</i> [ca. pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-niyuj" class="skt-link">niyuj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niyojya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niyojya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niyojyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niyojyā</i> qui doit être attaché, à fixer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à employer, à utiliser; à munir de ⟨i.⟩ — ifc. qui doit être employé à; qui mérite la charge de; qui doit être ordonné à — <a href="#entry-niyojya" class="skt-link">m.</a> fonctionnaire, officiel; employé, serviteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नियु</span> <i>niyu</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-yu_1" class="skt-link">yu_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(niyauti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niyuta</i><i>)</i> attacher, réunir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyuj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नियुज्</span> <i>niyuj</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-yuj_1" class="skt-link">yuj_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(niyunakti)</i> pr. md. <i>(niyuṅkte)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niyukta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> ordonner, assigner, désigner, charger de, confier qqc. ⟨acc.⟩ à qqn. ⟨loc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mettre sous le joug, subjuguer; soumettre; discipliner, régir, dompter — ca. <i>(niyojayati)</i> attacher à; charger qqn. ⟨acc.⟩ de ⟨loc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> disposer (un piège), poser (un collet).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyukta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नियुक्त</span> <i>niyukta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-niyuj" class="skt-link">niyuj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niyukta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niyukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niyuktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niyuktā</i> attaché, attelé; fixé; assigné à ⟨loc.⟩; prescrit, ordonné <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> chargé de ⟨dat. inf. iic.⟩; employé, préposé — <a href="#entry-niyukta" class="skt-link">m.</a> fonctionnaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niyuta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नियुत</span> <i>niyuta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-niyu" class="skt-link">niyu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-niyuta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-niyuta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niyutaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niyutā</i> fixé, attaché — <a href="#entry-niyuta" class="skt-link">n.</a> cent mille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-niz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निश्</span> <i>niś</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-nakta" class="skt-link">nakta</a>] <a href="#entry-niz" class="skt-link">f.</a> nuit.<br> <i>niśi</i> adv. de nuit.<br> <i>aharniśam</i> adv. jour et nuit.<br> <i>niśi</i> <i>niśi</i> adv. chaque nuit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizaa_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निशा</span> <i>niśā_1</i> [<a href="#entry-niz" class="skt-link">niś</a>] <a href="#entry-nizaa_1" class="skt-link">f.</a> nuit; songe — <a href="#entry-niza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <i>niśa</i> ifc. id. — loc. <i>niśāyām</i> adv. dans la nuit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizaa_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निशा</span> <i>niśā_2</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-zaa" class="skt-link">śā</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(niśyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niśita</i>, <i>niśāta</i><i>)</i> aiguiser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizaacara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niśācara</i> [<a href="#entry-cara" class="skt-link">cara</a>] <a href="#entry-nizaacara" class="skt-link">m.</a> fantôme, spectre; monstre nocturne, rôdeur de nuit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizaakara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niśākara</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] <a href="#entry-nizaakara" class="skt-link">m.</a> la Lune [«qui fait la nuit»]; cf. <a href="#entry-divaakara" class="skt-link">divākara</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] Niśākara; son ermitage était dans les monts <a href="#entry-Uvindhya" class="skt-link">Vindhya</a>; il recueillit <a href="#entry-Usampaati" class="skt-link">Sampāti</a>, qui s'était brûlé les ailes pour protéger <a href="#entry-Uja_taayu" class="skt-link">Jaṭāyu</a> de l'ardeur du Soleil.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizaam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निशाम्</span> <i>niśām</i> cf. <a href="#entry-nizam" class="skt-link">niśam</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizaamya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निशाम्य</span> <i>niśāmya</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-nizam" class="skt-link">niśam</a>] ind. ayant appris, ayant entendu dire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निशान</span> <i>niśāna</i> [<a href="#entry-nizaa_2" class="skt-link">niśā_2</a>] <a href="#entry-nizaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait d'aiguiser; stimulation; observation, perception.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizaapraa_nezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niśāprāṇeśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-praa_nezvara" class="skt-link">prāṇeśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-nizaapraa_nezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lune [«époux de la nuit»].<br> <a href="#entry-nizaa_1" class="skt-link">niśā_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निशात</span> <i>niśāta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nizaa_2" class="skt-link">niśā_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nizaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nizaata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nizaataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niśātā</i> aiguisé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निशम्</span> <i>niśam</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-zam_2" class="skt-link">śam_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(niśamayati)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>niśamya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>anu</i><i>)</i> percevoir, entendre; apprendre, entendre dire — ca. <i>(niśāmayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niśāmita</i><i>)</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizamana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निशमन</span> <i>niśamana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nizam" class="skt-link">niśam</a>] <a href="#entry-nizamana" class="skt-link">n.</a> perception; écoute.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizcala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निश्चल</span> <i>niścala</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-cala" class="skt-link">cala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nizcala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nizcala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nizcalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niścalā</i> immobile, immuable, inébranlable; impérissable.
