  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizcaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निश्चय</span> <i>niścaya</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nizci" class="skt-link">niści</a>] <a href="#entry-nizcaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> investigation; opinion arrêtée, conviction; certitude <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> résolution, décision; arrêt, sentence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] certitude, tautologie.<br> <i>niścayena</i> adv. certainement.<br> <i>iti</i> <i>niścaya</i> voici la décision.<br> <i>niścayaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> arrêter son opinion, prendre une décision.<br> <i>niścayaḥ</i> <i>nāsti</i> ça n'est pas sûr.<br> <i>uttiṣṭha</i> <i>kaunteya</i> <i>yuddhāya</i> <i>kṛtaniścayaḥ</i> [BhG.] Lève toi, fils de <a href="#entry-Ukunti" class="skt-link">Kunti</a>, fermement résolu à combattre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizchidra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निश्छिद्र</span> <i>niśchidra</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-chidra" class="skt-link">chidra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nizchidra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nizchidra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nizchidraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niśchidrā</i> sans déchirure, sans lacune; sans trou, sans défaut.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizci">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निश्चि</span> <i>niści</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-ci" class="skt-link">ci</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] pr. <i>(niścinoti)</i> pr. md. <i>(niścinute)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niścita</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>niścitya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> vérifier, réfléchir à, se convaincre de; considérer comme certain <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> arrêter son opinion, déterminer, décider, conclure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizcinta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निश्चिन्त</span> <i>niścinta</i> [<a href="#entry-nis" class="skt-link">nis</a>-<a href="#entry-cintaa" class="skt-link">cintā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nizcinta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nizcinta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nizcintaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niścintā</i> sans soucis <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> insouciant, indifférent; qui ne pense pas.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizcintataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niścintatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-nizcintataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> indifférence, insouciance.<br> <a href="#entry-nizcinta" class="skt-link">niścinta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizcita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निश्चित</span> <i>niścita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nizci" class="skt-link">niści</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nizcita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nizcita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nizcitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niścitā</i> convaincu, déterminé; résolu, décidé à ⟨dat. loc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> établi, certain — <a href="#entry-nizcita" class="skt-link">n.</a> certitude, décision.<br> <i>niścitam</i> adv. certainement, sûrement, décidément, sans aucun doute.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizcitya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निश्चित्य</span> <i>niścitya</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-nizci" class="skt-link">niści</a>] ind. s'étant assuré, ayant formé son opinion; ayant la conviction ou la certitude (que qqn. ⟨acc.⟩, de qqc. ⟨i.⟩); ayant pris sa décision.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निशित</span> <i>niśita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nizaa_2" class="skt-link">niśā_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nizita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nizita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nizitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>niśitā</i> aiguisé, tranchant; aigu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> excité, avide de ⟨loc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizumbh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निशुम्भ्</span> <i>niśumbh</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-zumbh" class="skt-link">śumbh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(niśumbhati)</i> écraser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizumbha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निशुम्भ</span> <i>niśumbha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nizumbh" class="skt-link">niśumbh</a>] <a href="#entry-nizumbha" class="skt-link">m.</a> destruction, meurtre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Udeviimaahaatmya" class="skt-link">Devīmāhātmya</a>] np. de l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> Niśumbha «Meurtrier», fils de <a href="#entry-Ugave_s_thin" class="skt-link">Gaveṣṭhin</a>, tué avec son frère <a href="#entry-Uzumbha" class="skt-link">Śumbha</a> par <a href="#entry-Ukaalii" class="skt-link">Kālī</a> surgissant du front de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizvaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निश्वास</span> <i>niśvāsa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nizvas" class="skt-link">niśvas</a>] <a href="#entry-nizvaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> inspiration; respiration; soupir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizvaasatattvasa_mhitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>niśvāsatattvasaṃhitā</i> [<a href="#entry-tattva" class="skt-link">tattva</a>-<a href="#entry-sa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">saṃhitā</a>] <a href="#entry-nizvaasatattvasa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. du Niśvāsatattvasaṃhitā, tantra ancien du <a href="#entry-zaiva" class="skt-link">śaiva</a> <a href="#entry-siddhaanta" class="skt-link">siddhānta</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-nizvaasa" class="skt-link">niśvāsa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizvas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निश्वस्</span> <i>niśvas</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-zvas_1" class="skt-link">śvas_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(niśvasiti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niśvasita</i><i>)</i> inspirer; siffler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nizvasita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निश्वसित</span> <i>niśvasita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nizvas" class="skt-link">niśvas</a>] <a href="#entry-nizvasita" class="skt-link">n.</a> inspiration; respiration.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-no">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नो</span> <i>no</i> [<a href="#entry-na" class="skt-link">na</a>-<a href="#entry-u" class="skt-link">u</a>] part. (<a href="#entry-prag_rhya" class="skt-link">pragṛhya</a>) et non.<br> <i>no</i> <i>ced</i> sinon, afin que ne ... pas.<br> <i>no</i> <i>vā</i> ou non.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nod">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नोद्</span> <i>nod</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-nud" class="skt-link">nud</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(nodayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nodita</i><i>)</i> inciter, exciter, stimuler — ps. <i>(nodyate)</i> être incité, être stimulé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nodita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नोदित</span> <i>nodita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nod" class="skt-link">nod</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nodita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nodita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-noditaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>noditā</i> incité, stimulé, excité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nu_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">नु</span> <i>nu_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(nauti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nuta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pra</i><i>)</i> louer, acclamer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nu_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नु</span> <i>nu_2</i> part. interr. d'insistance de phrases <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> n'est-ce-pas? certes, sans doute, certainement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>nunc</i>; all. nun; ang. now.<br> <i>kathaṃ</i> <i>nu</i> comment donc?<br> <i>kiṃ</i> <i>nu</i> quoi donc? pourquoi donc?<br> <i>na</i> <i>nu</i> n'est ce pas? n'est-il pas vrai que? certainement.<br> <i>nu</i> <i>...</i> <i>nu</i> ou ... ou.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nud">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">नुद्</span> <i>nud</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(nudati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nutta</i>, <i>nunna</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>apa</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> pousser, exciter, hâter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> repousser, rejeter — ca. <i>(nodayati)</i> exciter, stimuler — dés. <i>(nunutsati)</i> vouloir rejeter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nud_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नुद्</span> <i>nud_1</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nud" class="skt-link">nud</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-nud_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nud_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nud_1" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui repousse, qui supprime; qui remédie à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nunna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नुन्न</span> <i>nunna</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nud" class="skt-link">nud</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nunna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nunna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nunnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nunnā</i> cf. <a href="#entry-nutta" class="skt-link">nutta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nunuts">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नुनुत्स्</span> <i>nunuts</i> [dés. <a href="#entry-nud" class="skt-link">nud</a>] v. pr. <i>(nunutsati)</i> vouloir rejeter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nunutsu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नुनुत्सु</span> <i>nunutsu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-nunuts" class="skt-link">nunuts</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nunutsu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nunutsu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nunutsuu" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nunutsū</i> qui désire inciter ou rejeter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nuti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नुति</span> <i>nuti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nu_1" class="skt-link">nu_1</a>] <a href="#entry-nuti" class="skt-link">f.</a> éloge, hommage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nutta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नुत्त</span> <i>nutta</i> var. <i>nunna</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-nud" class="skt-link">nud</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nutta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nutta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nuttaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nuttā</i> poussé, envoyé, chassé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nuunam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नूनम्</span> <i>nūnam</i> [<a href="#entry-nu_2" class="skt-link">nu_2</a>] adv. vraisemblablement, sûrement; vraiment, en effet <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> maintenant, désormais.<br> <i>kadā</i> <i>nūnam</i> quand donc?<br> <i>kva</i> <i>nūnam</i> où donc?
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nuupura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नूपुर</span> <i>nūpura</i> <a href="#entry-nuupura" class="skt-link">m.</a> anneau de chevilles.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nuutana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">नूतन</span> <i>nūtana</i> a. <a href="#entry-nuutana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nuutana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nuutanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nūtanā</i> neuf, nouveau; récent; frais, jeune — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(nūtanayati)</i> renouveler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nuutanatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nūtanatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-nuutanatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> nouveauté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nuutanavar_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nūtanavarṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-var_sa" class="skt-link">varṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-nuutanavar_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> nouvel an.<br> <a href="#entry-nuutana" class="skt-link">nūtana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">न्यास</span> <i>nyāsa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nyas" class="skt-link">nyas</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de poser, de placer; fait d'écrire, de peindre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> placement, mise en dépôt; fait de confier; soumission <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> abandon de, renoncement à ⟨g.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> texte écrit, littéral, verbatim <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. position, formulation; teneur d'un <a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. commentaire de la grammaire de Bénarès [<a href="#entry-Ukaazikaa" class="skt-link">Kāśikā</a>]; not. le Nyāsa de <a href="#entry-Ujinendrabuddhi" class="skt-link">Jinendrabuddhi</a>, aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Ukaazikaavivara_napa_njikaa" class="skt-link">Kāśikāvivaraṇapañjikā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [tantr.] imposition rituelle des doigts sur des parties du corps, associée à un <a href="#entry-biijamantra" class="skt-link">bījamantra</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-afganyaasa" class="skt-link">aṅganyāsa</a>, <a href="#entry-karanyaasa" class="skt-link">karanyāsa</a>, <a href="#entry-h_rdayanyaasa" class="skt-link">hṛdayanyāsa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaasavaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāsavāda</i> [<a href="#entry-vaada" class="skt-link">vāda</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaasavaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-nik_sepavaada" class="skt-link">nikṣepavāda</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-nyaasa" class="skt-link">nyāsa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">न्याय</span> <i>nyāya</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nii_3" class="skt-link">nī_3</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«nature originelle»] méthode, système, règle; modèle; axiome, loi; maxime, exemple <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> procès; jugement; justice <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. dialectique; l'un des 6 points de vue [<a href="#entry-_sa_ddarzana" class="skt-link">ṣaḍdarśana</a>] de la tradition philosophique orthodoxe, fondé par <a href="#entry-Uak_sapaada" class="skt-link">Akṣapāda</a> <a href="#entry-Ugautama" class="skt-link">Gautama</a>, et basé sur l'analyse logique, le raisonnement et la rhétorique; la nouvelle logique [<a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a>] développe la sémiotique, et renouvelle la logique traditionnelle [<a href="#entry-praaciinanyaaya" class="skt-link">prācīnanyāya</a>].<br> <i>eṣo</i> <i>'tra</i> <i>nyāyaḥ</i> c'est ce qui est approprié ici.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayaalaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyālaya</i> [<a href="#entry-aalaya" class="skt-link">ālaya</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayaalaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> tribunal.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayaam_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyāmṛta</i> [<a href="#entry-am_rta" class="skt-link">amṛta</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayaam_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Nyāyāmṛta «Nectar de logique», œuvre <a href="#entry-dvaita" class="skt-link">dvaita</a> de <a href="#entry-Uvyaasatiirtha" class="skt-link">Vyāsatīrtha</a>; il y attaque la doctrine <a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a> comme non fondée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayaavali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyāvali</i> [<a href="#entry-aavali" class="skt-link">āvali</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayaavali" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Nyāyāvali, recueil mod. de maximes et proverbes.<br> <a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayaavataara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyāvatāra</i> [<a href="#entry-avataara" class="skt-link">avatāra</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayaavataara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Nyāyāvatāra, traité <a href="#entry-jaina" class="skt-link">jaina</a> d'épistémologie de Siddhasena Mahāmati (8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayabhaa_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyabhāṣya</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_sya" class="skt-link">bhāṣya</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Nyāyabhāṣya, commentaire de <a href="#entry-Uvaatsyaayana_1" class="skt-link">Vātsyāyana_1</a> sur le <a href="#entry-Unyaayasuutra" class="skt-link">Nyāyasūtra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayabhuu_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyabhūṣaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuu_sa_na" class="skt-link">bhūṣaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayabhuu_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Nyāyabhūṣaṇa «Commentaire sur la logique», dû à <a href="#entry-Ubhaasarvaj_na" class="skt-link">Bhāsarvajña</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayabindu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyabindu</i> [<a href="#entry-bindu" class="skt-link">bindu</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayabindu" class="skt-link">n.</a> bd. np. du Nyāyabindu «Perle de logique», ouvrage de logique dû au bd. <a href="#entry-Udharmakiirti" class="skt-link">Dharmakīrti</a>; il développe la théorie selon laquelle les noms et les relations sont imposés par l'intellect, alors que la perception révèle la vraie nature des objets.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayabodhinii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyabodhinī</i> [<a href="#entry-bodhinii" class="skt-link">bodhinī</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayabodhinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Nyāyabodhinī, commentaire du <a href="#entry-Utarkasafgraha" class="skt-link">Tarkasaṃgraha</a> de <a href="#entry-Ugovardhana_2" class="skt-link">Govardhana_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayadarzana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyadarśana</i> [<a href="#entry-darzana" class="skt-link">darśana</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayadarzana" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. np. du Nyāyadarśana, point de vue philosophique de la logique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayakalikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyakalikā</i> [<a href="#entry-kalikaa" class="skt-link">kalikā</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayakalikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Nyāyakalikā, œuvre de <a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a> due à <a href="#entry-Ujayanta_1" class="skt-link">Jayanta_1</a> <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a>, commentaire du <a href="#entry-Unyaayasaara" class="skt-link">Nyāyasāra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayakandalii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyakandalī</i> [<a href="#entry-kandalii" class="skt-link">kandalī</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayakandalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Nyāyakandalī «Fleur de la logique», ouvrage philosophique d'orientation théiste des systèmes <a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a> et <a href="#entry-vaize_sika" class="skt-link">vaiśeṣika</a>, dû à <a href="#entry-Uzriidhara_1" class="skt-link">Śrīdhara_1</a> <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a>; c'est un commentaire sur le <a href="#entry-Upadaarthadharmasafgraha" class="skt-link">Padārthadharmasaṃgraha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayakoza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyakośa</i> [<a href="#entry-koza" class="skt-link">kośa</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayakoza" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Nyāyakośa, dictionnaire des termes techniques du <a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Ubhiimaacaarya" class="skt-link">Bhīmācārya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayakusumaa_njali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyakusumāñjali</i> [<a href="#entry-kusumaa_njali" class="skt-link">kusumāñjali</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayakusumaa_njali" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a>] np. du Nyāyakusumāñjali «Offrande de logique», ouvrage d'<a href="#entry-Uudayanaacaarya" class="skt-link">Udayanācārya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayaliilaavatii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyalīlāvatī</i> [<a href="#entry-liilaavatii" class="skt-link">līlāvatī</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayaliilaavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Nyāyalīlāvatī, œuvre de logique de <a href="#entry-Uvallabha_2" class="skt-link">Vallabha_2</a>; <a href="#entry-Umathuraanaatha" class="skt-link">Mathurānātha</a> en fit un commentaire [<a href="#entry-rahasya" class="skt-link">rahasya</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayamakaranda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyamakaranda</i> [<a href="#entry-makaranda" class="skt-link">makaranda</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayamakaranda" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Nyāyamakaranda «Nectar de logique», traité de philosophie <a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a> d'<a href="#entry-Uaanandabodha" class="skt-link">Ānandabodha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayama_njarii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyamañjarī</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_njarii" class="skt-link">mañjarī</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayama_njarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> gram. np. de la Nyāyamañjarī «Gerbe de logique», œuvre de sémantique de <a href="#entry-Ujayanta_1" class="skt-link">Jayanta_1</a> <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayanibandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyanibandha</i> [<a href="#entry-nibandha" class="skt-link">nibandha</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayanibandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. cf. <a href="#entry-Unyaayavaarttikataatparyaparizuddhi" class="skt-link">Nyāyavārttikatātparyapariśuddhi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayaparizuddhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyapariśuddhi</i> [<a href="#entry-parizuddhi" class="skt-link">pariśuddhi</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayaparizuddhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Nyāyapariśuddhi d'<a href="#entry-Uudayanaacaarya" class="skt-link">Udayanācārya</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Unyaayavaarttikataatparyaparizuddhi" class="skt-link">Nyāyavārttikatātparyapariśuddhi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayapraveza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyapraveśa</i> [<a href="#entry-praveza" class="skt-link">praveśa</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayapraveza" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Nyāyapraveśa «Introduction à la logique», de <a href="#entry-Uzafkarasvaamii" class="skt-link">Śaṅkarasvāmī</a>; il est qqf. attribué à <a href="#entry-Udifnaaga" class="skt-link">Diṅnāga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayasaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyasāra</i> lit. np. du Nyāyasāra, court texte de <a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Ubhaasarvaj_na" class="skt-link">Bhāsarvajña</a>; la <a href="#entry-Unyaayakalikaa" class="skt-link">Nyāyakalikā</a> en est un commentaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayasiddha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyasiddha</i> [<a href="#entry-siddha_1" class="skt-link">siddha_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nyaayasiddha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nyaayasiddha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nyaayasiddhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nyāyasiddhā</i> phil. logique, de bon sens <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. se dit not. de méta-règles [<a href="#entry-paribhaa_saa" class="skt-link">paribhāṣā</a>] évidentes; syn. <a href="#entry-yuktisiddha" class="skt-link">yuktisiddha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayasiddhaanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyasiddhānta</i> [<a href="#entry-siddhaanta" class="skt-link">siddhānta</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayasiddhaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. enseignement de la logique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayasiddhaantadiipa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyasiddhāntadīpa</i> [<a href="#entry-diipa" class="skt-link">dīpa</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayasiddhaantadiipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du traité de logique Nyāyasiddhāntadīpa de <a href="#entry-Uzazadhara" class="skt-link">Śaśadhara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayasiddhaantajana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyasiddhāntajana</i> [<a href="#entry-jana" class="skt-link">jana</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayasiddhaantajana" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Nyāyasiddhāntajana de <a href="#entry-Uvedaantadezika" class="skt-link">Vedāntadeśika</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayasiddhaantamuktaavalii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyasiddhāntamuktāvalī</i> [<a href="#entry-muktaavalii" class="skt-link">muktāvalī</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayasiddhaantamuktaavalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Nyāyasiddhāntamuktāvalī ou <a href="#entry-Umuktaavalii" class="skt-link">Muktāvalī</a>, œuvre de <a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a> due à <a href="#entry-Uvizvanaatha_4" class="skt-link">Viśvanātha_4</a> <a href="#entry-Upa_ncaanana" class="skt-link">Pañcānana</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-nyaayasiddhaanta" class="skt-link">nyāyasiddhānta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayasudhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyasudhā</i> [<a href="#entry-sudhaa" class="skt-link">sudhā</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayasudhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Nyāyasudhā «Nectar de la logique», commentaire exhaustif [<a href="#entry-_tiikaa" class="skt-link">ṭīkā</a>] de l'<a href="#entry-Uanuvyaakhyaana" class="skt-link">Anuvyākhyāna</a> de <a href="#entry-Umadhva" class="skt-link">Madhva</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Ujayatiirtha" class="skt-link">Jayatīrtha</a>, qui en reçut le titre de <a href="#entry-U_tiikaacaarya" class="skt-link">Ṭīkācārya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayasuutra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyasūtra</i> [<a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayasuutra" class="skt-link">n.</a> pl. <i>nyāyasūtrāṇi</i> lit. np. du Nyāyasūtra «Aphorismes de logique», compilé entre le 1⟨sup⟩er⟨/sup⟩ siècle ant. et le 2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; la tradition attribue son origine à <a href="#entry-Ugotama_1" class="skt-link">Gotama_1</a>; ses principaux commentaires sont le <a href="#entry-Unyaayabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Nyāyabhāṣya</a> de <a href="#entry-Uvaatsyaayana_1" class="skt-link">Vātsyāyana_1</a> et le <a href="#entry-Unyaayavaarttika" class="skt-link">Nyāyavārttika</a> d'<a href="#entry-Uuddyotakara" class="skt-link">Uddyotakara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayasuutrabhaa_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyasūtrabhāṣya</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_sya" class="skt-link">bhāṣya</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayasuutrabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Nyāyasūtrabhāṣya; cf. <a href="#entry-Unyaayabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Nyāyabhāṣya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-nyaayasuutra" class="skt-link">nyāyasūtra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayavaarttika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyavārttika</i> [<a href="#entry-vaarttika" class="skt-link">vārttika</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayavaarttika" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Nyāyavārttika, œuvre du logicien <a href="#entry-Uuddyotakara" class="skt-link">Uddyotakara</a>; il commente le <a href="#entry-Unyaayasuutra" class="skt-link">Nyāyasūtra</a> et justifie le <a href="#entry-Unyaayabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Nyāyabhāṣya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayavaarttikataatparya_tiikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyavārttikatātparyaṭīkā</i> [<a href="#entry-taatparya" class="skt-link">tātparya</a>-<a href="#entry-_tiikaa" class="skt-link">ṭīkā</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayavaarttikataatparya_tiikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Nyāyavārttikatātparyaṭīkā de <a href="#entry-Uvaacaspati_1" class="skt-link">Vācaspati_1</a> <a href="#entry-Umizra" class="skt-link">Miśra</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Utaatparya_tiikaa" class="skt-link">Tātparyaṭīkā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayavaarttikataatparyaparizuddhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyavārttikatātparyapariśuddhi</i> [<a href="#entry-taatparya" class="skt-link">tātparya</a>-<a href="#entry-parizuddhi" class="skt-link">pariśuddhi</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayavaarttikataatparyaparizuddhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Nyāyavārttikatātparyapariśuddhi d'<a href="#entry-Uudayanaacaarya" class="skt-link">Udayanācārya</a>, unification des systèmes <a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a> et <a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>, aussi appelée <a href="#entry-Unyaayaparizuddhi" class="skt-link">Nyāyapariśuddhi</a> ou <a href="#entry-Unibandha" class="skt-link">Nibandha</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-nyaayavaarttika" class="skt-link">nyāyavārttika</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayazaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyaśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayazaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. sciences cognitives.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">न्याय्य</span> <i>nyāyya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-nii_3" class="skt-link">nī_3</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nyaayya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nyaayya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nyaayyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nyāyyā</i> régulier, usuel, correct, juste; approprié, convenable; légal <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> autorisé à être ⟨inf.⟩; qui convient pour ⟨i.⟩ d'être ⟨inf.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaayyatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyāyyatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaayyatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> adéquation, justesse.<br> <a href="#entry-nyaayya" class="skt-link">nyāyya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyac">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">न्यच्</span> <i>nyac</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-ac" class="skt-link">ac</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nyac" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nyac" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-niicii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nīcī</i> dirigé vers le bas.<br> <i>nyak</i> adv. vers le bas, dessous.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyafku">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">न्यङ्कु</span> <i>nyaṅku</i> <a href="#entry-nyafku" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. sorte d'antilope.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyag">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">न्यग्</span> <i>nyag</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-nyac" class="skt-link">nyac</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyagrodha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyagrodha</i> [<a href="#entry-rodha_1" class="skt-link">rodha_1</a>] <a href="#entry-nyagrodha" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Ficus indica</i>, arbre banian [«qui pousse vers le bas»]; syn. <a href="#entry-va_ta" class="skt-link">vaṭa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyagrodhapaadapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyagrodhapādapa</i> [<a href="#entry-paadapa_1" class="skt-link">pādapa_1</a>] <a href="#entry-nyagrodhapaadapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. id.<br> <a href="#entry-nyag" class="skt-link">nyag</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyarbuda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">न्यर्बुद</span> <i>nyarbuda</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-arbuda" class="skt-link">arbuda</a>] <a href="#entry-nyarbuda" class="skt-link">n.</a> math. cent millions.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">न्यस्</span> <i>nyas</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-as_2" class="skt-link">as_2</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(nyasyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>nyasta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>upa</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> jeter à terre, laisser tomber; poser sur, déposer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> abandonner; renoncer à ⟨acc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> confier, laisser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">न्यय</span> <i>nyaya</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nii_3" class="skt-link">nī_3</a>] <a href="#entry-nyaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> destruction, perte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nya_nc">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">न्यञ्च्</span> <i>nyañc</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-a_nc" class="skt-link">añc</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(nyañcati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>niañcita</i><i>)</i> pencher, suspendre — pr. md. <i>(nyañcate)</i> se baisser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nya_ncana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">न्यञ्चन</span> <i>nyañcana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-nya_nc" class="skt-link">nyañc</a>] <a href="#entry-nya_ncana" class="skt-link">n.</a> courbe, creux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cachette.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyuuna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">न्यून</span> <i>nyūna</i> [<a href="#entry-ni" class="skt-link">ni</a>-<a href="#entry-uuna" class="skt-link">ūna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-nyuuna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-nyuuna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-nyuunaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>nyūnā</i> inférieur, défectif; diminué <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> privé de ⟨i. iic.⟩; inférieur à ⟨abl.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vil; mauvais — <a href="#entry-nyuuna" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>nyūnavaraka</i>] phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] manque de l'un des 5 membres du syllogisme [<a href="#entry-pa_ncaavayava" class="skt-link">pañcāvayava</a>].<br> <i>nyūnam</i> adv. moins.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyuunataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyūnatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-nyuunataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> infériorité (à ⟨abl.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-nyuunatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>nyūnatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-nyuunatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> infériorité (à ⟨abl.⟩).<br> <a href="#entry-nyuuna" class="skt-link">nyūna</a>
  </div>
</div>
